We identify a key property of the task: high-noise timesteps determine layer assignment and coarse layer organization, while low-noise timesteps mainly refine color, alpha, texture, and boundaries. Based on this observation, we propose a terminal-boosted timestep sampling strategy to increase training coverage in the high-noise layer assignment regime. Trained on 4M multi-layer design templates, PixelART achieves state-of-the-art layer decomposition and composite reconstruction on Design-Multi-Layer-Bench and LICA with over 80% fewer parameters, 98% lower latency, and 85% lower memory than the recent Qwen-Image-Layered model. Ablation experiments show that pixel-space $\mathbf{x}$-prediction, terminal-boosted timestep sampling, and data/model scaling are critical, while T2I pretraining brings marginal benefits to the I2L task.

</details>


### [36] [C$^{2}$-INR: Customized Convolutional Implicit Neural Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22807)
*Jinglei Shi,Xinran Chang,Jiaqi Cui,Yingjie Xia,Zhaolin Xiao,Chongyi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出C²-INR，通过图像频谱方向能量引导的不规则方向卷积核、正交基稀疏重构及退火Gumbel-Softmax激活函数选择，实现卷积INR的网络定制，在表示、修复、超分任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有INR多采用MLP，逐像素渲染无法利用相邻像素的空间相关性；卷积INR可并行处理并建模像素间依赖，但研究不足且多使用固定架构，缺乏针对图像的自适应能力。

Method: 1) 用不规则方向卷积核替换常规卷积核，核的分配由图像频谱中的方向能量引导，能量强的方向分配更多核；2) 通过正交基重新表述卷积核以实现更优稀疏表示；3) 引入退火Gumbel-Softmax机制进行核级激活函数选择，为每个卷积核匹配最合适的激活函数。

Result: 在表示、修复、超分辨率等多种图像处理任务上，在可比参数预算下，C²-INR 均优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 面向卷积INR的网络定制是有效方向，内容自适应的卷积核与激活函数选择能显著提升INR性能。

Abstract: Implicit Neural Representation (INR) leverages neural networks to represent discrete signals such as images as continuous ones, where the network weights serve as a compact form of the signal itself. Most existing INR methods adopt Multi-Layer Perceptrons (MLPs) as their backbone. Since these models render each pixel independently, they inherently fail to exploit the spatial correlations that exist between neighboring pixels. In contrast,convolutional INRs can process pixels in parallel while inherently accounting for inter-pixel dependencies, making them a more natural fit for representing images. Nevertheless, convolutional INRs remain relatively underexplored, and the majority of them rely on fixed architectural settings, leaving little room for image-specific adaptation. In this paper, we investigate network customization for convolutional INRs. We replace conventional filters with irregular directional kernels, whose allocation is guided by the directional energy in the image spectrum, i.e., directions exhibiting stronger energy are assigned a larger number of kernels, enabling content-tailored convolution settings. These kernels are further reformulated via an orthogonal basis to achieve a superior sparse representation. Moreover, we introduce an annealed Gumbel-Softmax-based mechanism for kernel-level activation function selection, which gives the most suitable activation function for each convolution kernel. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method, namely C$^{2}$-INR, achieves superior performance against state-of-the-art approaches under comparable parameter budgets across a wide range of image processing tasks, including representation, inpainting, and super-resolution.

</details>


### [37] [SatOV: Restoring Spatial Priors for Training-Free Open-Vocabulary Segmentation in Remote Sensing Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22834)
*Changhao Zhao,Linglin Zeng,Hai Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SatOV：一种免训练遥感开放词汇语义分割框架，通过在表征与分辨率两个阶段恢复空间先验来提升分割效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言基础模型在通用领域表现良好，但其图像级分类设计削弱了高分辨率遥感分割所需的空间先验：深层特征变换中结构空间关系退化，下采样过程中细粒度空间细节丢失。

Method: 提出免训练框架SatOV，分两阶段恢复空间先验。ResQQ从CLIP中间层提取Query-Key自注意力，并与末层Query-Query注意力残差融合，恢复被末层抑制的结构先验；SatUp以原始高分辨率RGB图像为空间引导，结合空间特征调制与引导式交叉注意力重建像素级纹理与边界。

Result: 在DOTA、UDD、LoveDA和Vaihingen数据集上，SatOV持续改进免训练OVS，并在定量与定性结果上优于现有先进方法。

Conclusion: 结果验证了在表征阶段与空间分辨率阶段同时恢复空间先验对遥感开放词汇分割的有效性。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVS) of remote sensing imagery is a challenging pixel-level task requiring strong generalization and adaptation to the spatial characteristics of remote sensing data. Although existing vision-language foundation models perform well in general domains, their image-level classification design weakens the spatial priors needed for high-resolution remote sensing segmentation: structural spatial relations are degraded during deep feature transformation, and fine-grained spatial details are lost during downsampling. To address these complementary deficiencies, we propose SatOV, a training-free framework for open-vocabulary remote sensing segmentation that restores spatial priors at two stages of the representation pipeline. Specifically, Residual QQ Attention (ResQQ) extracts Query-Key self-attention from an intermediate CLIP layer and fuses it with final-layer Query-Query attention via a residual combination, restoring structural spatial priors suppressed by the final-layer representation. Spatially Modulated Upsampling (SatUp) uses the original high-resolution RGB image as spatial guidance, combining spatial feature modulation with guided cross-attention to reconstruct pixel-level textures and boundaries. Extensive experiments on DOTA, UDD, LoveDA, and Vaihingen show that SatOV consistently improves training-free OVS and achieves competitive quantitative and qualitative results against state-of-the-art methods. These results validate the effectiveness of restoring spatial priors at both the representation and spatial-resolution stages for remote sensing open-vocabulary segmentation.

</details>


### [38] [LINGO: Latent Initialization and Gradient Optimization for Sparse-view X-ray Novel View Synthesis and CT Reconstruction with 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22849)
*Lifeng Xing,Dequan Jin,Kunpeng Bu,Peigeng He,Shihui Ying*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 LINGO 框架，结合潜在掩码空间初始化与动态梯度优化，改进稀疏视角 X 射线下的 3DGS 新视角合成与 CT 重建，加速训练并提升质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视角 X 射线成像中角度覆盖不足导致结构模糊与噪声累积；将 3DGS 与 X 射线吸收物理结合虽有效，但存在初始化噪声大、位置不敏感、低密度区域梯度弱等问题。

Method: LINGO 统一框架：1) 融合潜在掩码空间初始化与动态梯度优化；2) 从 X 射线掩码构建体素级 3D 滤波器，抑制背景噪声并提供几何先验；3) 采用自适应体素缩放策略与动态缩放损失，调整空间分辨率并放大低密度结构梯度；4) 提出 IPSD 指标评估初始化质量。

Result: X3D 数据集上，新视角合成任务 PSNR/SSIM 平均提升 0.72/0.0039，5k 步即可达到 SOTA 30k 迭代的重建质量；CT 重建任务 PSNR/SSIM 平均提升 0.36/0.0134。

Conclusion: LINGO 在不同稀疏视角成像场景中均能加速训练并提升重建质量，验证了潜在初始化与梯度优化结合的有效性。

Abstract: In novel view synthesis and Computed Tomography (CT) reconstruction with sparse-view X-ray imaging, insufficient angular coverage leads to structural ambiguity and accumulated noise. Integrating 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) with X-ray absorption physics can achieve promising results, but it suffers from noisy initialization, positional insensitivity, and weak gradients in low-density regions. In this paper, we propose a unified Latent Initialization and Gradient Optimization (LINGO) framework to address these issues. LINGO combines latent mask-space initialization with dynamic gradient optimization to improve point cloud structural completeness while accelerating training. It constructs voxel-level 3D filters from X-ray masks to robustly suppress background noise and provide reliable geometric priors. By employing an adaptive voxel scaling strategy and dynamically scaling loss, LINGO can adjust spatial resolution and explicitly amplify gradients in low-density structures. To evaluate the quality of initialization, we introduce the Initialization Point Cloud Structural Deviation (IPSD) metric. Experiments on the X3D dataset indicate that for the novel view synthesis task, LINGO improves the Peak Signal-to-Noise Ratio (PSNR) and Structural Similarity Index (SSIM) by an average of 0.72 and 0.0039, respectively, over baselines under identical sparse-view settings, achieving comparable reconstruction quality within 5k steps to state-of-the-art models typically trained with 30k iterations. For the CT reconstruction task, LINGO also demonstrates consistent improvements, with average PSNR and SSIM gains of 0.36 and 0.0134. These results highlight LINGO's effectiveness in both accelerating training and enhancing reconstruction quality across different sparse-view imaging scenarios.

</details>


### [39] [Image Frame Dynamic Object Segmentation and Ego Motion Estimation using Radar Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22857)
*Astik Srivastava,Suhani Grover,Avinash Sharma,Madhava Krishna*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Radar-Dot，利用雷达多普勒测量解耦自车运动估计与动态目标分割，在nuScenes上取得20.24%动态IoU和33.67% F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中动态目标分割与自车运动估计高度耦合：准确自车运动估计需静态场景观测，而识别静态观测又需已知自车运动。论文希望利用雷达多普勒测量打破这一循环依赖。

Method: 提出雷达-图像（radar-RGB）框架Radar-Dot。首先通过线性多普勒约束估计自车速度，并用基于残差的静态/动态分割与鲁棒估计降低运动目标影响；随后将估计运动与度量深度、稠密光流结合，识别观测运动与刚性场景运动不一致的图像区域。

Result: 在10个nuScenes场景（nuscenes-mini部分）的394对帧上，几何流水线达到20.24%动态IoU与33.67% F1-score；基于雷达的静态点滤波相比使用全部雷达点提升了自车速度估计。

Conclusion: 结果表明雷达可作为联合提升自车运动估计与动态目标分割的模态，具备应用潜力。

Abstract: Dynamic object segmentation and ego-motion estimation are closely coupled problems in autonomous driving, as accurate ego-motion estimation typically requires static scene observations, while identifying static observations requires knowledge of the ego motion. We present Radar-Dot, a radar--RGB framework that exploits radar Doppler measurements to address this coupling. Radar returns are first used to estimate ego velocity through a linear Doppler constraint, with residual-based static/dynamic segmentation and robust estimation used to reduce the influence of moving objects. The estimated motion is then combined with metric depth and dense optical flow to identify image regions whose observed motion is inconsistent with the rigid scene motion. Experiments on 10 nuScenes scenes (part of nuscenes-mini) demonstrate that the resulting geometric pipeline achieves 20.24% dynamic IoU and 33.67% F1-score over 394 frame pairs, while radar-based static-point filtering improves ego-velocity estimation compared with using all radar returns. These results demonstrate the potential of radar as a modality for jointly improving ego-motion estimation and dynamic object segmentation.

</details>


### [40] [Planning-Aligned Pretraining of BEV Representations with Sparse Action-Conditioned Targets for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22868)
*Jaeha Song,Soonmin Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PAVER，一种面向规划的BEV编码器预训练方法，利用单帧LiDAR构建稀疏风险与未知目标，无需驾驶任务标注或稠密重建，仅迁移BEV编码器，在nuScenes与Bench2Drive上显著提升碰撞率、规划与闭环驾驶表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端驾驶需要与规划相关的鸟瞰图（BEV）表示，但现有预训练方法往往依赖任务标注或稠密场景重建，成本高且限制下游架构。

Method: PAVER从单次LiDAR扫描构建稀疏风险与未知目标，描述基于规则自车运动下的已占据与未观测证据；用一个约1万参数的预测头，在动作状态条件下从掩码BEV特征预测这些目标，将监督导向候选运动的几何约束。预训练无需驾驶任务标注或稠密重建，仅迁移BEV编码器，保留下游架构与纯相机推理。

Result: 在nuScenes上，PAVER将VAD-Tiny平均碰撞率从0.51%降至0.19%，并提升规划L2、运动预测、检测与建图；所选VAD-Tiny与VAD-Base训练方案比从头训练减少约36%总训练时间。在Bench2Drive Town05 Long上，PAVER将UniAD-Tiny闭环驾驶分数从48.45提升至58.79。

Conclusion: PAVER以极低标注与计算代价实现规划对齐的BEV编码器预训练，可即插即用地提升多种端到端驾驶模型的规划安全性与闭环性能。

Abstract: End-to-end driving requires planning-relevant bird's-eye-view (BEV) representations, but existing pretraining approaches often rely on task annotations or dense scene reconstruction. We introduce PAVER, Planning-Aligned BEV Encoder Pretraining. From a single LiDAR sweep, PAVER constructs sparse risk and unknown targets describing occupied and unobserved evidence along rule-based ego motions. A 10K-parameter head predicts these targets from masked BEV features conditioned on the action state, directing supervision toward geometric constraints on candidate motions. Pretraining requires no driving-task annotations or dense reconstruction. Only the BEV encoder is transferred, preserving the downstream architecture and camera-only inference. On nuScenes, PAVER reduces VAD-Tiny's average collision rate from 0.51% to 0.19%, while improving planning L2, motion prediction, detection, and mapping. The selected VAD-Tiny and VAD-Base schedules use about 36% less estimated total training time than scratch training, including pretraining. On Bench2Drive Town05 Long, PAVER improves UniAD-Tiny's closed-loop Driving Score from 48.45 to 58.79. The project page is available at https://archiiive99.github.io/PAVER.

</details>


### [41] [Combining Foundation Model Confidence and Monocular Depth for Training-Free Out-of-Distribution Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22896)
*Serin Varghese,Fabian Hüger,Kira Maag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种免训练的方法，直接从基础分割模型的置信度预测中导出稠密OOD分数，并融合单目深度估计的几何信息，用于自动驾驶开放场景中未知目标的检测与分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶在开放世界场景中会遇到未知目标（如奇异动物、散落货物），可靠地检测和分割这些分布外（OOD）目标对环境理解与安全决策至关重要。现有方法多依赖OOD训练样本、需要重训练分割主干或专用辅助架构，限制了实际应用性。

Method: 一种免训练方法：无需任务特定微调，也无需接触异常数据，直接从基础分割模型的置信度预测中导出稠密的OOD分数。为提升OOD分割的鲁棒性，将单目深度估计提供的几何信息引入决策过程，为基于不确定性的预测提供互补线索。

Result: 在SegmentMeIfYouCan基准上进行了评估，并额外评估了其在视频序列中进行OOD跟踪的性能，以反映真实感知系统的时间特性。该方法在以道路为中心的基准上表现强劲。

Conclusion: 所提免训练方法无需OOD数据、无需重训练或辅助架构，即可实现稠密OOD分割，并通过融合深度几何信息增强鲁棒性；在道路中心基准上性能出色，且可扩展至视频OOD跟踪。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles operating in open-world scenarios are inevitably confronted with previously unknown objects, such as exotic animals or loose cargo. The reliable detection and segmentation of these out-of-distribution (OOD) objects is therefore crucial for a safe understanding of the environment and decision-making. Most existing approaches require access to OOD training samples, retraining of the segmentation backbone, or dedicated auxiliary architectures, limiting their practical applicability. We propose a training-free method that derives dense OOD scores directly from the confidence predictions of a foundation segmentation model, without any task-specific fine-tuning or access to anomalous data. To improve the robustness of our OOD segmentation, geometric information from monocular depth estimation is incorporated into the decision process, providing complementary cues to uncertainty-based predictions. We evaluate the proposed method on the SegmentMeIfYouCan benchmark and additionally assess its performance on OOD tracking in video sequences, reflecting the temporal nature of real-world perception systems. The method performs strongly on road-centered benchmarks.

</details>


### [42] [Scout: Open-World Species Recognition on the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22897)
*Mohammad Mehdi Rastikerdar,Hui Guan,Deepak Ganesan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Scout 是一个自主开放世界识别系统，通过间歇调用云端 VLM 来教会边缘小模型新类别，在严格的计算、能量和带宽预算下，将 VLM 的开放世界识别能力带到边缘设备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型具备开放世界识别能力，但算力需求高，难以在边缘设备运行；云端卸载虽可解决算力问题，却消耗稀缺带宽和通信能量。作者希望在紧张的计算、能量与带宽预算内，让边缘设备获得 VLM 的开放世界识别能力。野生动物监测是理想场景，因为相机陷阱会遇到部署时未知的物种。

Method: 提出 Scout 自主开放世界识别系统，间歇性地调用云端 VLM，将识别出的每个物种教给紧凑的边缘模型，转化为持久的、站点条件化的识别能力；仅需部署位置和空站点帧，无需预定义物种列表、人工标注或手动调参。

Result: 在三个区域 30 个相机陷阱部署、NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano 上，Scout 的准确率与给定预定义物种列表的模型相差仅 0.1–2.5%；对初始类别集之外的物种，Scout 达到 53.7–59.1% 准确率，而全云端卸载为 56.5–65.1%，但 Scout 减少 59–71% 的部署能耗。

Conclusion: Scout 能在有限的计算、能量和带宽预算下，将云端 VLM 的开放世界识别能力有效迁移到边缘设备，在接近全云端卸载精度的同时显著降低能耗，适用于野生动物监测等开放世界识别场景。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (VLMs) enable recognition beyond a fixed class set, but their computational demands prevent them from running on many edge devices. Cloud offload makes this capability accessible, but sending every image consumes scarce bandwidth and communication energy. We ask how to bring the open-world recognition capability of VLMs to the edge while operating within tight compute, energy, and bandwidth budgets. Wildlife monitoring provides a natural setting for exploring this question because camera traps encounter species not known at deployment. We present Scout, an autonomous open-world recognition system that invokes a cloud VLM intermittently to teach new classes to a compact edge model. Given only the deployment location and empty site frames, Scout autonomously turns each species identified by the VLM into persistent, site-conditioned recognition capability in a resource-efficient edge model, without a predefined species list, human labeling, or manual tuning. Across 30 camera-trap deployments in three regions on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano, the accuracy of Scout remains within 0.1-2.5% of a model given a predefined species list. On species outside its initial class set, Scout achieves 53.7-59.1% accuracy, compared with 56.5-65.1% for full cloud offload, while using 59-71% less deployment energy.

</details>


### [43] [AVTR-1: Open Stack for Real-Time Interactive Avatars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22913)
*Artem Kravtsov,Dmitrii Ziganshin,Vsevolod Poletaev,Gleb Balitskiy,Anastasia Tikhonova,Egor Burkov,Vadim Lebedev*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AVTR-1 是一个面向实时交互式虚拟人对话的开放技术栈，核心是 153M 参数的自回归流匹配动作生成器，可接入外部语音代理并实现同步的音视频流，同时提出 R-DGG 指标验证听者动作对说话人语音的预测依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 talking-head 与双人模型虽已实现实时推理，但快速动作生成并不等于可交互的对话：实际系统需与外部语音代理的语音同步、调度视频帧播放并处理打断。此外，传统聆听动作指标无法判断说话人语音是否真正影响生成动作。

Method: 1) 构建以 153M 参数自回归流匹配动作生成器为核心的开放技术栈，条件为双方音频；2) 通过自蒸馏将音频编码器适配为流式；3) 将基于 chunk 的生成转化为由外部语音代理驱动的连续同步音视频流，并解析推导其对用户感知延迟的贡献；4) 提出 R-DGG 指标，在扣除听者历史与说话人动作后衡量说话人语音带来的额外预测信息。

Result: 1) 延迟边界在两个商用语音代理上得到验证；2) 在所有报告的视觉质量指标、多数常规聆听动作指标上领先对比的双人系统，唇同步保持竞争力；3) 推理在数据中心与消费级 GPU 上均可实时运行；4) R-DGG 在真实听者与所评估双人系统中发现统计显著的预测依赖，而在无配对音频的 talking-head 生成器或说话人-听者错配时未发现。

Conclusion: AVTR-1 提供了可实时交互的虚拟人对话完整开放栈，实现模型、渲染器与后端服务，模型权重、渲染器与服务后端按组件分别授权发布；R-DGG 为验证双人交互中的语音-动作依赖提供了新工具。

Abstract: Talking-head and dyadic models now achieve real-time inference, yet fast motion generation alone does not produce an interactive conversation. A live system must synchronize the model's output with speech from an external voice agent, schedule video frames for playback, and handle interruptions. We introduce AVTR-1, an open stack for real-time interactive avatar conversations, built around a compact 153M-parameter autoregressive flow-matching motion generator conditioned on both participants' audio. We adapt its audio encoder for streaming through self-distillation. The stack turns the model's chunk-based generation into a continuous, synchronized audio-video stream driven by an external voice agent, and we analytically derive its contribution to the user-facing latencies and validate the resulting bounds with two commercial voice agents. Further experiments demonstrate that AVTR-1 leads the compared dyadic systems on all reported visual-quality metrics and most conventional listening-motion metrics while remaining competitive in lip synchronization. Its inference runtime operates in real time on data-center and consumer GPUs. However, conventional listening metrics do not establish whether the paired speaker's speech contributes to generated motion. We therefore introduce the Reference-Based Directed Granger Gain (R-DGG), which measures the additional predictive information carried by speaker speech after accounting for listener history and speaker motion. R-DGG finds statistically supported predictive dependence for recorded listeners and all evaluated dyadic systems, but not for talking-head generators without paired audio or mismatched speaker-listener pairs. We release the model weights, renderer, and serving backend under component-specific licenses.

</details>


### [44] [Planning and Rendering in Concert: DeepFusion of Autoregressive Layouts and Diffusion for Visual Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22916)
*Guanqiao Chen,Jingru Tan,Dongxing Mao,Catherine Chen,Zijian Du,Libo Qin,Hu Jian Guo,Alex Jinpeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DuetGen提出统一框架，将自回归布局规划与扩散渲染联合学习，在文本图像生成基准上取得接近更大模型的高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从提示生成富含文本的图像需要文本保真度与文本在图像中的连贯融合。显式布局可提供“写什么、写在哪”的结构化指导，但良好的规划并不保证渲染器忠实实现。现有基于布局的自回归-扩散系统通常将规划与渲染分开优化，导致规划器表示无法与图像合成联合适配。

Method: 提出DuetGen，基于DeepFusion的自主视觉文本生成器，联合学习自回归规划与连续扩散渲染。DeepFusion以规划器的提示及bbox-content隐藏状态为条件驱动扩散Transformer，使渲染监督能塑造连接文本规划与视觉输出的表示。联合目标包含自回归规划监督、文本区域加权扩散学习与辅助坐标监督。推理时采用Phase-Aware Attention Modulation增强图像区域与匹配坐标、内容状态的对应关系。

Result: 采用2B规划器与4B单流DiT，DuetGen在CVTG-2K上达到0.8293词准确率，在LongText-Bench上达到0.938准确率，两项基准均接近规模大得多的Qwen-Image。

Conclusion: 结果表明，联合学习的规划表示与区域特定渲染对自主视觉文本生成具有重要价值。

Abstract: Generating text-rich images from prompts requires both textual fidelity and the coherent integration of text into the surrounding image. An explicit layout can provide structured guidance about what text should appear and where, but a well-formed plan alone does not guarantee that the renderer will realize it faithfully. Existing layout-based AR-diffusion systems typically optimize planning and rendering separately, preventing the planner's representations from being adapted jointly with image synthesis. We introduce DuetGen, an autonomous visual text generator built on DeepFusion, which jointly learns autoregressive planning and continuous diffusion rendering. DeepFusion conditions a diffusion transformer on the planner's prompt and bbox-content hidden states, allowing rendering supervision to shape the representations connecting textual plans with visual outputs. Its joint objective combines autoregressive plan supervision, text-region-weighted diffusion learning, and auxiliary coordinate supervision to maintain structured planning, emphasize text-bearing regions, and improve the spatial precision of planner representations. During inference, Phase-Aware Attention Modulation strengthens the correspondence between image regions and their matched coordinate and content states, facilitating region-specific execution of the generated plan. With a 2B planner and a 4B single-stream DiT, DuetGen achieves 0.8293 word accuracy on CVTG-2K and 0.938 accuracy on LongText-Bench, closely matching the substantially larger Qwen-Image on both benchmarks. These results demonstrate the value of jointly learned planning representations and region-specific rendering for autonomous visual text generation.

</details>


### [45] [D3GS: Depth, DINO, and RGB Diffusion Co-Guided 3D Gaussian Splatting for Sparse-View Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22941)
*Yunqi Gao,Zhanfeng Liao,Hanzhang Tu,Zhaoqi Su,Guoqing Zheng,Songtao Wang,Hongwen Zhang,Zhou Xue,Leyuan Liu,Yebin Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出D³GS框架，通过深度补全、DINO引导的视角一致性学习和扩散生成先验，联合增强稀疏视角下3DGS的几何与外观重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏输入下3D高斯泼溅面临几何歧义、跨视角不一致以及欠约束区域细节缺失等问题，导致重建退化与渲染不稳定，亟需联合增强几何与外观的解决方案。

Method: D³GS包含三个模块：(1) 基于扩散补全与DPT精修恢复高分辨率度量深度图，提供鲁棒的高斯初始化与几何约束；(2) DINO引导的视角一致性学习，用结构特征增强高斯属性；(3) 扩散引导的高斯精修模块，将生成先验注入迭代优化策略以增强高频细节。

Result: 在DTU、LLFF和Mip-NeRF 360数据集上，D³GS相较强基线取得一致且显著的性能提升，消融实验验证了各组件的作用与互补性。

Conclusion: 深度、DINO特征与扩散先验的协同引导能有效缓解稀疏视角3DGS的重建难题，提升几何与外观质量及渲染稳定性。

Abstract: Novel view synthesis from sparse inputs remains challenging for 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) due to ambiguous geometry, cross-view inconsistency, and missing details in under-constrained regions, resulting in degraded reconstruction and unstable rendering. To tackle these issues, we propose D$^{3}$GS, a Depth-DINO-Diffusion guided sparse-view Gaussian reconstruction framework that jointly enhances geometry and appearance. D$^{3}$GS first recovers a high-resolution, metric depth map via diffusion-based completion and DPT (Dense Prediction Transformer) refinement, providing robust Gaussian initialization and geometric constraints. Then, a DINO-guided view-consistent learning is introduced to augment Gaussian attributes with structural features, improving multi-view consistency. Finally, a diffusion-based Gaussian refinement module injects generative priors into an iterative optimization strategy, enhancing high-frequency geometric and appearance details within the Gaussian representation. Experiments on DTU, LLFF, and Mip-NeRF 360 show that D$^{3}$GS achieves consistent and substantial improvements over strong baselines, with ablation studies validating the effectiveness and complementary roles of each component.

</details>


### [46] [An Evolutionary Agentic Approach for Open-ended Image Quality Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22942)
*Zhenchen Tang,Bo Peng,Zichuan Wang,Songlin Yang,Leilei Cao,Fengjie Zhu,Jing Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PACE，一种免训练的多智能体框架，将开放式图像质量评估（IQA）形式化为显式的评估协议构建，通过可验证的VQA探针和四张标注图像的校准，缓解大模型在未见过维度评分时复用通用质量先验的“整体偏置”问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型推动IQA从传统保真度扩展到物理合理性、文本渲染正确性等新兴维度，但现有IQA模型依赖固定定义和大量监督，难以扩展到开放式感知维度。作者识别出“整体偏置”这一关键局限：在评分未见过维度时，模型会复用通用的整体质量先验，导致评分错误和排序反转。

Method: PACE（Perceptual Agentic Collaborative Evolution）是一个免训练的多智能体框架，将开放式IQA表述为显式的协议构建过程。给定目标维度，多个协作智能体构建由可验证的视觉问答（VQA）探针组成的评估协议，以具体视觉证据而非整体印象为基础进行评估。所得协议仅用每个维度四张人工标注图像进行校准，并采用双轨评分机制使模型感知与人工评分尺度对齐。

Result: 在传统IQA、结构保真度、上下文感知美学以及新定义的开放式维度等场景中，PACE持续提升其多模态大模型（MLLM）基座的表现，在多种IQA设置下取得有竞争力的性能，并将整体覆盖（HOR）从44.4%降低至8.6%。

Conclusion: 通过将开放式IQA转化为可验证协议构建并仅依赖少量标注进行校准，PACE有效抑制了整体偏置，使免训练的多智能体框架能够灵活扩展到多样化的图像质量评估维度。

Abstract: Generative models are rapidly expanding image quality assessment (IQA) beyond traditional fidelity factors to emerging dimensions such as physical plausibility and text-rendering correctness. However, existing IQA models rely on fixed definitions and heavy supervision, making them difficult to extend to open-ended perceptual dimensions. We identify holistic bias as an important limitation: when scoring an unseen dimension, models reuse generic quality priors, leading to scoring errors and rank inversion. To address this, we propose PACE (Perceptual Agentic Collaborative Evolution), a training-free multi-agent framework that formulates open-ended IQA as explicit protocol construction. Given a target dimension, PACE uses collaborative agents to construct an evaluation protocol composed of verifiable Visual Question Answering (VQA) probes, grounding evaluation in concrete visual evidence rather than holistic impressions. The resulting protocol is calibrated using only four human-annotated images per dimension, while a dual-track scoring mechanism aligns model perception with human scoring scales. Across traditional IQA, structural fidelity, context-aware aesthetics, and newly defined open-ended dimensions, PACE consistently improves its MLLM backbone, achieving competitive performance across diverse IQA settings, and reduces the Holistic Override Rate (HOR) from 44.4\% to 8.6\%.

</details>


### [47] [RewardVerse: Rubric-Guided Policy Optimization for Video Reward Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22947)
*Zhenchen Tang,Yang Li,Songlin Yang,Bo Peng,Xiaotong Zhao,Shuai Li,Haotian Fan,Alan Zhao,Jing Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RewardVerse，一个基于评分标准（rubric）的视频奖励框架，通过在评估查询与打分器之间引入动态rubric作为中间表示，缓解标量漂移问题，并配合两阶段训练算法RGPO进行优化，在EvalVerse基准上取得最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频奖励模型直接将复杂主观的视频质量映射为单一分数，缺乏显式评估标准，导致在不同提示间出现标量漂移（分数尺度崩塌或偏移），使奖励信号对强化学习不可靠。受专业人工标注工程启发，作者希望引入中间评估标准来提供稳定的语义锚点。

Method: 提出RewardVerse框架，引入动态rubric作为评估查询与打分器之间的中间表示：先生成显式评估标准，再进行rubric引导打分，以提供稳定语义锚点。为高效优化该协同流程，提出Rubric-Guided Policy Optimization (RGPO)两阶段训练算法：第一阶段用自演化种子rubric预热打分器，第二阶段联合优化rubric生成器以产生查询自适应评估标准，并持续对齐人类评分。

Result: 在16维EvalVerse基准及外部数据集上的大量实验表明，RewardVerse缓解了标量漂移，在pointwise和pairwise评估上均达到state-of-the-art性能，并为视频生成强化学习提供稳健且可解释的奖励信号。

Conclusion: 通过引入rubric作为中间表示并结合RGPO训练，RewardVerse有效解决了视频奖励模型评分不稳定的问题，提供了稳健、可解释且可靠的奖励信号，适用于视频生成模型的强化学习优化。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is vital for optimizing video generation models, with a robust reward model (RM) serving as the cornerstone. However, existing video reward models often produce unstable scalar scores because they directly map complex, subjective video quality into a single score without explicit evaluation criteria. This leads to scalar drift, where the scoring scale collapses or shifts across different prompts, making the reward unreliable for RL. Drawing inspiration from professional human annotation engineering, we address this problem with RewardVerse, a rubric-based video reward framework that introduces a dynamic rubric as an intermediate representation between the evaluation query and the scorer. Instead of unconstrained direct scoring, RewardVerse first generates explicit evaluation criteria and then performs rubric-guided scoring, providing a stable semantic anchor that mitigates scalar drift. To efficiently optimize this collaborative pipeline, we propose Rubric-Guided Policy Optimization (RGPO), a two-stage training algorithm. RGPO first warms up the scorer using self-evolving seed rubrics and then jointly optimizes the rubric generator to produce query-adaptive evaluation criteria while continuously aligning the scorer with human ratings. Extensive experiments on the 16-dimensional EvalVerse benchmark and external datasets demonstrate that RewardVerse mitigates scalar drift, achieves state-of-the-art performance on both pointwise and pairwise evaluation, and provides a robust and interpretable reward signal for RL in video generation.

</details>


### [48] [CLEAR: Complex Learned Explicit Analytical Regularization for Ultra-Accelerated 4D Flow CMR Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22950)
*German Shâma Wache,Sebastian Neumayer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 CLEAR，一种面向 4D Flow CMR 重建的可学习正则化器，兼顾压缩感知的可解释性与学习模型的灵活性，在 10×–50× 加速下优于 LLR 和 FlowVN，参数量不到 10k。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压缩感知正则化虽可解释，但在高加速下因人工设计而过于受限；现有学习类方法通过展开网络隐式编码正则化，牺牲了可解释性。

Method: 提出 CLEAR，将压缩感知的可解释性与学习模型灵活性结合，是首个用于 4D 重建任务的可学习正则化器，并保持可解释的正则化结构。

Result: 在 CMRx4DFlow2026 挑战赛 10×–50× 超高加速条件下，CLEAR 优于压缩感知 LLR 与变分网络 FlowVN，且参数量少于 10k。

Conclusion: CLEAR 说明可学习正则化器能在保持可解释性的同时提升 4D Flow CMR 重建性能，实现高效且透明的重建。

Abstract: While compressed-sensing regularizers enable interpretable reconstruction of 4D Flow CMR through transparent variational objectives, their hand-crafted nature is too restrictive under high acceleration. State-of-the-art learning-based approaches mitigate this, but typically encode regularization implicitly through unrolled network modules, which limits their interpretability. To address this limitation, we propose CLEAR, designed to combine the interpretability of compressed sensing with the flexibility of learned models. To the best of our knowledge, it is the first learned regularizer for a 4D reconstruction task. In the ultra-accelerated \(10\times\)--\(50\times\) regime of the CMRx4DFlow2026 challenge, CLEAR outperforms compressed sensing locally low-rank (LLR) and the popular variational network FlowVN, while using less than 10k parameters and preserving an interpretable regularization structure.

</details>


### [49] [General Collaborative Intelligence: Architecting Cognition for Resilient Multi-Agent Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22967)
*Lei Zhang,Chun Ye,Le Yang,Zhaozhong Wang,Deng-Ping Fan,Hang Dai,Binglu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该综述针对多智能体无人系统协作感知领域现有研究"维度孤立、依赖关系缺失"的问题，提出五维分类法与三项认知协同条件两大统一分析视角，并构建GCI-Bench五支柱评分协议，为跨研究的协作方法比较提供可比基准，同时指出可复现性、仿真到现实鸿沟等开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体无人系统正从孤立的自中心感知转向协作智能，通过交换紧凑特征克服单智能体无法逃脱的局部观测困境（遮挡、有限感知范围、环境退化）。该领域已在架构、通信、具身、韧性、信任等维度成熟，但现有综述孤立审视这些维度，很少揭示其相互依赖关系，且面临基准饱和与评估碎片化问题。

Method: 论文采用综述与统一综合的方法，提出两个互补视角：一是横跨协作阶段、通信范式、融合架构、学习策略、应用领域的五维分类法；二是将认知协同转化为可操作标准的三项认知协同条件——语义消歧、语用信息交换、主动信息觅食。在此基础上综述协作架构与拓扑、将信道视为可微管线组件的神经通信协同设计、基于多智能体强化学习的具身行动-感知回路，以及同步、不确定性量化、标签高效学习的韧性机制。

Result: 论文将上述进展映射到V2X、无人机、工业物流、智慧城市四个应用领域，以及安全-隐私-效用三元框架；并提出GCI-Bench——一套包含成熟度模型的五支柱评分协议，使协作方法的权衡取舍在跨研究中可比，以应对基准饱和与评估碎片化。

Conclusion: 论文对可复现性、仿真到现实的鸿沟，以及协作反而导致性能退化的条件进行批判性反思，识别出开放挑战，并指明在真实世界不确定性下通向通用协作智能的方向。

Abstract: Multi-agent unmanned systems are moving from isolated, ego-centric sensing toward collaborative intelligence, in which distributed agents exchange compact features to overcome a local observation trap that no single agent can escape: occlusions, finite sensor range, and environmental degradation. The field has matured across architectural, communication, embodied, resilience, and trust dimensions, yet existing surveys examine these dimensions in isolation and rarely expose their dependencies. This review offers a unified synthesis through two complementary lenses. The first is a five-dimensional taxonomy spanning collaboration stage, communication paradigm, fusion architecture, learning strategy, and application domain. The second is three cognitive synergy conditions, Semantic Disambiguation, Pragmatic Information Exchange, and Proactive Informational Foraging, that turn cognitive synergy into operational criteria. Across these lenses we survey collaboration architectures and topologies, neural-communication co-design that treats the channel as a differentiable pipeline component, embodied action-perception loops via multi-agent reinforcement learning, and resilience mechanisms for synchronization, uncertainty quantification, and label-efficient learning. We then map these advances onto four operational domains, V2X, unmanned aerial, industrial logistics, and smart cities, and onto the safety-privacy-utility triad. To counter benchmark saturation and evaluation fragmentation, we propose GCI-Bench, a five-pillar scoring protocol with a maturity model that makes the trade-offs of collaborative methods comparable across studies. A critical reflection on reproducibility, the sim-to-real gulf, and conditions under which collaboration degrades performance identifies open challenges and charts directions toward general collaborative intelligence under real-world uncertainty.

</details>


### [50] [M3GA-Wild: A Large-Scale Dataset and Benchmark for Multi-Modal Multi-session Ground-to-Aerial Place Recognition in Forests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23003)
*Ethan Griffiths,Maryam Haghighat,Simon Denman,Clinton Fookes,Milad Ramezani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: M3GA-Wild是首个面向森林的、多模态多会话的“地面到空中”位置识别基准，统一并扩展了现有森林定位数据集，提供同步地面RGB与LiDAR、高分辨率航拍影像与多高度LiDAR及精确6-DoF位姿，支持系统评测多类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化自然环境下（尤其森林）的跨平台、长期自主定位面临显著挑战，缺乏统一且覆盖多模态、多会话、跨视角的标准化基准，难以系统评估视觉、LiDAR及多模态融合方法。

Method: 构建并发布M3GA-Wild基准：整合与扩展森林定位数据，提供36 km地面轨迹的同步RGB与LiDAR，370公顷对齐的高分辨率航拍影像与多高度LiDAR，以及地理参考的精确6-DoF位姿；支持视觉、LiDAR、跨模态、多模态方法的系统评测，并利用航拍RGB与航拍LiDAR配对评估单目深度估计基础模型。

Result: 基线实验显示：在显著视角差异下，基于LiDAR的方法明显优于纯视觉方法；现有多模态融合策略因跨模态对齐不佳而收益有限；将航拍RGB与地理参考航拍LiDAR配对可评估单目深度估计基础模型，初步实验揭示现有方法存在不足。

Conclusion: 结果凸显跨平台定位中的关键难题，包括模态错配与严重域差；M3GA-Wild为鲁棒多模态定位与非结构化自然环境中长期自主性研究建立了新基准，数据集与代码将在论文录用后公开。

Abstract: We present M3GA-Wild, the first benchmark for multi-modal, multi-session ground-to-aerial place recognition in forests. M3GA-Wild unifies and extends existing forest localisation datasets, providing a holistic benchmark with synchronised RGB imagery and LiDAR from ground traversals spanning 36 km, aligned high-resolution aerial imagery and multi-altitude LiDAR covering 370 hectares, and accurate geo-referenced 6-DoF poses for precise evaluation. M3GA-Wild captures diverse forest scenes with varying viewpoints, occlusion, and environmental conditions, enabling systematic evaluation of visual, LiDAR, cross-modal, and multi-modal methods. Baseline experiments show that LiDAR-based approaches significantly outperform vision-only methods under severe viewpoint differences, while current multi-modal fusion strategies yield limited gains due to poor cross-modal alignment. By pairing aerial RGB imagery with geo-referenced aerial LiDAR, M3GA-Wild also enables evaluation of foundation models for monocular depth estimation as a cheap source of 3D geometry from forest imagery, with initial experiments revealing shortfalls of current methods. These results highlight key challenges in cross-platform localisation, including modality misalignment and severe domain gaps. M3GA-Wild establishes a new benchmark to support research in robust multi-modal localisation and long-term autonomy in unstructured natural environments. The dataset and code will be available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [51] [Compressing 3D Gaussian Splatting via Cross-Representation Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23005)
*Yezheng Zhang,Huanxiong Liang,Chuqin Zhou,Guo Lu,Wenjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CRP-GS框架，利用跨表示先验优化锚点级熵建模，在保持渲染质量的同时平均降低约30%码率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS虽能实现高质量新视角合成，但密集高斯基元带来高存储与传输成本；现有基于锚点的压缩方法虽减少单基元冗余，但锚点间冗余仍未被充分利用。

Method: 提出CRP-GS（跨表示先验的高斯泼溅压缩框架），包含两个模块：一是面向对应关系的层次结构（COHS），按特征对应而非空间邻近组织锚点，构建根-叶依赖关系，使部分锚点作为先验条件化编码其他锚点，提升似然预测精度并降低条件熵；二是共享特征聚合（SFA），从上下文哈希网格中提取全局共享特征并注入锚点表示，剥离场景一致的低频信息以避免冗余。两模块在统一的率失真目标下训练。

Result: 在多个基准测试上取得良好的整体率失真权衡，相比基于锚点的基线平均降低约30%码率，同时保持可比的渲染质量。

Conclusion: 跨表示先验可有效挖掘锚点间冗余，在率失真意义下显著提升3DGS压缩效率。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables high-quality novel view synthesis but incurs high storage and transmission costs due to dense Gaussian primitives. Recent anchor-based compression reduces per-primitive redundancy, yet redundancy across anchors remains largely unexploited. We propose CRP-GS (Cross-Representation Priors for Gaussian Splatting), a rate-distortion optimized compression framework that leverages cross-representation priors to improve anchor-level entropy modeling. First, a Correspondence-Oriented Hierarchical Structure (COHS) organizes anchors by feature correspondence rather than spatial proximity, constructing root-leaf dependencies so that selected anchors can act as informative priors to conditionally encode others, yielding more accurate likelihood prediction and lower conditional entropy. Second, Shared Feature Aggregation (SFA) extracts globally shared features from a contextual hash grid and injects them into anchor representations, factoring out scene-consistent low-frequency information that would otherwise be redundantly embedded in individual anchors. Both modules are trained under a unified rate-distortion objective to balance bitrate reduction and rendering fidelity. Experiments across multiple benchmarks show that CRP-GS achieves a favorable overall rate-distortion trade-off, yielding around 30% average bitrate reduction compared to anchor-based baselines while maintaining comparable rendering quality.

</details>


### [52] [MixiMotion: One-Step Text-to-Motion Generation via Asymmetric Set Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23010)
*Hung Dinh,Binh Mai,Tran Quoc Bao Le,Lam Nguyen,Cong Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MixiMotion 是一个严格单步文本到动作生成框架，基于离线集合蒸馏，通过非对称双向匹配和可微解码空间运动学监督，在单次网络评估下实现高质量动作生成，速度提升约 89 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迭代式文本到动作生成质量高、语义对齐好，但需要多次网络评估，推理延迟大。目标是实现严格单步生成，同时保持质量并大幅降低延迟。

Method: 提出 MixiMotion，基于离线集合蒸馏。为每个文本提示构建多教师动作的离线库，而非蒸馏单一教师轨迹；采用非对称双向匹配对齐教师与学生样本集合：教师到学生方向促进覆盖多样教师支持的动作，学生到教师方向抑制不受支持的生成；并引入可微解码空间运动学监督，补充归一化表示匹配。推理时单次网络评估生成完整动作序列，无需教师查询、迭代采样或候选排序。

Result: 在 ViMoGen 上，语义对齐得分 0.835，优于所评估的单步基线，接近 50 步 HY-Motion-1.0-Lite 教师的 0.858。盲测人工评估中，总体评分 4.33，教师为 4.50，优于单步/少步基线。生成延迟从 829.58 ms 降至 9.30 ms，约 89.2 倍加速。

Conclusion: MixiMotion 在严格单步文本到动作生成中实现了有效的质量—效率权衡。

Abstract: Iterative text-to-motion generation delivers high-quality and semantically aligned motions but requires multiple network evaluations, resulting in substantial inference latency. We present \textbf{MixiMotion}, a strict one-step text-to-motion generation framework based on offline set distillation. Instead of distilling a single teacher trajectory for each text prompt, MixiMotion constructs an offline bank of multiple teacher motions and aligns teacher and student sample sets through \textbf{asymmetric bidirectional matching}. The teacher-to-student direction promotes coverage of diverse teacher-supported motions, while the student-to-teacher direction suppresses unsupported generations. We further introduce differentiable decoded-space kinematic supervision to complement normalized representation matching with constraints in the decoded motion space. At inference, MixiMotion generates a complete motion sequence with a single network evaluation, without teacher queries, iterative sampling, or candidate ranking. On ViMoGen, MixiMotion achieves a semantic alignment score of $0.835$, outperforming the evaluated one-step baselines and approaching the $0.858$ score of its 50-step HY-Motion-1.0-Lite teacher. In blinded human evaluation, MixiMotion obtains an overall rating of $4.33$, compared with $4.50$ for the teacher, while outperforming the evaluated one-/few-step baselines. Meanwhile, generation latency is reduced from $829.58$\,ms to $9.30$\,ms, corresponding to an $89.2\times$ speedup. These results demonstrate an effective quality--efficiency trade-off for strict one-step text-to-motion generation.

</details>


### [53] [CrowdCue: Specialist-Cue Conditioning for Vision-Language Crowd Counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23012)
*Moshiur Farazi,Bekir Ciftler,Abdulhalim Dandoush,Reda Bendraou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将专用人群计数模型的整数值计数作为离散符号提示融入生成式VLM，提出CrowdCue，视觉通道变体达到MAE 62.65，为最佳结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式VLM可同时输出计数和自然语言描述，但原始计数精度仅相当于参数不到百万的专用回归器，需借助预训练专用模型的辅助指导；尚不清楚该指导应通过何种通道传递最有效。

Method: 在Qwen2.5-VL-7B上，于ShanghaiTech A/B、UCF-QNRF、NWPU-Crowd四个基准评估；用LoRA监督微调建立基线；测试将P2PNet密度热力图作为辅助视觉信号；并提出CrowdCue，将专用模型的整数值计数作为离散符号，分别通过文本通道和视觉通道（打印数字作为第二图像）注入。

Result: 零样本提示几乎无法产生可解析计数；LoRA微调基线MAE 81.64；密度热图条件在所有编码下均失败，对抗交换实验显示模型能读取但反向使用；CrowdCue文本通道MAE 72.04，视觉通道MAE 62.65，优于提供提示的专用模型本身（84.45）。

Conclusion: 在研究的后融合VLM中，制约因素不是传递通道，而是专用信号所提供的抽象层次。

Abstract: Generative vision-language models (VLMs) offer a counting paradigm in which one model produces both a count and a natural-language account of the scene, yet their raw counting accuracy sits in the range of sub-million-parameter specialist regressors. The open question is whether auxiliary guidance from a pretrained specialist can lift them into useful territory, and through which channel that guidance is best routed. We evaluate Qwen2.5-VL-7B on four widely used crowd counting benchmarks (ShanghaiTech A and B, UCF-QNRF, NWPU-Crowd). Zero-shot prompting rarely produces a parseable count, so LoRA supervised fine-tuning establishes the baseline at overall MAE 81.64. Conditioning on a P2PNet-derived density heatmap as an auxiliary visual signal fails in every encoding we tested, and an adversarial-swap protocol shows the model reads the heatmap but applies it counterproductively. We propose CrowdCue, a family that supplies the same specialist's already-integrated integer count to the VLM as a discrete symbol. The text-channel variant reaches MAE 72.04. The visual-channel variant, which renders the integer as printed digits and supplies it as a second image, reaches MAE 62.65, the strongest result in this paper and well ahead of the cue-supplying specialist alone (84.45 on the same split). In the late-fusion VLM we study, the binding constraint is not the channel but the abstraction level at which the specialist signal is delivered.

</details>


### [54] [Reconstructed holograms and explanation-aware evaluation for low-cost computational pollen analysis in veterinary cytology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23017)
*Swarn Warshaneyan,Joial Danyal,Blaž Cugmas,Mindaugas Tamošiūnas,Edgars Kviesis-Kipge,Kirishanth Manivannan,Roberts Kadiķis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究评估了无透镜数字全息显微镜重建图像在花粉自动分析中能否缩小与明场显微镜的差距，并检验模型解释在模态变化下的可靠性。重建全息图在检测和分类任务上表现分化，AHIR协议能区分真实的归因失败与模型脆弱性造成的假象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动花粉分析可支持兽医细胞学，但明场显微镜成本更高、更复杂，而无透镜数字全息显微镜成本更低。研究希望评估重建全息图能否缩小这一差距，并检验模型解释在模态变化下是否仍然可靠。

Method: 对六种花粉进行明场和全息成像；生成原始、单次反向传播和迭代相位恢复三种全息图；经基于锚框的标注迁移后，使用YOLOv26s做检测、MobileNetV4做分类；采用六种归因方法评估空间定位与忠实度，并使用AHIR协议测试弱噪声下的模型脆弱性、必要时修正归因图粒度。

Result: 明场检测达0.6890 mAP50-95（0.8865 mAP50），分类达0.9687 macro-F1（0.9705准确率）。重建全息图缩小差距且呈任务依赖分化：p型检测最强（0.5324 mAP50-95，0.8229 mAP50），r型分类最强（0.7695 macro-F1，0.7866准确率），均远高于原始全息图基线。激活类解释在花粉粒上定位良好，区域方法在全息下保留约60–80%忠实度。全息检测器对弱扰动高度脆弱，删除评估饱和而插入仍有信息量。像素级梯度解释接近随机下限，但空间平滑将p型梯度忠实度从0.05恢复到0.51。全息分类中，扰动类解释保持忠实，梯度类低于随机下限。

Conclusion: 重建可改善低成本全息花粉分析；AHIR协议能够区分真实的归因失败与由模型脆弱性和归因图粒度引起的假象。

Abstract: Automated pollen analysis supports veterinary cytology, but brightfield microscopy is costlier and more complex than lens-less digital in-line holographic microscopy. We evaluate whether reconstructed holograms can narrow this gap and whether model explanations remain reliable under modality change. Six pollen species were imaged by brightfield and holographic microscopy. Raw, single back-propagation and iterative phase retrieval holograms were evaluated with YOLOv26s detection and MobileNetV4 classification after anchor-based annotation transfer. Six attribution methods were assessed for spatial grounding and faithfulness with the Attribution Health Inspection and Repair (AHIR) protocol, which tests model brittleness under weak noise and corrects attribution-map granularity when needed. Brightfield achieved 0.6890 mAP50-95 (0.8865 mAP50) for detection and 0.9687 macro-F1 (0.9705 accuracy) for classification. Reconstructed holograms narrowed the gap with a task-dependent split: p-type was strongest for detection at 0.5324 mAP50-95 (0.8229 mAP50), while r-type was strongest for classification at 0.7695 macro-F1 (0.7866 accuracy), both far above raw-hologram baselines. Activation-based explanations localized strongly on grains, and region-based methods retained ~60 to ~80% of faithfulness under holography. The holographic detector was highly brittle to weak perturbations, saturating deletion-based evaluation while insertion remained informative. Pixel-level gradient explanations approached random floor, yet spatial smoothing restored p-type gradient faithfulness from 0.05 to 0.51. For holographic classification, perturbation-based explanations remained faithful while gradient-based methods fell below random floor. Reconstruction improves low-cost holographic pollen analysis, while AHIR distinguishes genuine attribution failure from artifacts caused by model brittleness and map granularity.

</details>


### [55] [BrainIAC: Interactive 3D Brain Lesion Segmentation across Heterogeneous MRI Modalities with Online Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23026)
*Wentian Xu,Anthony P Addison,Ziyun Liang,Harry Anthony,Guang Yang,Konstantinos Kamnitsas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BrainIAC统一框架，整合多模态主干、3D交互式分割与在线自适应机制，解决脑病变分割中的数据分布偏移问题，在七个脑MRI数据集上取得优越表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑病变分割在临床诊断、治疗规划和纵向监测中至关重要，但现有模型难以应对真实临床中的数据分布偏移，这些偏移源于扫描仪硬件差异、成像协议（不同MRI模态组合）以及新出现的病变类型。因此需要一种能同时处理异构模态与在线自适应的统一框架。

Method: BrainIAC包含三个核心组件：（1）多模态主干网络，通过零填充和随机模态丢弃训练，可分割多种脑病变并处理异构模态组合；（2）3D交互式分割，支持边界框和点击提示，无提示时保持全自动预测；（3）在线自适应机制，结合交互中与交互后自适应，以网络自身预测作为伪标签监督，并由额外的点击中心高斯损失引导。

Result: 在七个脑MRI数据集上的实验表明，各组件提供互补且协同的效果。该方法持续优于现有方法，并能很好地泛化到异构成像模态（包括训练中未见过的模态）以及先前未见的脑病变类型。

Conclusion: BrainIAC是首批面向交互式分割的3D在线自适应方法之一，也是首个将异构模态集处理与在线自适应相结合的方法。代码及3D Slicer插件将随论文发表公开。

Abstract: Brain lesion segmentation is a fundamental task in medical image analysis, playing a critical role in diagnosis, treatment planning, and longitudinal disease monitoring. Yet existing models still struggle to meet the demands of real clinical use, where deployments contain data distribution shifts, arising from differences in scanner hardware, imaging protocol (varying MRI modality sets), and new pathologies. We present BrainIAC (Brain lesion Interactive Adaptive Continuously learning segmentation), a unified framework that integrates (i) a multi-modal backbone network trained to segment multiple types of brain lesions and handle heterogeneous sets of modalities via zero-filling and random modality dropping; (ii) 3D interactive segmentation with bounding-box and click prompts that preserves fully automatic prediction when no prompt is given; and (iii) an online adaptation mechanism combining Mid-Interaction adaptation and Post-Interaction adaptation, supervised by the network's own predictions as pseudo labels and guided by an extra Click-Centered Gaussian loss. To our knowledge, this is one of the first 3D online adaptation methods for interactive segmentation, and the first to combine handling of heterogeneous modality sets with online adaptation. Experiments across seven brain MRI datasets demonstrate that the proposed components provide complementary and synergistic benefits. The method consistently outperforms existing approaches and generalizes well across heterogeneous imaging modalities, including those unseen during training, as well as previously unseen brain pathology types. The code and a 3D Slicer plug-in will be released at https://github.com/WenTXuL/BrainIAC upon publication.

</details>


### [56] [VDGS: Visibility-Driven Large-Scale 3D Gaussian Splatting for Aerial Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23049)
*Haolin Yu,Jiadong Tang,YiXian Wang,Yu Gao,Shi He,Zhilin Lai,Yi Yang,Mengyin Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VDGS框架，将相机分布引入3DGS场景建模，通过可见性驱动的锚点统计实现场景分区与梯度补偿，解决大规模航拍场景中的内存消耗与视点不均问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模场景重建是机器人自主系统（如3D建图、自动驾驶）的关键基础技术。3DGS在视觉质量和计算效率上有优势，但在大规模场景中面临内存消耗过大以及UAV采集导致的视点覆盖不均问题，限制了实际应用。

Method: VDGS将相机分布纳入场景建模，为场景锚点引入可见性驱动的统计量以量化监督强度；这些统计量被用于场景分区，并对欠优化区域进行梯度补偿，从而促进不同区域间的均衡优化。

Result: 在多个大规模航拍场景数据集上的大量实验表明，在视点分布不均衡的情况下，VDGS持续优于现有方法；在视点分布较均匀的场景中也能保持有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: VDGS通过将相机分布与可见性统计融入3DGS优化，有效缓解大规模航拍场景的视点不均与优化失衡问题，具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Large-scale scene reconstruction is a critical foundational technology in robotic autonomous systems such as 3D mapping and autonomous driving. In recent years, 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has demonstrated remarkable advantages in both visual quality and computational efficiency, making it a promising representation for large-scale scene reconstruction. However, it still faces challenges in large-scale scenes, including excessive memory consumption and uneven viewpoint coverage caused by UAV acquisition, limiting its real-world applications. To address this, we propose VDGS, a novel 3DGS framework that incorporates camera distribution into scene modeling. VDGS introduces visibility-driven statistics for scene anchors to quantify supervision strength. These statistics are further leveraged for scene partitioning and for gradient compensation in under-optimized regions, thereby promoting balanced optimization across different regions. Extensive experiments on multiple large-scale aerial scene datasets demonstrate that, under imbalanced viewpoint distributions, VDGS consistently outperforms existing methods, while maintaining competitive performance in scenarios with more uniform view distributions.

</details>


### [57] [HDMamba-YOLO: Efficient State-Space Perception and Local Spatial Reconstruction for UAV Small Object](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23061)
*Linduo Wei,Junjie Fan,Yijun Mai,Yong Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HDMamba-YOLO，一种按"感知-重建-对齐-交互"分阶段异构设计的SSM-CNN无人机小目标检测器，在VisDrone2019和AI-TOD上取得竞争力的精度与效率平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机图像中小目标检测面临视觉证据弱、边界模糊、目标密集分布和背景复杂等挑战。有效检测既需要长程上下文信息以区分目标与背景，又需要保留显式的局部二维结构以准确定位。这两类需求出现在检测流程的不同阶段，统一的特征处理策略难以同时满足。

Method: 提出HDMamba-YOLO，采用分阶段异构SSM-CNN架构，遵循感知-重建-对齐-交互的设计思路。主干中使用基于EfficientVMamba的EVSS建立长程上下文感知，PhasePatchMerging2D实现相位感知的层级过渡；DST-Wrapper与Native C3k2-ASSAF在FPN/PAN聚合中完成感知到重建的过渡及重复局部二维重建；DySample提供内容自适应的跨尺度重采样；OS-CVTIA引入宏微交互与任务特定调制，分别服务于定位与分类。

Result: 在VisDrone2019上，HDMamba-YOLO-B达到42.737% mAP50和25.713% mAP50:95，参数量10.042M，修正GFLOPs为29.879；Lite版本达到41.140% mAP50和24.741% mAP50:95，参数量5.344M。在统一AI-TOD评估协议下，B版本获得21.621% AP和47.881% AP50。消融实验进一步验证了状态空间感知、卷积重建、动态对齐与任务交互的分阶段分配有效性。

Conclusion: 分阶段异构地分配状态空间感知、卷积重建、动态对齐与任务交互，能有效应对无人机小目标检测中长程上下文与局部结构需求并存的难题，实现精度与效率的良好平衡。

Abstract: Small-object detection in UAV imagery is challenged by weak visual evidence, ambiguous boundaries, dense object distributions, and complex backgrounds. Effective detection therefore requires long-range contextual information for target-background discrimination while preserving explicit local two-dimensional structures for accurate localization. These requirements arise at different stages of the detection pipeline and are not naturally addressed by a uniform feature-processing strategy. We propose Hybrid Dual-domain Mamba-YOLO (HDMamba-YOLO), a stage-wise heterogeneous SSM-CNN detector organized according to a perception-reconstruction-alignment-interaction rationale. EfficientVMamba-based EVSS establishes long-range contextual perception in the backbone, while PhasePatchMerging2D provides phase-aware hierarchical transitions. DST-Wrapper and Native C3k2-ASSAF then perform perception-to-reconstruction transition and repeated local two-dimensional reconstruction during FPN/PAN aggregation. DySample provides content-adaptive cross-scale resampling, while OS-CVTIA introduces macro-micro interaction and task-specific modulation for localization and classification. On VisDrone2019, HDMamba-YOLO-B achieves 42.737% mAP50 and 25.713% mAP50:95 with 10.042M parameters and 29.879 corrected GFLOPs. HDMamba-YOLO-Lite achieves 41.140% mAP50 and 24.741% mAP50:95 with 5.344M parameters. Under the unified AI-TOD evaluation protocol, HDMamba-YOLO-B obtains 21.621% AP and 47.881% AP50. Controlled ablations further support the stage-wise allocation of state-space perception, convolutional reconstruction, dynamic alignment, and task interaction for UAV small-object detection.

</details>


### [58] [StyleAT: Defending Face Recognition Against Semantic Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23596)
*Ben Shapira,Roi Cohen,Shang-Tse Chen,Mahmood Sharif*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BoundStyle语义对抗攻击方法，在StyleGAN潜空间中高效生成语义扰动，比现有SOTA攻击快约9.5倍，并基于此提出StyleAT对抗训练方案，提升模型对语义攻击的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脸识别模型广泛用于身份认证与监控，但对语义对抗编辑（如轻微老化、姿态变化）极为脆弱。现有攻击虽强但计算代价高，难以用于对抗训练等防御开发，因此需要一种高效且强力的语义攻击方法。

Method: 提出BoundStyle，在StyleGAN丰富的潜空间中操作，通过优化最大化误分类率来生成语义对抗样本；并在其基础上开发StyleAT对抗训练方案，融合低预算攻击变体进行训练。

Result: BoundStyle在保持高攻击成功率的同时，比现有最先进攻击快约9.5倍；在训练未见过的两个数据集和七个模型上评估，StyleAT在多种设置下提升了对SOTA攻击的鲁棒准确率，并优于常见防御方法。

Conclusion: BoundStyle证明高效语义攻击的可行性，使其适用于对抗训练；StyleAT能够有效防御更强及未见过的语义攻击，为鲁棒人脸识别提供了实用方案。

Abstract: With face-recognition models now embedded in everyday authentication and surveillance, recent works have pinpointed a critical weakness: these models remain acutely vulnerable to adversarial semantic edits. I.e., adversarially produced semantic alterations to the input, such as slight aging or pose changes, can induce misclassifications. Certain existing attacks are powerful, but they can be computationally costly, rendering them inadequate for developing defenses (e.g., through adversarial training). To fill the gap, we introduce BoundStyle, a potent semantic attack operating in StyleGAN's rich latent space to maximize misclassification rates. Notably, BoundStyle achieves high attack success rates while being ${\sim}{\times}9.5$ faster than existing state-of-the-art attacks, making it suitable for adversarial training. Building on BoundStyle, we develop StyleAT, an efficient adversarial training scheme that incorporates low-budget attack variants yet defends against stronger and unseen semantic attacks. We evaluate on two datasets unseen during training and seven models, and find that StyleAT boosts robust accuracy against state-of-the-art attacks and outperforms common defenses in various settings.

</details>


### [59] [LD-RSVIS: A Large-Scale and Diverse Benchmark for Referring Surgical Video Instrument Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23067)
*Zan Wang,Yunhe Feng,Dong Nie,Oluwatosin Oluwadare,Kewei Sha,Yan Huang,Heng Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LD-RSVIS，一个大规模、多样化的指代手术视频器械分割（RSVIS）基准，包含3,536个视频、109万帧、30类器械和25种术式，并支持无目标、单目标、多目标指代设置；同时提出Cascade-RSVIS方法，取得有前景的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RSVIS方法仅在相对小规模基准上训练和评估，限制其通用性；且现有基准只支持单目标指代表达，忽略多目标和无目标情形，限制了实际应用。

Method: 构建LD-RSVIS基准：3,536个手术视频、109万帧、30类器械、25种术式，经多轮人工标注与检查；提供无目标、单目标、多目标指代设置；评估12种代表性方法；并提出Cascade-RSVIS，先利用互补多线索文本信息挖掘目标特定线索，再结合该线索与文本信息进行分割。

Result: 评估12种代表性方法显示仍需更多改进；所提Cascade-RSVIS取得有前景的性能。

Conclusion: LD-RSVIS是迄今最大、最多样的RSVIS基准，可促进更通用、更实用的RSVIS研究；基准与代码将公开。

Abstract: Referring surgical video instrument segmentation (RSVIS) aims at segmenting the instrument in a surgical video, given a textual description. Despite recent progress, current models are trained and assessed on relatively small-scale benchmarks, hindering the development of more general RSVIS. In addition, existing benchmarks support only the single-target expression that refers to one instrument in the video, while overlooking multi-target and no-target referring expressions, restricting the applicability of RSVIS in practical scenarios. Addressing these issues, we propose LD-RSVIS, a new benchmark aiming to facilitate more robust and general RSVIS. Specifically, LD-RSVIS consists of 3,536 surgical videos with 1.09 million frames and covers a broad set of 30 instrument classes from 25 various procedures. By including abundant videos and classes, LD-RSVIS could benefit large-scale training and evaluation of more general RSVIS methods. Besides, unlike existing datasets, LD-RSVIS offers diverse referring settings, including no-target, single-target, and multi-target expressions, which enables the development of more practical RSVIS models in real applications. In order to ensure high-quality annotations, all videos in LD-RSVIS are manually labeled with multiple rounds of inspection and refinement. To our knowledge, LD-RSVIS is the largest and most diverse benchmark for RSVIS. To analyze LD-RSVIS and to provide comparison for future research, we evaluate 12 representative methods, and the results reveal that more efforts are required for improvements. To encourage future research, we present a simple yet effective RSVIS method, dubbed Cascade-RSVIS, that first mines target-specific cues using the complementary multi-cue text information and then employs such cues and textual information for segmentation, achieving promising performance. Our benchmark and code will be released.

</details>


### [60] [MM-ContextFold: Context Folding for Multimodal Agentic Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23121)
*Yang Tian,Fan Liu,Jingyuan Zhang,Zhenyang Li,Yupeng Hu,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出免训练的 MM-ContextFold 框架，通过仅在需要时加载原始图像、为主上下文保留纯文本并折叠图像子任务结果，解决多模态智能体检索中的上下文爆炸问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态智能体检索（MAR）中，ReAct 等框架将原始多模态输入和不断累积的交互历史保存在单一且持续增长的上下文中，导致上下文爆炸。现有方法多通过压缩冗余文本来缓解，但对令牌密集的视觉内容如何管理仍缺乏有效策略。

Method: 作者先对约 10,000 条轨迹进行系统性实证研究，发现随着视觉线索被外部工具逐步提取并文本化到上下文，原始图像日益冗余，持续保留图像会提高输出熵甚至降低任务准确率。基于此提出免训练框架 MM-ContextFold：仅按需加载原始图像，维护一个持久的纯文本主上下文用于高层规划，并为依赖图像的子任务生成临时分支上下文；在分支中加载相关图像、完成子任务、将结果折叠为简洁文本摘要回写主上下文，随后丢弃图像和分支轨迹。

Result: 在五个骨干模型、七个 MAR 基准上，MM-ContextFold 相比 ReAct 平均准确率提升 6.3 个百分点，同时工作上下文长度减少 27.5%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，多模态智能体检索中持续保留原始图像并不必要，反而可能有害；按需加载图像并将图像子任务结果文本化折叠回主上下文，可在显著缩短上下文的同时提升任务准确率，为视觉上下文管理提供了有效且免训练的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Agentic Retrieval (MAR) requires agents to solve complex information-seeking tasks by iteratively invoking external tools. Typical frameworks such as ReAct maintain raw multimodal inputs and the accumulating interaction history in a single, ever-growing context, leading to the context explosion problem. While existing methods alleviate this issue by compressing redundant text, effective strategies for managing token-intensive visual content remain largely underexplored. To address this gap, we first conduct a systematic empirical study of approximately 10,000 trajectories. The results show that as visual cues are progressively extracted through external tools and textualized into the context, raw images become increasingly redundant. Continued image retention is associated with higher output entropy and can even degrade task accuracy. Motivated by these findings, we propose MM-ContextFold, a training-free framework that loads raw images only when needed. It maintains a persistent, text-only main context for high-level planning and spawns ephemeral branch contexts for image-dependent subtasks. Within each branch, the agent loads the relevant images, completes the subtask, and folds the result back into the main context as a concise textual summary; the images and branch trace are then discarded. Experiments on seven MAR benchmarks across five backbone models show that MM-ContextFold improves average accuracy by 6.3 percentage points over ReAct while reducing the working context length by 27.5\%.

</details>


### [61] [QwenVLConnector: A Fast, Unified Medical VLM Chatbot for Fine-Grained Clinical Perception and Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23139)
*Le Thien Phuc Nguyen,Thien Nguyen,Thanh-Huy Nguyen,Gia Minh Hoang,Anh Mai Vu,Ulas Bagci*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出QwenVLConnector，一个基于Qwen2.5-VL的医疗聊天机器人，在统一的下一个token目标下整合分类、多标签分类、文本化检测、计数、回归和自由形式报告生成等任务。核心是轻量级密集多层Connector，聚合低层与高层视觉特征，经预训练视觉Merger对齐后与最终视觉表示融合，不增加序列长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数医疗视觉语言模型擅长开放式报告生成和VQA，但在统一接口内对结构化、细粒度的临床感知支持有限。

Method: 采用轻量级密集多层Connector，聚合低层和高层视觉特征，通过预训练视觉Merger对齐，并与最终视觉表示融合，保持序列长度不变，从而用互补的空间与语义线索丰富视觉token。同时探索了用于报告生成的多模态上下文学习。

Result: 在FLARE-2D上，检测F1从0.55提升到0.85，单标签分类从0.37提升到0.51，报告生成GREEN较Qwen2.5-VL基线最高提升18.3分；多模态上下文学习在不更新参数情况下带来额外提升。

Conclusion: QwenVLConnector为结合结构化医疗感知与开放式临床文本生成提供了一个统一且高效的框架，代码已开源。

Abstract: Most medical vision-language models (VLMs) excel at open-ended report generation and VQA but provide limited support for structured, fine-grained clinical perception within a unified interface. We present QwenVLConnector, a Qwen2.5-VL-based medical chatbot that unifies classification, multi-label classification, textualized detection, counting, regression, and free-form report generation under a single next-token objective. Our key component is a lightweight dense multi-layer Connector that aggregates low- and high-level visual features, aligns them through the pretrained vision Merger, and fuses them with the final visual representation without increasing sequence length. This design enriches visual tokens with complementary spatial and semantic cues while preserving efficiency. On FLARE-2D, QwenVLConnector improves detection F1 from 0.55 to 0.85, raises single-label classification from 0.37 to 0.51, and boosts report-generation GREEN by up to 18.3 points over the Qwen2.5-VL baseline. We further explore multimodal in-context learning for report generation, showing additional improvements without updating model parameters. Overall, QwenVLConnector offers a unified and efficient framework for combining structured medical perception with open-ended clinical text generation. Our code can be found at https://github.com/plnguyen2908/QwenConnector.

</details>


### [62] [Dissecting Agentic Forensics: The Role of Triage, Prompting, and Evidence Arbitration in Open-World Fake Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24359)
*Xianlong Li,Pietro Bongini,Niccoló Pancino,Marco Blanchini,Benedetta Tondi,Mauro Barni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究免训练智能体图像取证框架，发现推理质量是性能主导因素，操作召回接近饱和，真正挑战在于校准对检测器的信任与仲裁冲突证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像取证正成为开放世界问题，操纵类型涵盖全合成、局部编辑、拼接与交换等多种家族，而现有取证检测器多只针对单一操纵类型。智能体AI虽有潜力评估检测器可靠性、识别超范围证据并仲裁冲突报告，但尚不清楚哪些组件真正驱动性能，以及其收益能否在分布偏移下保持。

Method: 设计免训练智能体框架，围绕专家检测器、逐检测器分流（triage）与冲突感知的证据仲裁构建。使用六种配置和三种多模态大语言模型骨干，在分布内与分布外数据上剖析分流、提示与推理质量的作用。

Result: 朴素检测器融合在真实图像上误报率严重。分流与提示通过过滤不可靠证据、暴露检测器局限而持续提升性能。主导因素是推理本身：更强的评判模型显著优于较弱者，在分布偏移下尤为明显。操作召回在所有配置下几乎饱和。

Conclusion: 开放世界图像取证的主要挑战不在于检测操纵，而在于校准对专用取证工具的信任并仲裁冲突证据。

Abstract: Image forensics is increasingly an open-world problem: manipulations range from fully synthetic images to localized edits, splicing and swapping, while most forensic detectors remain specialized to a single manipulation family. Agentic AI has recently emerged as a promising solution. In principle, such systems can assess the reliability of individual detectors, identify out-of-scope evidence, and arbitrate conflicting reports. However, it remains unclear which components actually drive performance and whether their benefits persist under distribution shift. To answer these questions, we study a training-free agentic framework built around specialist detectors, per-detector triage, and conflict-aware evidence arbitration. Using six configurations and three multimodal large language model backbones, we dissect the role of triage, prompting, and reasoning quality on both in-distribution and out-of-distribution data. Our results show that naive detector fusion suffers from severe false-positive rates on authentic images. Triage and prompting consistently improve performance by filtering unreliable evidence and exposing detector limitations. However, the dominant factor is represented by reasoning itself: A stronger judge substantially outperforms a weaker one, particularly under distribution shift. Most notably, manipulation recall is nearly saturated across all configurations, indicating that the main challenge of open-world image forensics is not detecting manipulations, but calibrating trust in specialized forensic tools and arbitrating conflicting evidence.

</details>


### [63] [SparkDiffusion: Mitigating the High-Sparsity Trap --- A Unified Framework for up to $265\times$ Single-GPU Acceleration of Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23153)
*Yuxi Liu,Haoyu Li,Zekun Zhang,Tengxu Sun,Yixiang Cai,Jiayong Li,Yifei Xia,Tianle Liu,Baole Ai,Ang Wang,Jiamang Wang,Lin Qu,Kai Zhang,Kun Yuan,Bin Cui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文识别出视频扩散Transformer在极端注意力稀疏下的"高稀疏陷阱"，提出"先粗先验后终端校正"的分阶段原则，并实例化为统一加速框架，实现高达265倍端到端加速且保持视觉质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散Transformer因注意力机制在长时空token序列上的二次复杂度而计算昂贵。作者发现"高稀疏陷阱"：在极端注意力稀疏时，逐步局部训练损失持续下降，但最终生成质量停滞甚至退化。该陷阱本质是监督问题——主要终端误差源于高噪声的结构生成阶段，终端对齐训练能纠正逐步局部训练无法纠正的误差。

Method: 提出"先让稀疏架构适配为粗先验，再校正终端分布"的分阶段原则，并实例化为一个统一加速框架：结合短稀疏预热、少步轨迹混合蒸馏，以及带融合核的FP8量化。

Result: 在Wan2.1/Wan2.2骨干和T2V/I2V任务的长序列720P生成上，维持97%注意力稀疏度且视觉质量强；在Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B-480P上达90%稀疏度。3步无CFG推理下，Wan2.1-T2V-14B-720P在单张RTX 5090上相比50步CFG稠密基线实现265倍端到端加速（H100上220倍），并将Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B-480P视频去噪缩短至1.3秒。

Conclusion: 通过分阶段训练原则（先粗先验、后终端校正）并结合稀疏注意力、蒸馏与量化，可有效突破高稀疏陷阱，在保持高质量视频生成的同时实现数量级的端到端加速。

Abstract: Video diffusion transformers are expensive because attention dominates long spatiotemporal token sequences. We identify the \emph{high-sparsity trap}: at extreme attention sparsity, step-local training losses keep decreasing while terminal generation quality stagnates or degrades. The trap is one of supervision: the dominant terminal errors originate in the high-noise structure-generation stage, and terminal-aligned training corrects terminal errors that substantially extended step-local training cannot. This yields a simple staging principle: \emph{first adapt the sparse architecture into a coarse prior, then correct the terminal distribution}. We instantiate the principle as \method, a unified acceleration framework for visual generation that combines a short sparse warm-up, few-step trajectory-mixed distillation, and FP8 quantization with fused kernels. \method sustains $97\%$ attention sparsity with strong visual quality on long-sequence 720P generation across Wan2.1/Wan2.2 backbones and T2V/I2V tasks, and $90\%$ sparsity on Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B-480P. With 3-step CFG-free inference, \method achieves a $265\times$ end-to-end speedup over the 50-step CFG dense baseline for Wan2.1-T2V-14B-720P on a single RTX~5090 ($220\times$ on H100), and denoises a Wan2.1-T2V-1.3B-480P video in $1.3$s.

</details>


### [64] [MIRAGE: Full-Body Bystander Privacy for Smart Glasses with Consent-Based Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24537)
*Muhammad Umair,Muhammad Danial Maqbool,Fatima Arshad Cheema,Kapal Dev,Muhammad Hamad Alizai,Muhammad Ali Siddiqi,Naveed Anwar Bhatti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MIRAGE三层架构,在智能眼镜上实现全身隐私保护,支持合成全身替换并保留加密恢复材料,有效抵抗基于步态和轮廓的重识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能眼镜的连续视频采集不仅暴露人脸,还泄露步态、姿态和轮廓等全身生物特征,使得即便对人脸进行脱敏处理后,仍可通过全身特征进行人物重识别(ReID),现有隐私保护方案难以覆盖这一威胁。

Method: MIRAGE采用三层架构,部署在Raspberry Pi 5(作为智能眼镜算力的纯CPU代理)、配套手机和云端生成式后端;通过精确检测完整可见身体,进行边界框掩蔽,并支持合成全身替换,同时保留加密恢复材料以供经同意的恢复使用。

Result: MIRAGE达到0.948 AP和0.976 AR,能准确检测完整可见身体;边界框掩蔽使基于轮廓的重识别降至近乎随机猜测,Rank-1准确率10.86%,而实测随机水平为11.12%;即使面对在MIRAGE脱敏姿态信号上重训练的适应性对手,步态识别Rank-1从90.25%降至26.20%,消除对手72.5%的识别优势。

Conclusion: MIRAGE能够在智能眼镜场景下有效实现全身隐私保护,在保持身体检测精度的同时大幅削弱轮廓与步态层面的重识别能力,兼顾隐私与经同意的可恢复性。

Abstract: Video recording on smart glasses exposes more than faces. Continuous capture reveals full-body biometric signatures, including gait, posture, and silhouette, that enable person re-identification (ReID) even after conventional face sanitization.
  We present MIRAGE, a three-tier architecture for privacy-preserving smart glasses that enforces full-body privacy, supports synthetic full-body replacement, and retains encrypted recovery material for consent-based restoration. We implement MIRAGE on a Raspberry Pi~5 (a CPU-only proxy for smart-glasses compute), companion phones, and a cloud generative backend. Compared to prior systems, MIRAGE achieves 0.948 AP and 0.976 AR while accurately detecting the complete visible body. Its bounding box masking reduces learned silhouette-based ReID to essentially random guessing, with 10.86% Rank-1 accuracy compared with an 11.12% measured chance level. Even against an adaptive adversary retrained on MIRAGE's sanitized pose signals, Rank-1 gait identification drops from 90.25% to 26.20%, removing 72.5% of the adversary's identification advantage.

</details>


### [65] [An Eternal Irradiance Camera](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23161)
*Jeremy Klotz,Shree K. Nayar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种全向辐照度相机，仅用少量探测器即可重建球面辐照度函数，并可完全由环境光供能，兼顾低功耗与隐私保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相机需数百万像素测量视场内各方向辐射，功耗高且会捕获可识别个人身份的视觉细节，带来隐私问题。作者希望以极少量测量捕获光照信息。

Method: 在频域分析球面辐照度函数，证明其可由前七个球谐阶次的加权和良好近似，因而少量采样即可精确重建；据此构建原型FluxCam，将49个探测器布置于球面，并由落在探测器上的光供能，无线回传数据。

Result: 在光照良好的室内环境中，FluxCam以30帧/秒读出并无线传输测量值，无需电池、线缆或外部电源；并展示了作为光学陀螺仪计算旋转、监测工作空间、非束缚光探针用于漫反射重光照，以及作为全向总辐射表估计天空状况并确定太阳能板最佳朝向等应用。

Conclusion: 辐照度函数是带限且低频的，少量探测器即可捕获，从而实现自供能、保护隐私的全向光照传感，并支持多种实际应用。

Abstract: A conventional camera uses millions of pixels to measure radiance from all directions within its field of view. We present an omnidirectional irradiance camera that measures the irradiance function---the illumination incident upon every point on a sphere. The irradiance function varies smoothly over the sphere and hence is bandlimited. We have analyzed this function in the frequency domain and have shown that it is well approximated by a weighted sum of the first seven degrees of spherical harmonics. As a result, the irradiance function can be accurately reconstructed from a small number of samples. This implies that an irradiance camera does not need millions of detectors (pixels)---just a handful of measurements suffice. This brings two major benefits. First, the camera consumes such little energy that it can be completely powered by the light falling on its detectors. Second, it does not capture the visual details needed to identify an individual, and hence privacy is preserved. We have built a prototype irradiance camera, called FluxCam, using 49 detectors arranged on the surface of a sphere. In a well-lit indoor environment, FluxCam can read out and wirelessly transmit its measurements at 30 frames per second using energy harvested from the light falling on it (i.e., without a battery, cable, or external power supply). We show how FluxCam can be used as an optical gyroscope for computing rotation, to monitor a workspace, as an untethered light probe for diffuse relighting, and as an omnidirectional pyranometer for estimating sky conditions and determining the best orientation of a solar panel.

</details>


### [66] [UltraTex: Unleashing 2K Multi-View Diffusion for 3D Texturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23169)
*Yibo Zhang,Ze Yuan,Nan Cao,Li Zhang,Yan-Pei Cao,Yuan-Chen Guo,Rui Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UltraTex 是一个高效的端到端框架，用于基于多视图扩散的高分辨率（2K）3D 纹理生成，通过去除冗余并加速计算显著提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像引导的多视图扩散纹理方法受限于低分辨率（512/768），难以保留高分辨率参考图中的高频细节。而直接扩展到 2048 分辨率会导致多视图序列超过 212K tokens，带来过高的内存和延迟开销。

Method: 观察到以物体为中心的多视图渲染存在背景引起的序列冗余和前景内稀疏 token 交互两类冗余。为此提出：Background Token Dropping（在 DiT 主干前去除背景 tokens）、Block-Sparse Attention（减少前景序列的注意力计算）、Foreground-Aware VAE Decoding（保证最终高分辨率视图质量、避免重建伪影）。此外构建了 G-buffer TexVerse 大规模超高分辨率多视图渲染数据集，覆盖超 26.8 万个 3D 资产。

Result: UltraTex 生成视觉逼真、细粒度细节丰富的纹理，并大幅提升效率，在数据集常见样本上相比基线实现 20.6×–91.1× 训练加速和 22.3×–74.6× 端到端推理加速。

Conclusion: UltraTex 为高分辨率 3D 纹理生成提供了一种高效、可扩展的端到端方案，在保证高质量纹理的同时显著降低计算成本。

Abstract: High-quality texture generation is essential for creating realistic and production-ready 3D assets. Recent multi-view diffusion methods have shown promising results for image-guided 3D texturing, but they are typically constrained to low operating resolutions such as 512 or 768, making it difficult to preserve high-frequency details from high-resolution reference images. Scaling this paradigm to 2048 resolution is computationally prohibitive, as the unified multi-view sequence exceeds 212K tokens and incurs excessive memory and latency. In this paper, we present UltraTex, an efficient end-to-end framework for high-resolution multi-view diffusion-based 3D texturing. Our key observation is that object-centric multi-view renderings contain two major sources of redundancy: background-induced sequence redundancy and sparse token interactions within the foreground. To address them, we introduce Background Token Dropping, which removes background tokens before the DiT backbone, and Block-Sparse Attention, which reduces attention computation over the retained foreground sequence. To enable efficient foreground-only inference while avoiding reconstruction artifacts, we further design Foreground-Aware VAE Decoding to ensure the quality of the final high-resolution views. To satisfy the demanding data requirements of 2K-resolution multi-view diffusion training, we construct G-buffer TexVerse, a large-scale, ultra-high-resolution multi-view rendering dataset covering over 268,000 3D assets. Extensive experiments show that UltraTex generates visually faithful textures with rich fine-grained details, while substantially improving efficiency, achieving $20.6\times$--$91.1\times$ training speedup and $22.3\times$--$74.6\times$ end-to-end inference speedup over the baseline on common samples in our dataset. Code and data is at https://yiboz2001.github.io/UltraTex.

</details>


### [67] [GrapeSplat: Geometry-Grounded Reconstruction via Amalgamated Pose-Free Encoding for Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23182)
*Si-Yu Lu,Yung-Yao Chen,Yi Jan Chen,Shang-Lin Li,Ching-Chan Liao,Wen-Huang Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GrapeSplat 是一种前馈 3D 高斯泼溅方法，用体素对齐的场景表示直接从学习到的网格解码高斯，无需逐场景优化，并能从 4 到 64 个视角零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈 3D 高斯泼溅虽能从无位姿、未标定图像重建可渲染场景，但多只监督光度一致性并逐像素预测高斯，导致全局结构脆弱，且图元数量与图像分辨率和视角数绑定。

Method: GrapeSplat 将多视角线索融合为体素对齐的场景表示，直接从学习到的网格解码高斯。Atlas Encoder 将各视角提升为锚定在预测 3D 点上的逐像素几何与外观特征；PEACH-Vox 通过平滑的逐轴映射及精确闭式逆映射，把无界场景压缩为有界稀疏网格；Sparse Decoder 用稀疏卷积整合该网格，并按占用单元解码多个高斯。

Result: 该方法利用稀疏体素占用：高斯数量随占用单元变化，并在视角覆盖场景后饱和，而网格分辨率设定其上限。在 8 视角序列上用 2D 和 3D 监督训练后，可在室内和无界场景中从 4 到 64 视角零样本泛化。代码与训练权重已开源。

Conclusion: GrapeSplat 通过体素对齐与稀疏解码，将无位姿图像单次前向传播即可转为可渲染高斯场景，无需逐场景优化或后处理，并具备良好的视角泛化能力。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D Gaussian Splatting now reconstructs renderable scenes from unposed, uncalibrated images. Yet, most models supervise only photometric consistency and predict Gaussians pixel by pixel, which leaves global structure fragile and ties primitive count to image resolution and view count. To this end, GrapeSplat amalgamates multi-view cues into a voxel-aligned scene representation and decodes Gaussians directly from the learned grid, requiring no per-scene optimization or post-processing. An Atlas Encoder lifts all views into pixel-wise geometry-and-appearance features anchored at predicted 3D points. PEACH-Vox compands the unbounded scene into a bounded sparse grid through a smooth per-axis map with an exact closed-form inverse. The Sparse Decoder then consolidates the grid with sparse convolutions and decodes the full scene as multiple Gaussians per occupied cell. This amalgamated representation exploits sparse voxel occupancy, where the Gaussian count follows the occupied cells and saturates as views cover the scene, while grid resolution sets its ceiling. GrapeSplat turns unposed images into a renderable Gaussian scene in a single forward pass. Trained with 2D and 3D supervision on 8-view sequences, it generalizes zero-shot from 4 to 64 views across indoor and unbounded scenes. Code and trained weights are available at https://github.com/VAISR/GrapeSplat

</details>


### [68] [CausalWM: Causal Chain-of-Thought Reasoning for Embodied World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23184)
*Ziming Xu,Shuang Liang,Ruobing Han,Ziqiao Xi,Mingxing Rao,Kun Zhou,Zijun Zhang,Yuchen Yan,Yufan Wei,Junbo Huang,Yifei Shao,Fang Nan,Biwei Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CausalWM是一个16B参数具身世界模型，在预测未来视频前进行显式因果思维链推理，通过三阶段训练范式实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身世界模型将物理知识隐式纠缠在潜表征中，缺乏显式的因果推理能力，难以刻画物理演化背后的因果依赖关系。

Method: 提出CausalWM（16B具身世界模型），将有用变量组织为推理轨迹以显式进行因果思维链推理；收集31K小时具身数据，采用三阶段训练范式：大规模视频预训练、因果CoT中期训练、多目标强化学习后训练。

Result: 尽管仅使用有限的监督CoT变量，模型涌现出上下文学习能力，支持上下文视觉特征引导和高效少步生成；在语言条件、动作条件、单视图及多视图基准上达到SOTA，并在TriWorldBench排行榜取得Top-1。

Conclusion: 显式因果思维链推理可有效提升具身世界模型的物理动态预测能力，并在多种基准上取得领先表现。

Abstract: Embodied world models learn to predict future physical dynamics from visual observations and control signals, where physical knowledge is implicitly entangled within latent representations. We introduce CausalWM, a 16B embodied world model that performs explicit causal chain-of-thought reasoning before future video prediction. CausalWM organizes useful variables into a reasoning trajectory, allowing the model to progressively capture causal dependencies underlying physical evolution. To train CausalWM, we collect 31K hours embodied data and develop a three-stage paradigm consisting of large-scale video pre-training, causal CoT mid-training, and multi-objective RL post-training. Despite using only a limited set of supervised CoT variables, CausalWM exhibits emergent in-context learning capabilities, enabling contextual visual feature guidance and efficient few-step generation. CausalWM achieves state-of-the-art performance across language-conditioned, action-conditioned, single-view and multi-view benchmarks, including Top-1 performance on TriWorldBench leaderboard.

</details>


### [69] [SPACE: Semantic Projection and Alignment of CLIP Embeddings for Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23248)
*João Renato Ribeiro Manesco,Danilo Samuel Jodas,Douglas Rodrigues,Leandro Aparecido Passos,João Paulo Papa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SPACE方法，利用CLIP视觉-语言空间的语义结构进行无监督域适应，通过文本描述构建语义锚点，将两个域的视觉特征投影到语义子空间，实现基于语义而非外观的对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉模型部署面临域偏移问题，当同一语义概念以不同视觉形式（如照片与草图）出现时，视觉相似性弱但语义对应，性能退化尤为严重。现有无监督域适应方法虽对齐域间分布，却常忽略同类样本间的语义关系。

Method: SPACE利用CLIP视觉-语言空间的语义结构：以文本描述作为语义锚点，对类别描述的CLIP嵌入应用奇异值分解（SVD），得到能捕捉类别间语义关系的正交基；随后将两个域的视觉特征投影到该语义子空间，从而依据语义而非外观对齐图像。

Result: 摘要未提供具体量化结果，仅说明该方法通过语义子空间投影实现了基于语义的图像对齐，以缓解视觉相似性弱带来的域偏移问题。

Conclusion: SPACE通过引入CLIP语义结构与SVD构建的语义子空间，弥补了传统域适应忽略类内语义关系的不足，为跨视觉形式的域适应提供了语义驱动的对齐思路。

Abstract: A fundamental challenge in deploying vision models is domain shift, which arises when training and test data follow different distributions, leading to degraded performance. This challenge is amplified when the same semantic concept appears under distinct visual forms, such as photographs and sketches, where visual similarity is weak despite semantic correspondence. Existing unsupervised domain-adaptation methods aim to align distributions across domains but often ignore semantic relationships among samples of the same class. To address this issue, this paper introduces SPACE, a method that exploits the semantic structure of CLIP's vision-language space for domain adaptation. The key idea is to use text descriptions as semantic anchors by applying Singular Value Decomposition to CLIP embeddings of class descriptions, yielding an orthogonal basis that captures semantic relationships among categories.
