Visual features from both domains are projected into this semantic subspace, aligning images based on meaning rather than appearance.

</details>


### [70] [Exact Quotients of Fresnel-Kummer Surfaces and Certified Biaxial Refraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23249)
*Tanush Shaska*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文精确识别了双轴晶体中Fresnel波面为显式亏格二曲线Jacobian的Kummer四次曲面，刻画了其与两椭圆曲线乘积的同源结构，并给出波面参数的不变量、节点闭式解及界面透射波的精确代数判定方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fresnel波面是双轴晶体中光传播的核心对象，属于特殊的Kummer四次曲面，但此前缺乏对其精确代数身份的完整刻画。同时，物理参数存在冗余变换，界面透射波判定对数值方法敏感，需要一个能够识别冗余参数并给出精确代数判据的框架。

Method: 将复波面识别为显式亏格二曲线的Jacobian的Kummer曲面（分支点为三个主介电常数的带符号平方根）；构造核为四阶的同源，将其映到两条椭圆曲线之积；利用"任务感知商"识别冗余变换下的参数，提取不变坐标与显式分层。对界面透射波，将候选波视为精确四次方程的根，用精确代数谓词判定实根数、根序与重根事件。

Result: 物理族在带额外对合的亏格二曲线轨迹中Zariski稠密，且其自同构分层显式可得。两个介电常数比值构成波面在旋转与缩放下的完全不变量，四个实节点有闭式表达。沿一般单节点相遇，判别式二阶消失；浮点求解器会丢失光轴附近的透射模式，而认证求解器不会。学习模型在商坐标上具有不变性且比原始张量模型更准确，但学习到的根数谓词在光轴附近失效。

Conclusion: 该工作通过代数几何精确识别了Fresnel波面并实例化了任务感知商，为双轴晶体提供了参数不变量、节点闭式解与界面透射波的精确代数判定，同时揭示了浮点方法与学习模型在光轴附近的失效风险。

Abstract: The Fresnel wave surface governs the propagation of light in a transparent biaxial crystal. It is a special Kummer quartic, and we identify it exactly. Over the complex numbers the wave surface of a crystal is the Kummer surface of the Jacobian of an explicit genus-two curve branched at the signed square roots of the three principal permittivities. This Jacobian is isogenous, by an isogeny with kernel of order four, to a product of two elliptic curves. One elliptic curve carries the three permittivities, and the other carries the optic-axis angle. The physical family is Zariski dense in the locus of genus-two curves with an extra involution, and its automorphism strata are explicit. The identification instantiates a task-aware quotient, which identifies parameters that differ by a nuisance transformation and carries invariant coordinates and explicit strata. For biaxial crystals, two ratios of the permittivities form a complete invariant of the wave surface up to rotation and rescaling, and the four real nodes are given in closed form. At an interface the candidate transmitted waves are the roots of a quartic of exact degree four. Its real-root count, root order, and repeated-root events are decided by exact algebraic predicates, and along the generic single-node encounters of the paper its discriminant vanishes to second order. Floating-point solvers drop forward transmitted modes near the optic axes, and the certified solver does not. On exact equivalence classes, learned models on quotient coordinates are invariant and more accurate than models on raw tensors, while learned root-count predicates fail near the optic axes.

</details>


### [71] [Blind Deconvolution of Binary and Pattern Images with Pixel Intensity Constraints and Sparse Gradient Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23268)
*Qinghua Zhang,Xuesong Yang,Liangtian He,Liang-jian Deng,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对二值图像和图案图像的盲图像去卷积统一框架，通过引入像素强度约束并结合梯度稀疏正则化，显著提升了此类图像的恢复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模型的盲图像去卷积方法主要针对自然图像，依赖对图像和模糊核的先验知识。但对于条码、文本、图案等图像类别，像素取值非常有限（如二值），这种重要先验常被忽视，导致恢复性能不足。

Method: 提出一种新的像素强度约束以利用上述先验信息，构建了一个统一的盲二值图像和图案图像去卷积框架，该框架同时融合了像素强度约束与梯度稀疏正则化项。

Result: 数值实验表明，该方法在视觉质量和定量指标上均优于许多现有盲图像去卷积技术。

Conclusion: 通过引入像素强度约束，可有效利用特殊图像类别的有限像素取值先验，提升盲去卷积性能，为二值和图案图像恢复提供了有效的统一解决方案。

Abstract: Blind image deconvolution (BID) is a prominent research topic in the field of imaging sciences, given its significant practical applications. Most existing model-based BID methods focus on natural images, incorporating appropriate prior knowledge about both the underlying image and the blur kernel. However, for certain classes of images, such as barcodes, text, and patterns, pixels can only take very limited values, a specific prior that is often overlooked in the literature. In this article, we introduce a novel pixel intensity constraint to leverage this important information, improving recovery performance for these specialized image classes. Specifically, we propose a unified framework for blind binary and pattern image deconvolution that incorporates both the pixel intensity constraint and a gradient sparsity regularizer. Numerical experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms many existing BID techniques, achieving superior results in terms of both visual quality and quantitative metrics.

</details>


### [72] [RegVGGT: Sustainable Visual Geometry Grounding for Streaming via Regulated Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23286)
*Hongbo Mao,Junjun Jiang,Youyu Chen,Jiaxin Zhang,Zhemeng Dong,Xianming Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 RegVGGT，一种无需训练的词元调控方法，通过每帧仅保留约1%的词元更新上下文记忆，解决长视频流3D重建中GPU显存受限与推理上下文完整性之间的矛盾，在消费级GPU上可处理数千帧并达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频流输入的3D重建对前馈重建模型（FFRM）而言存在两难：有限GPU显存无法保留整段流的推理上下文。现有方法在上下文完整性与显存占用之间做权衡，要么显存快速膨胀，要么因人为限制显存而损害上下文完整性。

Method: 基于关键观察——词元的初始显著性可可靠预测其在整段流中的长期重要性——提出 RegVGGT，一种免训练的词元调控方法，对进入帧的词元进行激进调控：每帧最多接纳1%的词元用于更新上下文记忆，从而显著抑制随流推进的显存膨胀。同时配备与 FlashAttention 兼容的词元显著性估计方案。

Result: 可在消费级GPU上处理数千帧，重建质量损失可忽略；在多种FFRM预测任务的长时序基准上取得SOTA性能，大幅超越以往基于FFRM的流式重建基线。

Conclusion: 通过利用词元初始显著性预测长期重要性并激进调控词元，RegVGGT 在不训练的前提下有效缓解显存膨胀，兼顾长时序重建的上下文完整性与效率，为流式3D重建提供了高效可行的方案。

Abstract: 3D reconstruction from a lengthy video stream input poses a dilemma for feed-forward reconstruction models (FFRMs), that a whole-stream inference context cannot be retained under limited GPU memory.Recent studies seek to resolve this problem via a trade-off between the integrity of inference context and GPU memory usage, which either suffer from a rapid memory inflation or degraded context integrity due to artificially capping memory usage.Driven by our key observation that the initial saliency of a token reliably dictates its long-term importance across the stream, we propose RegVGGT, a training-free token regulation method which aggressively regulates the tokens of incoming frames.By admitting at most 1% of tokens per frame to update the context memory, our method dramatically suppresses memory inflation as the stream progresses.Equipped with a FlashAttention-compatible token saliency estimation scheme, RegVGGT is capable of processing thousands of frames on a consumer-grade GPU with negligible compromise to reconstruction quality.Extensive experiments demonstrate that RegVGGT achieves state-of-the-art performance on long-horizon benchmarks across diverse FFRM prediction tasks, surpassing prior FFRM-based stream reconstruction baselines by a large margin.

</details>


### [73] [MinCU: A Fine-Grained Benchmark for Grounded Minimal-Change Understanding in Image Pairs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23336)
*Chaoqian Mu,Wenhao Wu,Zichen Liang,Jiaxu Li,Lijun Wang,Yifan Wang,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 MinCU 基准测试与 SG-ISA 方法，用于多模态大模型的"有依据的最小变化理解"任务；SG-ISA 通过 Think-Locate-Describe 的隐式空间锚点机制，在提升定位与描述联合表现的同时减少约26%推理 token。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多孤立地评估语义比较或单图定位，缺乏对"忠实描述+物理定位"的联合要求。近同图像间的细粒度差异定位与描述是 MLLM 的关键但被忽视的能力，需要新基准与新方法填补空白。

Method: 1) 构建 MinCU 基准：每样本为一对仅含单一原子变化（类别、属性、数量或空间位置）的图像，评估描述变化、定位变化区域、识别变化实体三类能力。2) 提出 SG-ISA（语义引导隐式空间锚点）结构化自回归方法，将预测分解为 Think-Locate-Describe 序列：先预测变化概念的语义线索，再用离散空间锚点作为隐式定位脚手架，最后同时生成变化描述与定位框。

Result: 即便是最强的闭源 MLLM 及近期 R1 式推理模型在 MinCU 上也表现不佳，多数无法同时产出准确描述与定位框。相比此前的思维链方法，SG-ISA 微调在定位准确率与描述质量上取得显著联合提升，并将推理 token 开销降低约26%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，对于双图有依据理解任务，引入隐式中间空间接口比单纯依赖模型规模更有效，为 MLLM 的细粒度差异理解提供了新范式。

Abstract: Localizing and describing fine-grained differences between near-identical images is a critical yet underexplored capability for multimodal large language models (MLLMs). Existing benchmarks largely assess semantic comparison or single-image grounding in isolation, without jointly requiring faithful description and physical localization. To bridge this gap, we introduce MinCU, a benchmark for grounded minimal-change understanding, where each sample consists of an image pair differing by a single atomic variation in object category, attribute, count, or spatial position, and models are evaluated on their ability to describe the change, localize the changed regions, and identify the changed entity. We further propose Semantic-Guided Implicit Spatial Anchors (SG-ISA), a structured autoregressive method that decomposes prediction into a Think-Locate-Describe sequence. SG-ISA first predicts a semantic cue for the changed concept, then uses discrete spatial anchors as an implicit localization scaffold, and finally generates the change description together with the grounding box. Experiments reveal that even the strongest closed-source MLLMs and recent R1-style reasoning models struggle on MinCU, with most failing to jointly produce accurate descriptions and grounding boxes. Compared to the previous chain-of-thought method, fine-tuning with SG-ISA yields substantial joint improvements in grounding accuracy and description quality while reducing reasoning-token overhead by approximately 26%. These results suggest that an implicit intermediate spatial interface can be more effective than relying solely on model scale for grounded dual-image understanding.

</details>


### [74] [AniPrO: Interpretable Anime Image Provenance Detection via Multi-Dimensional Semantic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23345)
*Yan Liu,Baoxiang Huang,Zi'an Wang,Wenbo Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AniPrO框架，用于可解释的动漫图像来源鉴别（区分手绘、AI修复和文生图），并构建了包含五维结构化描述的数据集及两个评测基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI在动漫风格图像创作中的普及，区分手绘、AI修复和文生图图像对版权归属、视觉溯源和内容治理至关重要。现有AI生成图像检测器主要针对真实世界照片，忽视了动漫特有的扁平上色、夸张结构和艺术线条控制等线索。

Method: 提出AniPrO多维度描述增强框架，基于AnimeDL-2M构建，从35,000张候选池中筛选15,000张均衡样本，涵盖真实、修复、文生图三类，配有结构化五维描述；并引入AniPrO-SFD-Bench和AniPrO-MFR-Bench两个基准，分别从统计特征判别和多模态融合推理角度评估来源检测。

Result: 实验表明，结构化语义引导能揭示系统性的AI生成偏差（如全局视觉合理性与局部细节一致性之间的差距），并提升对具有挑战性的修复样本的检测效果。

Conclusion: AniPrO为动漫图像的来源鉴别提供了可解释的解决方案，数据集和代码将在指定GitHub地址发布。

Abstract: As generative AI becomes increasingly used in anime-style image creation, distinguishing human-drawn, AI-inpainted, and text-to-image images is important for copyright attribution, visual provenance, and content governance. Existing AI-generated image detectors mainly target real-world photographs and often overlook anime-specific cues such as flat coloring, exaggerated structures, and artistic line control. To address this gap, we propose AniPrO, a multi-dimensional description-enhanced framework for interpretable anime image provenance. Built upon AnimeDL-2M, AniPrO contains 15,000 balanced samples from a 35,000-image candidate pool, covering Real, Inpainting, and Text2Image categories with structured five-dimensional descriptions. We further introduce AniPrO-SFD-Bench and AniPrO-MFR-Bench to evaluate provenance detection from statistical feature discrimination and multimodal fusion reasoning perspectives. Experiments show that structured semantic guidance reveals systematic AI-generation biases, such as the gap between global visual plausibility and local detail coherence, and improves the detection of challenging inpainting samples. The dataset and code will be released at: https://github.com/YAN-LIU05/AniPrO.

</details>


### [75] [BiView-Touch: Learning Bimanual Tactile Representations by Cross-Hand Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23352)
*Chenxin Liang,Youchen Lai,Chuqiao Lyu,Tianxing Chen,Shoujie Li,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BiView-Touch 是一个仅基于触觉的自监督框架，通过用同步的对侧手信息补全被掩蔽的目标手潜表示，学习跨手依赖关系，在低标签场景显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双手交互产生同一物理过程的互补触觉视角，但现有触觉表示学习多独立建模双手或仅在下游预测中组合，忽视了跨手关系这一未被利用的结构。

Method: 提出 BiView-Touch：学生编码器配合几何条件的方向性解码器，从可见的目标手区域和同步的对侧全手预测完整视图 EMA 潜目标；引入时间与布局反事实增强对同步性和解剖组织源信息的敏感性。

Result: 消融与源上下文干预表明模型确实学到结构化跨手依赖；在 HumanTouch 数据集上冻结表示在低标签设置持续超越代表性自监督基线；仅用 5% 标签时，双侧手腕运动识别平衡准确率相对提升 7.1%，力推导交互阶段识别提升 14.1%；并发布 20 任务的 BVT-20 数据集，验证跨会话、跨预训练语料迁移及留出双手任务迁移。

Conclusion: 利用对侧手的同步且解剖组织化的触觉上下文可有效学习跨手依赖表示，为双侧触觉自监督学习及低标签迁移提供新范式。

Abstract: Bimanual interaction produces complementary tactile views of the same physical process, yet existing tactile representation learning largely models the two hands independently or combines them only for downstream prediction, leaving their cross-hand relationship unexplored. To exploit this overlooked structure, we introduce BiView-Touch, a tactile-only framework that completes masked target-hand latents from the remaining visible target-hand regions and the synchronized full contralateral hand. A student encoder with a geometry-conditioned directional decoder predicts full-view EMA latent targets, while temporal and layout counterfactuals encourage sensitivity to synchronized and anatomically organized source information. Controlled ablations and source-context interventions show that BiView-Touch learns structured cross-hand dependence on temporally aligned and anatomically organized contralateral tactile context, rather than benefiting from bilateral input alone. On the public HumanTouch dataset, its frozen representations consistently outperform representative self-supervised baselines across low-label settings. With only 5\% downstream labels, BiView-Touch achieves relative balanced-accuracy gains of 7.1\% on bilateral wrist-motion recognition and 14.1\% on force-derived interaction-phase recognition. We further introduce BVT-20, a 20-task bilateral tactile dataset, and demonstrate transfer across recording sessions and pretraining corpora, including transfer to a held-out bimanual task. Our code and dataset details are available on the anonymous project page: https://anonymous.4open.science/w/biview-touch-review-site-050C/.

</details>


### [76] [The Right Future for Action: Learning Action-Relevant Predictive States in World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23369)
*Qiwen Gu,Jifan Li,Bingjie Gao,Rui Chen,Jing Tang,Xiangxiang Chu,Junqiao Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Action-Relevant Predictive States (ARPS)，一种在视频专家与动作专家之间的紧凑预测接口，通过未来表示监督训练，在推理时仅用当前观测生成预测状态，显著提升分布偏移下的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无生成式的世界动作模型（WAMs）在训练时保留未来视频预测，但推理时依赖内部视频特征进行动作控制，这些特征应保留何种信息并不明确。作者通过表示诊断发现：未来变化更可预测的表示并不一定更利于线性动作解码；观测到的未来变化能提供超越当前时刻的动作信息，且可线性读取的动作信息在空间上呈集中分布。这些发现促使作者设计一个更契合控制需求的特征接口。

Method: ARPS 作为视频专家与动作专家之间的紧凑预测接口：使用"时间跨度条件状态预测器"将中间视频特征聚合为紧凑状态，为动作专家提供全部视觉上下文；通过"未来表示监督"训练该状态的不同部分去预测不同未来时刻的视觉表示及其相对当前的变化。推理阶段移除监督分支，动作专家仅使用由当前观测计算出的预测状态。

Result: 受控消融实验表明，未来监督能大幅提升分布偏移下的泛化能力。ARPS 在 LIBERO 上达到 99.2% 成功率，并可无自适应迁移到 LIBERO-Plus，取得 87.3%，比 Fast-WAM 高出 39.2 个百分点。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了未来可预测性与动作可解码性之间的非等价关系，证明面向动作的、紧凑且带未来表示监督的预测状态接口能有效提升世界动作模型的控制性能与跨分布泛化能力。

Abstract: Generation-free world action models (WAMs) retain future-video prediction during training but act from internal video features at inference, leaving unclear what these features should preserve for control. Our representation diagnostics show that representations with more predictable future changes need not make linear action decoding easier. Observed future changes provide additional action information beyond the present, and linearly readable action information is spatially concentrated. These findings motivate Action-Relevant Predictive States (ARPS), a compact predictive interface between the video and action experts. ARPS uses a horizon-conditioned state predictor to aggregate intermediate video features into a compact state that supplies all visual context to the action expert. Future-representation supervision trains different parts of this state to predict visual representations at different future times, together with their changes relative to the present. At inference, the supervision branch is removed, and the action expert only uses the learned predictive state computed from current observations. Controlled ablations show that future supervision substantially improves generalization under distribution shift. ARPS achieves 99.2% success on LIBERO and transfers to LIBERO-Plus without adaptation, reaching 87.3% and exceeding Fast-WAM by 39.2 percentage points.

</details>


### [77] [Vision-Wireless Fusion for Multi-User Localization: A Cross-Modal Transformer Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23372)
*Can Zheng,Jiguang He,Guofa Cai,Henk Wymeersch,Merouane Debbah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种视觉-无线融合的多用户定位框架，利用导频索引的CSI（信道状态信息）令牌结合跨注意力机制从视觉空间记忆中检索用户特定信息，实现多用户精确定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂城市环境中，无线测量受噪声、遮挡和多径影响容易产生歧义，而视觉观测可提供互补的空间上下文，因此需要融合视觉与无线信息实现准确的多用户定位。

Method: 采用正交导频索引保留UE身份于CSI令牌序列中；将每个导频索引的CSI观测编码为查询令牌，用跨注意力从空间视觉记忆中检索用户特定信息；通过CSI令牌间的自注意力捕获用户间交互；所得多模态表示用于逐用户定位。

Result: 在不同数据集上相比基于模型、仅CSI及多模态融合的基线方法均取得一致提升；并在不同无线与视觉条件下进行了进一步实验评估。

Conclusion: 视觉-无线融合框架能有效提升复杂环境下的多用户定位性能，导频索引与跨注意力设计可有效保持用户身份并融合互补信息。

Abstract: Accurate multi-user localization is challenging in complex urban environments, where wireless measurements can become ambiguous under noise, blockage, and multipath, while visual observations provide complementary spatial context. This paper presents a vision-wireless fusion framework for multi-user localization using pilot-indexed channel state information (CSI). Orthogonal pilot indices preserve the identities of the communicating UEs in the CSI token sequence and localization outputs. The model encodes each pilot-indexed CSI observation as a query token and uses cross-attention to retrieve user-specific information from spatial visual memory. Self-attention among CSI tokens further captures inter-user interactions, while the resulting multimodal representations are used for user-wise localization. Experiments on different datasets show consistent improvements over model-based, CSI-only, and multimodal-fusion baselines. Further experiments evaluate the model under different wireless and visual conditions.

</details>


### [78] [LiteTex-GS: Fast and Lightweight Texturing for Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23380)
*Zhiwei Li,Yijia Guo,Yishi Lu,Liwen Hu,Hong Rao,Shengbo Chen,Lei Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LiteTex-GS，一种快速轻量的高斯泼溅纹理框架，通过紧凑初始化、渐进式纹理分配、贡献-面积感知剪枝和分辨率感知更新规则，在保持渲染质量的同时显著减少参数和训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯泼溅中几何与外观的紧耦合表示需要大量基元来再现高频纹理细节，导致内存和优化成本高；近期纹理化2D高斯方法虽缓解此问题，但离散高斯与连续2D网格之间的结构差异需要复杂参数化，带来严重计算开销，破坏了高斯泼溅原有的效率，使得细节纹理与计算敏捷性之间的平衡成为未解决的挑战。

Method: LiteTex-GS初始化极紧凑的表示，为每个高斯分配最小的局部纹理，仅对重建误差显著的基元逐步分配更高分辨率；引入贡献-面积感知剪枝策略消除低效用高斯以保持精简的几何骨架；设计分辨率感知更新规则以缓解纹理上采样引起的梯度稀释，保持快速稳定收敛。

Result: 在标准新视角合成基准上的大量实验表明，该方法实现了与现有纹理化高斯基线相当或更优的渲染质量，同时使用的参数更少、训练时间更短。

Conclusion: LiteTex-GS有效解决了纹理化高斯泼溅中细节纹理与计算效率之间的平衡问题，在保证渲染质量的前提下显著降低了参数规模和训练成本，为快速轻量的高斯泼溅纹理化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Gaussian Splatting has enabled real-time novel view synthesis, but its tightly coupled geometry and appearance representation often require a large number of primitives to reproduce high-frequency texture details, leading to substantial memory and optimization costs. Recent textured 2D Gaussian methods alleviate this limitation by attaching texture maps to Gaussian primitives. However, bridging the fundamental structural gap between discrete Gaussians and continuous 2D grids requires complex parameterizations that introduce severe computational overhead. This overhead fundamentally compromises the original efficiency of Gaussian Splatting, making the balance between detailed texturing and computational agility an unresolved challenge. To address these challenges, we propose LiteTex-GS, a fast and lightweight texturing framework for Gaussian Splatting. Our method initializes an extremely compact representation, assigning minimal local texture to each Gaussian and progressively allocates higher resolution only to primitives with significant reconstruction errors. To maintain a streamlined geometric scaffold, we introduce a contribution- and area-aware pruning strategy that eliminates low-utility Gaussians. Furthermore, to mitigate the gradient dilution caused by texture upsampling, we design a resolution-aware update rule that preserves rapid and stable convergence. Extensive experiments on standard novel view synthesis benchmarks demonstrate that our method achieves competitive or superior rendering quality while using substantially fewer parameters and less training time than existing textured Gaussian baselines.

</details>


### [79] [ProxyBuild: Text-Guided Structured 3D Building Generation with Mesh-Anchored Procedural Proxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23386)
*Xiang Tang,Ruotong Li,Xiaopeng Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ProxyBuild——一种面向结构化建筑生成的混合框架。核心创新是"网格锚定程序化代理"(MAPP)中间表示,将生成任务解耦为代理预测与资产实例化两阶段。第一阶段训练面-边二部图编码器从网格推断面与边的语义角色;第二阶段由 LLM 解析文本风格与属性参数,结合空间放置逻辑与硬约束完成高精度资产检索与组装。实验表明该方法可缓解过平滑、部件碰撞与结构破损等问题,从文本稳健生成结构清晰、细节丰富且可后期编辑的 3D 建筑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本引导的 3D 建筑生成潜力巨大,但现有生成模型通常输出不可分离的单一网格或不可交互的渲染表示;程序化建模虽能生成可编辑的分层结构,但规则编写繁重,即便借助大语言模型也难以在几何约束下有效求解程序化规则。因此亟需一种兼具可编辑性与自动化的结构化生成方案。

Method: 1) 提出 MAPP(网格锚定程序化代理)作为中间表示,将建筑部件锚定到几何外壳上,把生成任务解耦为代理预测和代理到资产的实例化两阶段。2) 构建带 MAPP 标注的建筑数据集,训练所设计的面-边二部图编码器,在异构网格图上显式建模拓扑元素间的特征交互,推断面与边的语义角色。3) 在 LLM 解析的文本风格与属性参数条件下,融合空间放置逻辑与硬约束,实现高精度资产检索与组装。

Result: 大量实验显示,ProxyBuild 显著缓解了建筑生成中常见的过平滑、部件碰撞与结构破损等问题,并能准确解析来自多种来源的无语义外壳;在多项指标上优于既有基线方法。

Conclusion: 该方法能够从文本稳健生成结构清晰、细节丰富且可后期编辑的 3D 建筑,为虚拟现实、数字孪生等下游应用提供了可靠且可交互的内容基础。

Abstract: Text-guided 3D building generation holds tremendous application potential, yet existing generative models typically output inseparable single meshes or non-interactive rendered representations. While procedural modeling can generate editable buildings with hierarchical structures, rule authoring is laborious, and even with the aid of large language models (LLMs), it remains challenging to effectively solve procedural rules under geometric constraints. In this paper, we propose ProxyBuild, a novel hybrid framework for structured building generation. We introduce the Mesh-Anchored Procedural Proxy (MAPP) as a novel intermediate representation, which tightly anchors building components onto geometric shells, thereby decoupling the generation task into two phases: proxy prediction and proxy-to-asset instantiation. First, we construct a building dataset with MAPP annotations to train our designed face-edge bigraph encoder. By explicitly modeling the feature interactions of topological elements on heterogeneous mesh graphs, this encoder accurately infers the semantic roles of faces and edges. Subsequently, conditioned on textual styles and attribute parameters parsed by LLMs, we accomplish high-precision asset retrieval and assembly by integrating a spatial placement logic with hard constraints. Extensive experiments show that ProxyBuild not only significantly mitigates common issues in building generation such as over-smoothing, component collisions, and structural corruptions, but also accurately parses semantic-free shells from diverse sources. Outperforming prior baselines across various metrics, our method can robustly generate structurally clear, detail-rich, and post-editable 3D buildings from text, thereby providing a reliable and interactive content foundation for downstream applications such as virtual reality and digital twins.

</details>


### [80] [Enhancing Shrimp Disease Detection via Deep Learning and Data Refinement for Resilient Aquaculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23397)
*Vinh Canh-Thanh Truong,Hai-Binh Pham,Ngoc Hong Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次将视觉Transformer（ViT）与自监督学习（SSL）应用于虾类养殖疾病检测，提出两条深度学习流程区分健康、黑鳃、白斑综合征及共感染四类状态。监督迁移学习达到96%准确率，基于SimCLR的自监督方法在少标签条件下达到85%验证准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虾类疾病给水产养殖业造成严重损失，亟需鲁棒、自动化的检测手段。同时，该领域面临模型性能瓶颈以及数据标注困难等问题，需要引入更先进的深度学习方法来应对。

Method: 构建包含4,348张图像的数据集，涵盖健康、黑鳃（BG）、白斑综合征病毒（WSSV）及两者共感染四类。方法一：使用ImageNet预训练的ViT-Small/16与EfficientNet骨干网络进行监督迁移学习。方法二：采用SimCLR对比学习框架，以ViT-Small为编码器，先从无标签图像中学习鲁棒表征，再进行微调。

Result: 监督迁移学习方法取得96%的准确率，收敛速度快，优于传统通用模型；标签高效的自监督学习方法达到具有竞争力的85%验证准确率。

Conclusion: 本研究为可持续水产养殖监测建立了新的强基线，证明了ViT与自监督学习在虾类疾病检测领域的有效性与应用潜力，兼顾了高性能与标签效率。

Abstract: Shrimp diseases continue to cause devastating losses in the aquaculture industry, driving a critical need for robust, automated detection. This work contributes the first application of Vision Transformers (ViT) and Self-Supervised Learning (SSL) to the shrimp farming domain, addressing both performance bottlenecks and data labeling challenges. We propose two deep learning pipelines to classify four key diseases: Healthy, Black Gill (BG), White Spot Syndrome Virus (WSSV), and a co-infection of both using a dataset of 4,348 images. First, our supervised transfer-learning approach leverages ImageNet-pretrained ViT-Small/16 and EfficientNet backbones. Second, we introduce a contrastive learning framework (SimCLR) with a ViT-Small encoder to extract robust representations from unlabeled images prior to fine-tuning. Our results establish strong new baselines for sustainable aquaculture monitoring. The supervised approach achieves an outstanding 96% accuracy with fast convergence, outperforming traditional generic models, while the label-efficient SSL approach reaches a highly competitive 85% validation accuracy.

</details>


### [81] [ScaleBlind: Point Cloud Completion under Unknown Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23404)
*Shenghui Wu,Chen Wang,Yuan Feng,Guangshun Wei,Yuanfeng Zhou,Changjian Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ScaleBlind框架，利用基础图像生成模型的先验，从部分点云中恢复全局尺度并完成3D补全，无需访问GT尺度信息，成为点云补全任务的新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有点云补全方法在训练和测试时的归一化中隐含依赖真值形状尺度（GT-scale），这是现实推理中不可得的信息；一旦移除该先知线索，会导致严重的补全伪影（如过度/不足补全、嵌套壳层），限制了实际部署。作者观察到基础图像生成模型具备强大的物体几何理解与多视图一致渲染能力，可作为无需GT尺度的3D补全先验。

Method: ScaleBlind框架：从渲染的部分视图"想象"出完整的多视图外观；将推断出的缺失区域提升（lift）回3D得到几何感知的粗略补全；再通过强大的跨模态融合网络与原始部分点云进行精细优化，直接恢复全局尺度。

Result: 大量实验证明该框架的优越性，使ScaleBlind成为点云补全任务的新最优方法。

Conclusion: 通过利用2D基础模型先验，方法在推理时无需访问GT尺度信息，并在2D生成先验与3D点云补全之间建立了有原则的桥梁。

Abstract: Point cloud completion aims to infer a complete 3D shape from a partial point cloud and serves as a fundamental building block for downstream tasks such as reconstruction, editing, and simulation. Despite the recent progress, existing learning-based methods often implicitly rely on access to the ground-truth shape scale (GT-scale) during both training- and testing-time normalization, assuming privileged information that is unavailable in real-world inference. This hidden assumption limits practical deployment and can lead to severe completion artifacts, e.g., over- or under-completion and nested shells, once the oracle GT-scale cue is removed. We observe that the recent foundation image generation models exhibit a strong capability of understanding objects and geometries, and producing multi-view consistent renderings, making them promising priors for GT-scale-free 3D completion. Motivated by this insight, we propose ScaleBlind, a novel framework that leverages foundation-model-based image completion to recover global scale directly from partial inputs and then faithfully produces the 3D completion. Specifically, ScaleBlind dreams out complete multi-view appearances from rendered partial views, lifts the inferred missing regions back into 3D to obtain a geometry-aware coarse completion, and further refines it via a powerful cross-modal fusion network with the original partial point cloud. By harnessing 2D foundation priors, our method eliminates the need for accessing GT-scale information at inference. Moreover, it provides a principled bridge between 2D generative priors and 3D point cloud completion. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of our framework, making ScaleBlind the new state-of-the-art for the point cloud completion task.

</details>


### [82] [Accurate Motion Estimation with Bézier Control Point for Efficient Frame Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23408)
*Shuhao Han,Chenyang Wu,Chun-Le Guo,Zheng-Peng Duan,Zhen Li,Ming-Ming Cheng,Chongyi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ABC-Inter帧插值方法，通过精确光流估计模块与Bézier控制点消除运动模糊歧义，并支持非均匀运动建模，取得多个基准的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练集中运动歧义导致生成的中间帧模糊，且推理时假设帧间匀速运动进一步造成中间帧不准确。

Method: 设计精确光流估计模块（AFM），通过解耦两帧特征并映射到对应坐标来更好估计光流；引入由输入帧与中间真值帧计算的Bézier控制点，消除训练集运动歧义；借助更准确的光流，引入额外帧直接用多光流计算Bézier控制点以建模非均匀运动而无需重训练；并提出新的Bézier控制点估计模块，通过第二阶段微调实现仅用两帧估计非线性运动。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上达到SOTA性能，并具有出色的视觉感知效果。

Conclusion: ABC-Inter通过精确光流估计与Bézier控制点有效解决模糊与运动歧义问题，并可扩展至非均匀/非线性运动建模，性能优于现有方法。

Abstract: In frame interpolation tasks, motion ambiguity in the training set causes models to generate blurry intermediate frames. Moreover, the assumption of uniform motion between frames during inference further leads to inaccuracies in the generated intermediate frames. To tackle these challenges, we propose an Accurate motion estimation algorithm with Bézier Control point, ABC-Inter, for efficient frame Interpolation. Specifically, ABC-Inter designs an Accurate Flow estimation Module (AFM) by decoupling two-frame features and mapping to corresponding coordinates to better estimate the optical flow between the two frames. Furthermore, ABC-Inter eliminates motion ambiguity in the training set by introducing Bézier control points that are computed using the input frames and the intermediate ground-truth (gt) frames. This allows the model to estimate accurate optical flow between two frames during the training process, thereby solving the blurriness problem in the generated intermediate frames during inference. Benefiting from the more accurate flow estimation between two frames, we can introduce additional frames and directly use multiple flows to calculate Bézier control points for modeling non-uniform motion without retraining the model. Simultaneously, to realize the estimation of non-linear motion using only two frames, we also introduce a new Bézier control point estimation module which achieves better motion estimation between the two frames by performing fine-tuning on the model in the second stage. Experimental results demonstrate that our ABC-Inter achieves state-of-the-art performance on multiple benchmark datasets and exhibits excellent visual perception.

</details>


### [83] [Retrieval Geometry Shapes Cache-Based Clip Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23409)
*Mahir Shahriar Tamim,Md. Samiul Alim,Azmine Toushik Wasi,Shahriyar Zaman Ridoy,Meharun Nesa,Mohammad Abu Yousuf,Alex Lamb,Mohammad Ali Moni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 缓存式测试时适应中，检索空间本身是一阶设计选择。作者固定内存只改检索编码器，发现相同内存的增益差异巨大（ImageNet-A 上从 +0.44 到 +19.7），据此提出免训练系统 MARC：用冻结 CLIP 预测、DINOv2-B 检索，单视图缓存即超过 64 视图集成，性能更高且成本与时间更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于缓存的测试时适应方法主要通过存储与检索目标流样本来改进 CLIP 预测，但大多把图像-图像检索所用的特征空间视为固定不变，未探究检索空间本身对适应效果的影响。作者希望回答：适应效果在多大程度上取决于检索空间，而非仅仅取决于存储了哪些样本。

Method: 1) 对照实验：固定记忆库，仅更换检索编码器，在 16 个检索空间中评估同一记忆带来的增益，并测试无标签的检索空间选择能否接近 oracle 增益。2) 提出 MARC（Memory Augmented Retrieval for CLIP）：免训练系统，预测使用冻结 CLIP，检索使用 DINOv2-B，仅设一个融合权重。

Result: ImageNet-A 上缓存增益随检索空间变化极大：CLIP 与 MAE 最多 +0.44 分，DINOv2-L 达 +19.7±0.4；无标签检索空间选择在 ImageNet-V2 上保留 98% 的 oracle 增益。MARC 单视图缓存修复 1074±21 个基线错误，优于 64 视图集成的 878±4，成本约为其七分之一。四个 ImageNet 分布偏移上 OOD 平均 67.91%；在 DINOv2-B 规模、8 视图下达到 64.17±0.31%，优于图式缓存系统的 62.75±0.15%，且快 2.6 倍。

Conclusion: 记忆质量不仅取决于存储哪些样本，也取决于如何检索。检索空间应被视为缓存式稳健适应的首要设计选择，对遥感、科学成像及变化视觉环境中的鲁棒适应具有指导意义。

Abstract: Cache-based test-time adaptation improves CLIP predictions by storing and retrieving examples from the target stream while keeping the model frozen. However, existing methods largely treat the feature space used for image-image retrieval as fixed, leaving open how much adaptation depends on the retrieval space itself. We study this question by fixing the memory and changing only the retrieval encoder, finding that the same memory can yield very different gains: across sixteen retrieval spaces, ImageNet-A cache gain ranges from at most +0.44 points for CLIP and MAE to +19.7 +/- 0.4 for DINOv2-L, while label-free retrieval-space selection retains 98% of oracle gain on ImageNet-V2. These results show that memory quality depends not only on which examples are stored, but also on how they are retrieved. Motivated by this finding, we propose MARC (Memory Augmented Retrieval for CLIP), a training-free system that uses frozen CLIP for prediction and DINOv2-B for retrieval with a single fusion weight. A single-view cache repairs 1074 +/- 21 baseline errors, compared with 878 +/- 4 for a 64-view ensemble, at roughly one seventh of the cost. Across four ImageNet distribution shifts, MARC reaches a 67.91% OOD average and, at matched DINOv2-B scale and eight views, achieves 64.17 +/- 0.31% versus 62.75 +/- 0.15% for a graph-based cache system while running 2.6 times faster. Overall, our results establish retrieval space as a first-order design choice for robust cache-based adaptation in remote sensing, scientific imaging, and changing visual environments.

</details>


### [84] [Semi-automated reconstruction of indoor geometry from 360-degree video for CFD-based airflow analysis in classrooms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23425)
*Dhruv Gamdha,James Afful,Shambhavi Joshi,Ulrike Passe,Adarsh Krishnamurthy,Baskar Ganapathysubramanian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一套半自动化工作流，将单段360度视频转化为可单独编辑、可直接用于CFD仿真的房间几何资产，并在教室与阶梯报告厅中验证其通风与污染物输运模拟可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑通风与污染物输运的CFD评估虽广泛使用，但规模化部署受三大瓶颈限制：获取房间几何需昂贵扫描硬件或手工CAD建模；场景难以分解为可单独操作的对象；改变布局需重新采集房间。作者希望以低成本方式获取几何，使逐房间的几何分辨比较型通风研究变得可行。

Method: 用NeRF从单段360度视频重建稠密点云；用文本提示的SAM 3分割得到2D实例掩码，并通过多视角一致性与深度带过滤提升到3D；用八叉树将点分离为对象实例，并用连通图修补遮挡缺口；通过ICP配准拟合椅子模板，表格几何则程序化生成；提供基于浏览器的编辑器做质量保证与快速构建替代布局；最后用稳态RANS的OpenFOAM解驱动瞬态被动标量输运，并以网格敏感性研究验证、以IEA Annex 20基准校验。

Result: 应用于两间大学教室与一间阶梯报告厅：单房间从采集到几何的耗时在消费级工作站上为2至5小时。在一间教室的受控障碍序列中，模拟得到的半清除时间随家具增加呈非单调变化；跨房间比较表明清除行为无法在不同房间间可靠外推，说明需要逐房间采集几何。

Conclusion: 该工作流将几何获取成本降到很低，使几何分辨的比较型通风研究在教室等空间变得实用可行。

Abstract: Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) is widely used to evaluate ventilation and contaminant transport in occupied buildings, but deployment at scale is limited by three bottlenecks: acquiring room geometry without costly scanning hardware or manual CAD modeling, decomposing the scene into individually manipulable objects, and reconfiguring those objects for alternative layouts without re-capturing the room. We present a semi-automated workflow that converts a single 360-degree video of a room into individually editable, simulation-ready geometry assets. A dense point cloud is reconstructed using Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF), and 2D instance masks from text-prompted SAM 3 segmentation are lifted to 3D using multi-view consensus and depth-band filtering. Points are separated into object instances with an octree, and occlusion gaps are healed with a connectivity graph. Chair templates are fitted by Iterative Closest Point (ICP) alignment, and table geometry is generated procedurally. A browser-based editor supports quality assurance and rapid construction of alternative layout configurations. A steady Reynolds-averaged OpenFOAM solution then drives transient passive-scalar transport; the setup is verified using a mesh-sensitivity study and validated against an IEA Annex 20 benchmark. We apply the workflow to two university classrooms and a tiered lecture-hall auditorium. The capture-to-geometry pass takes two to five hours per room on a consumer workstation. In a controlled obstruction sequence in one classroom, the modeled half-clearance time varies non-monotonically as furniture is added, and a cross-room comparison indicates that clearance behavior cannot be reliably extrapolated between rooms, motivating per-room geometry acquisition. By making that acquisition low-cost, the workflow makes geometry-resolved comparative ventilation studies practical for spaces such as classrooms.

</details>


### [85] [RSPDBench: Benchmarking Vision Foundation Models on Earth Observation Tasks Under Physically Grounded Remote-Sensing Product Degradations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23427)
*Tanjim Bin Faruk,Khondaker Masfiq Reza,Shrideep Pallickara,Sangmi Lee Pallickara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RSPDBench，一个面向遥感产品级退化的视觉基础模型鲁棒性基准，通过物理可解释的原始退化与复合退化链评估5个数据集、7个基础模型及2个监督基线，揭示退化敏感性具有强结构性，且复合退化可带来超出最强单组分最多38个百分点的额外性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地球观测视觉基础模型多在下游干净基准上评估，而实际遥感产品在进入模型前已存在空间、辐射、对齐、噪声与协调等缺陷。既有鲁棒性评估常使用通用图像损坏或宽泛域偏移，无法隔离这些产品级失效模式，因此需要物理依据明确的产品级退化基准。

Method: 提出RSPDBench：面向遥感产品退化的物理基础基准。覆盖5个EO数据集、7个基础模型与2个监督基线；在经审计的原始退化及复合产品退化链下测试。每个模型采用其干净数据上选定的原生协议评估，鲁棒性以相对自身干净基线的性能下降衡量。

Result: 退化敏感性具有强结构性：分辨率条件编码器与通道分组编码器保护不同的失效轴；同一物理缺陷可能损害一个模型却有利于另一个。复合退化链暴露出孤立退化无法预测的失效，表现为模型相关的放大、饱和或组分主导，最大额外下降达38个百分点。

Conclusion: EO鲁棒性不能仅靠干净准确率或通用扰动测试来刻画，还必须针对遥感产品在部署中携带的结构化缺陷进行测量。

Abstract: Vision foundation models targeting Earth observation (EO) tasks are commonly evaluated on clean downstream benchmarks, but operational EO products can already contain spatial, radiometric, alignment, noise, and harmonization defects before reaching the model. Existing robustness evaluations often use generic image corruptions or broad domain shifts, which do not isolate these product-level failure modes. We introduce \textbf{RSPDBench}, a physically grounded \textbf{r}emote-\textbf{s}ensing-\textbf{p}roduct \textbf{d}egradation \textbf{b}enchmark for vision foundation models. RSPDBench evaluates five EO datasets, seven foundation-model entries, and two supervised baselines under audited primitive degradations and compound product chains. Each model is evaluated under its clean-selected native protocol, with robustness measured as the drop from its own clean baseline. Our analysis reveals that degradation sensitivity is strongly structured: resolution-conditioned and channel-grouped encoders protect different failure axes, and the same physical defect can hurt one model while helping another. Compound chains expose failures that isolated degradations do not predict, with model-dependent amplification, saturation, or component dominance, and excess drops up to $38$ percentage points beyond the strongest component. These results show that EO robustness cannot be characterized by clean accuracy or generic perturbation tests alone; it must also be measured against the structured defects that remote-sensing products carry into deployment.

</details>


### [86] [HOIBlender: Blending Lightweight Detection with Vision-Language Priors for Efficient Human-Object Interaction Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23431)
*Junwen Chen,Keiji Yanai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HOIBlender 是一个轻量高效的 HOI 检测器，通过融合检测器视觉 token、空间主客体推理与 BLIP-2 语义先验，在单一路解码流程中实现长尾 HOI 检测，并在多个尺度上稳定超越对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 HOI 检测方法虽借助更强检测器和视觉语言先验提升了精度，但多数仍在检测器之上叠加沉重的 transformer 编码器、复杂去噪调度或事后语义校准，导致结构冗余、效率低，且长尾监督问题依然突出。

Method: 基于 RF-DETR/LW-DETR 风格框架与 DINOv2 骨干，直接从多尺度投影器选取 top-K 图像条件 token 作为主客体候选，省去专用编码器阶段；采用双阶段解码器，先稳定人物-物体几何关系，再通过渐进式 BLIP-2 先验融合完成动词与 HOI 分类，并用 BLIP-2 文本嵌入初始化分类器权重以应对长尾类别；同时引入分组查询训练增强优化而不增加推理成本。

Result: 在 Nano、Small、2XL 三种模型尺度上，HOIBlender 在 HICO-DET 上均持续优于 SOV-STG-VLA 与 Hybrid-SOV-VLA，仅用 9 个训练 epoch 即达到 44.49 Default Full mAP，同时保持有竞争力的延迟与参数预算。

Conclusion: 轻量检测、结构化空间-语义解码与深度集成的视觉语言先验可以融合为单一高效的 HOI 流程，验证了无需堆叠重型模块也能在长尾监督下取得优异 HOI 检测性能。

Abstract: Human-object interaction (HOI) detection requires grounding an interacting human-object pair and recognizing the verb that links them, often under severe long-tail supervision. Recent methods improve accuracy with stronger detectors and vision-language priors, but many still stack heavy transformer encoders, intricate denoising schedules, or post-hoc semantic calibration on top of the detector. We present \textbf{HOIBlender}, an efficient HOI detector named after its core design principle: blending detector-grounded visual tokens, spatial subject-object reasoning, and BLIP-2 semantic priors inside one lightweight decoding pipeline. HOIBlender builds on an RF-DETR/LW-DETR-style foundation with a DINOv2 backbone and selects top-$K$ image-conditioned tokens directly from the multi-scale projector as subject and object candidates, removing the dedicated encoder stage retained by prior HOI methods. A dual-stage decoder first stabilizes human-object geometry and then performs verb and HOI classification through progressive BLIP-2 prior fusion, with classifier weights initialized from BLIP-2 text embeddings for long-tail categories. Grouped-query training further enriches optimization without increasing inference cost. Across three model scales (Nano, Small, 2XL), HOIBlender consistently outperforms SOV-STG-VLA and Hybrid-SOV-VLA on HICO-DET, reaching $44.49$ Default Full mAP in only $9$ training epochs while maintaining competitive latency and parameter budgets. These results show that lightweight detection, structured spatial-semantic decoding, and deeply integrated vision-language priors can be blended into a single efficient HOI pipeline.

</details>


### [87] [GAPS: Generative Active Pseudo-view Selection for Sparse-View 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23436)
*Hongfei Zhu,Haochen Deng,Sitao Zhang,Ling Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种交替优化框架，用预训练扩散模型为稀疏视角下的3DGS生成几何一致的伪视角监督，并引入GAPS主动选择、准入过滤与密度自适应正则，显著提升新视角合成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏视角下的新视角合成严重欠约束。3DGS虽支持实时渲染，但在少视角训练时会产生漂浮物、几何破碎和背景发白等问题，需要额外监督信号来弥补约束不足。

Method: 采用交替优化框架：用预训练图像扩散模型生成几何一致的伪视角来监督3DGS。生成受深度条件ControlNet、IP-Adapter风格迁移、LoRA场景适配与img2img结构锚定约束。提出GAPS（生成式主动伪视角选择），其退火策略从早期保守插值过渡到后期探索性外推以覆盖未观测区域；并用双准则准入门与不确定性加权损失剔除不可靠生成，用密度自适应DropGaussian缓解复杂场景过拟合。

Result: 在LLFF的3/6/9视角下，平均PSNR较原版3DGS提升0.40/0.89/0.70 dB；在Mip-NeRF 360的12/24视角下提升1.18/0.80 dB。所有设置下SSIM提升、LPIPS下降。消融表明主动选择与密度自适应正则均不可缺，且在无界360度场景中只有完整方法能把LPIPS降至无伪视角基线以下。

Conclusion: 生成式伪视角监督结合主动选择与自适应正则能有效缓解稀疏视角3DGS的欠约束问题，在多个数据集与视角数设置下一致优于基线，且各组件均为必要。

Abstract: Novel view synthesis from sparse observations is severely under-constrained. Although 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables real-time rendering, it produces floaters, broken geometry, and washed-out backgrounds when trained with few views. We propose an alternating optimization framework that uses a pre-trained image diffusion model to generate geometrically consistent pseudo-views for additional 3DGS supervision. Generation is constrained by depth-conditioned ControlNet, IP-Adapter style transfer, LoRA scene adaptation, and img2img structural anchoring. We introduce Generative Active Pseudo-view Selection (GAPS) to balance reconstruction informativeness and generative reliability when choosing target views. Its annealing schedule shifts from conservative interpolation early in training to exploratory extrapolation later, gradually covering unobserved regions. A dual-criterion admission gate and uncertainty-weighted losses reject unreliable generations, while density-adaptive DropGaussian reduces overfitting in complex scenes. On LLFF with 3/6/9 views, our method improves average PSNR over vanilla 3DGS by 0.40/0.89/0.70 dB. On Mip-NeRF 360 with 12/24 views, the gains are 1.18/0.80 dB. SSIM improves and LPIPS decreases in every setting. Ablations show that active selection and density-adaptive regularization are both necessary; only the full method reduces LPIPS below the no-pseudo-view baseline on unbounded 360-degree scenes.

</details>


### [88] [PhysReflect: Geometry and Perception Guided Diffusion for Physically-Plausible Mirror Reflections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23442)
*Shuheng Ge,Hongwei Ren,Li Zhang,Xiangqian Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 PhysReflect，一种几何与感知引导的扩散框架，通过在训练中将预测的干净隐变量解码到像素空间并施加退火监督（几何损失+感知损失），解决扩散模型生成镜像反射时的几何畸变与外观不一致问题，在合成与真实基准上超越既有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽能生成高质量图像，却常违反镜像反射的物理规律，出现位置偏移、方向错位、比例失衡、结构扭曲等问题，即便最先进的生成系统也难以避免。现有方法依赖合成数据扩展或辅助深度条件，且仅在隐空间使用噪声重建损失作为隐式监督，无法直接施加反射特有的几何与感知约束。

Method: 提出 PhysReflect 框架：在每个训练步将预测的干净隐变量解码到像素空间，施加退火监督，包含两个可微目标。（1）几何损失：通过稀疏对极对应与稠密边界投影对齐，强制镜像诱导的空间一致性，其中基于 SAM2 的 TwinTrack 机制为边界感知监督提供稳定的镜内定位。（2）感知损失：由语义一致性损失（用 DINOv2 特征保持反射身份与外观）与光照一致性损失（在单目几何先验下正则化深度、表面法线与光照一致性）组成。

Result: 在合成与真实世界基准上的实验表明，PhysReflect 在几何、感知与物理合理性指标上均优于先前的镜像反射方法，定性视觉结果也更佳。

Conclusion: 通过在像素空间直接施加几何与感知的可微退火监督，PhysReflect 有效缓解了扩散模型生成镜像反射时的几何畸变与外观不一致，为物理合理的反射生成提供了新思路。

Abstract: Diffusion models generate high-quality images, yet often violate the physical laws governing mirror reflections. Reflections often suffer from geometric aberrations, including positional offsets, directional misalignment, proportional imbalance, and structural distortion. These failures remain evident even in contemporary state-of-the-art generative systems. Existing methods itigate this problem through synthetic data scaling or auxiliary depth conditioning, yet their merely reliance on latent-space noise reconstruction losses as implicit supervision prevents direct enforcement of reflection-specific geometric and perceptual constraints. To bridge this gap, we present PhysReflect, a geometry and perception guided diffusion framework that decodes the predicted clean latent into pixel space at each training step and applies annealed supervision through two complementary differentiable objectives. The Geometric Loss enforces mirror-induced spatial consistency through sparse epipolar correspondence and dense boundary projection alignment, where a SAM2-based TwinTrack mechanism provides stable in-mirror localization for boundary-aware supervision. The Perceptual Loss preserves reflected appearance by combining Semantic Consistency Loss, which maintains reflected identity and appearance via DINOv2 features, and Lighting Consistency Loss, which regularizes depth, surface-normal, and illumination coherence under monocular geometry priors. Experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks show that PhysReflect outperforms prior mirror-reflection methods in geometric, perceptual, and physical-plausibility metrics, as well as qualitative visual results.

</details>


### [89] [Rule-Constrained Assignment for Cue-Ball Identification in Broadcast Snooker](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23450)
*Yuxin Cao,Wei Song,Yuezhong Wu,Jin Song Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文将台球母球识别建模为规则约束的分配问题，而非逐候选独立判断，提升了广播斯诺克中母球识别准确率与状态恢复覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广播斯诺克度量分析依赖准确的母球识别。现有系统对每个候选独立与固定白色模板比较并做阈值剔除，在广播条件下易受光照变化（彩球显白）及球员、设备遮挡干扰，导致识别不准、覆盖不足。

Method: 把母球识别形式化为规则约束的分配问题，联合将检测到的候选分配到有界库存中：一个母球、最多15颗红球、六颗已知位置的彩球。通过把每个候选分配到白色槽位的增量代价来选择母球，传统外观检验成为单槽特例。

Result: 在419个手工标注击球上，身份准确率从88.1%提升至95.5%；在留出场地从80.5%提升至95.2%。在度量状态恢复系统CueLift中，分配使6241个可计分击球的覆盖率从36.2%提升至55.4%。在2529个击球的独立评估中为每个击球分配估计值，仍保持该优势。

Conclusion: 将母球识别作为联合分配问题处理，能有效应对光照与遮挡干扰，显著提升识别准确率和度量系统覆盖率，并在跨场地与不同评估设置下保持稳健。

Abstract: Accurate cue-ball identification is essential for metric analysis of broadcast snooker. Existing systems evaluate each candidate independently against a fixed white prototype and reject candidates above an appearance threshold. Under broadcast conditions, illumination changes can make colored balls appear white, while intrusions from players and equipment can obscure the cue ball or introduce competing candidates. We formulate cue-ball identification as a rule-constrained assignment problem that jointly assigns detected candidates to the bounded snooker inventory: one cue ball, up to 15 reds, and six colors with known spots. The cue ball is selected by the incremental cost of assigning each candidate to the white slot, and the conventional appearance test follows as the one-slot case. On 419 hand-annotated shots, our method improves identity accuracy from 88.1% to 95.5%, and from 80.5% to 95.2% on held-out venues. Within CueLift, our metric state-recovery system, the assignment expands coverage from 36.2% to 55.4% over 6,241 scorable shots. Assigning an estimate to every shot in a separate evaluation on 2,529 shots preserves this advantage.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

### [90] [Recognition, Simulation, and Refusal: A Contamination-Aware Study of Classic Psychological Effects in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22090)
*Joy Bose*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 PsyAgentBench 基准，通过因子设计（命名/盲测 × 教科书/反事实任务 × 人设操纵）重跑经典心理学实验，以区分 LLM 产生类人效应与真正具备该偏差，并报告五种范式下的复制画像而非标量偏差分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将「LLM 输出类人反应模式」等同于「LLM 拥有该心理偏差」，这一推断存在根本缺陷。同时，用单一标量偏差易感性分数概括 LLM 的心理学行为会掩盖其内部结构的异质性，需要一种能分离机制的设计来重新审视经典范式的可移植性。

Method: 提出 PsyAgentBench 基准，采用因子设计重跑经典心理学实验：每个范式在提示中显式标注（named）或以常规任务框定（blind），并采用教科书原始版本（canonical）或与训练数据词汇/情境重叠更少的结构匹配变体（counterfactual），再与人设操纵交叉。共释放 41,904 次试验，覆盖五个完整范式、最多三个开放权重模型家族。

Result: 类人效应通过性质不同的路径出现：(1) 范式标签门控并可被显式覆盖（Asch 从众：盲测 0% 到命名 83.3%，gpt-oss-120B）；(2) 依赖知识的信号依赖（锚定：有据事实上恰好为零，凭空数量上接近完全，也符合对唯一可用信号的理性使用）；(3) 标注下对新颖内容的放大（框架效应）；(4) 稳健缺失（沉没成本）；(5) 安全中介筛选，拒绝本身即主要发现（最小群体分配）。单句人设改变（宜人性，作为指令而非经验证的特质操纵）会消除、削弱或反转这些效应，依效应而异，反对任何单一反应偏差解释。另形式化并实证记录三种心理学范式向 LLM 智能体迁移失败的方式：人设支配、群体崩溃与安全筛选。

Conclusion: 标量偏差易感性分数会掩盖上述结构，应改为报告复制画像（replication profiles）。

Abstract: An LLM producing the response pattern associated with a human psychological effect is not the same claim as the LLM possessing that bias. We present PsyAgentBench, a benchmark that re-runs classic psychology experiments on LLM agents under a factorial design built to separate these: each paradigm is run with the paradigm explicitly labeled in the prompt (named) or framed as a routine task (blind), and on the literal textbook version of the task (canonical) or a structurally matched variant written to reduce lexical and scenario overlap with likely training data (counterfactual), crossed with a persona manipulation. Across five completed paradigms, evaluated on up to three open-weight model families with 41,904 trials released, apparently human-like effects arise through qualitatively different routes rather than one susceptibility: paradigm-label gating with explicit override (Asch conformity, 0 percent blind to 83.3 percent named on gpt-oss-120B), knowledge-dependent signal reliance (anchoring, exactly zero on grounded facts versus near total on invented quantities, a pattern equally consistent with rational use of the only available signal), amplification on novel content under labeling (framing), robust absence (sunk cost), and safety-mediated selection where refusal itself is the primary finding (minimal-group allocation). A one-sentence persona change (agreeableness, framed as an instruction rather than a verified trait manipulation) eliminates, dampens, or reverses these effects depending on which effect it is, arguing against any single response-bias account. We further formalize, and in two cases document empirically, three ways a psychology paradigm can fail to port to LLM agents: persona dominance, population collapse, and safety selection. We argue scalar bias-susceptibility scores obscure this structure and report replication profiles instead.

</details>


### [91] [Memory That Looks Forward: A Zero-Inference Prospective Term for Personal Memory Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22091)
*Jonathan Groff*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种零查询开销的"前瞻性"记忆检索机制：用显式承诺账本为相关记忆条目加权，显著提升对用户已承诺事项的召回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个人记忆检索是回溯式的，只返回与查询相似的内容，对用户"已承诺要做的事"视而不见，因此无法支持前瞻性记忆需求。

Method: 承诺以带日期或触发条件的条目存入显式账本；被触发条目所链接的记忆项获得显著性加成，并以乘法方式融入基于嵌入的检索，保证相关性仍占主导，且查询时无需额外推理。

Result: 在仿照 TriggerBench 结构构建的合成任务集上（48 段盲写对话、175 个任务），该机制将困难子集的 recall@5 从 0.000 提升至默认权重下的 0.955，floor 变体下达 1.000，且在 53 个已解除承诺任务上零误加成；盲写还发现仅 17-29% 自然表述的承诺-触发对会突破嵌入相似度。

Conclusion: 预计算承诺链接可作为分层设计的常开底层，查询时前瞻作为其扩展层；该机制只在少数真实案例中起作用，且在其余情况下不产生损害。结果属初步，评测集为作者自建，待 TriggerBench 数据发布后将开展正式评测。

Abstract: Retrieval over a personal memory store is retrospective: it surfaces what resembles the query, and it is blind to what the user has committed to do. We describe a prospective term for memory retrieval that costs no inference at query time. Commitments are held in an explicit ledger as dated or trigger-conditioned entries; memory items linked to a firing entry receive a salience boost, blended multiplicatively into embedding-based retrieval so that relevance remains sovereign. On a synthetic prospective-memory task set modeled on TriggerBench's published structure (48 blind-authored dialogues, 175 tasks), the term raised recall@5 on the hard stratum from 0.000 to 0.955 at the default blend weight and to 1.000 under a floor variant, with zero false boosts across 53 resolved-commitment tasks. Blind authorship also produced a scope finding: only 17-29% of naturally phrased commitment-trigger pairs defeat embedding similarity, so the term matters on a real minority of cases and must do no harm on the rest, which it does not. We position precomputed commitment linkage as the always-on floor of a layered design whose expansion layer is query-time prospection. Results are preliminary: the evaluation set is author-constructed, and evaluation on TriggerBench proper is committed follow-up work once its data is released.

</details>


### [92] [Beyond the Text: Verifying That Agent-Written Papers Are Backed by Their Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22111)
*Qiuhong Shen,Benlong Wu,Hanjin Liu,Yuang Qi,Kejiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 ReAgent，一个自动审计框架，用于评估 LLM agent 生成的研究文档与其关联代码库之间的一致性，通过静态与动态审计结合来发现隐藏的不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent 能自主开展研究并生成研究文档及配套代码，但现有评审实践主要评估文本质量，无法可靠识别诸如硬编码指标、方法未实现、实验结果缺乏支持等文档与实现不一致的问题。这一问题此前基本未被探索。

Method: ReAgent 从研究文档中构建科学声明的结构化表示，用以指导代码库分析与证据收集。静态审计检查所声称的方法、实现和实验配置是否在代码库中一致体现；动态审计则执行相关实验并收集执行证据以评估经验发现。两者结合识别单一视角下难以发现的不一致（如复现了报告数值却偏离声称方法）。收集的证据与审计决策被组织成结构化的代码库级审计报告，实现透明的证据可追溯。

Result: 在人工构建的 agent 生成研究文档—代码库配对基准上评估 ReAgent，并与代表性静态基线和基于复现的基线进行比较，实验结果表明 ReAgent 能有效识别报告的研究发现与支撑代码库证据之间的不一致。

Conclusion: ReAgent 通过静态分析与动态证据结合，能有效审计 agent 生成研究文档与代码库之间的一致性，弥补现有仅关注文本质量的评审实践的不足，并支持透明可追溯的审计。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly capable of conducting research autonomously, producing research documents alongside the code and experiments that ostensibly support them. Yet whether the reported findings are consistently supported by corresponding implementations and execution evidence remains largely unexplored: existing review practices primarily assess textual quality and cannot reliably identify inconsistencies such as hard-coded metrics, unimplemented methods, or unsupported experimental results. We present ReAgent, an automated auditing framework for assessing the consistency between agent-generated research documents and their associated repositories. ReAgent constructs structured representations of scientific claims from research documents and uses them to guide repository analysis and evidence collection. Static auditing examines whether claimed methodologies, implementations, and experimental configurations are consistently reflected in the repository, while dynamic auditing executes relevant experiments and collects execution evidence to assess empirical findings. By combining static analysis with dynamic evidence, ReAgent identifies inconsistencies that may remain hidden under either perspective alone, such as experiments that reproduce reported numbers while deviating from the claimed methodology. The collected evidence and audit decisions are organized into a structured repository-level audit report, enabling transparent evidence traceability. We evaluate ReAgent on a manually curated benchmark of agent-generated research document--repository pairs and compare it against representative static and reproduction-based baselines. Experimental results demonstrate that ReAgent effectively identifies inconsistencies between reported research findings and their supporting repository evidence.

</details>


### [93] [Summarize, Judge, Refine: Decoupled Content Understanding and Policy Learning for Multimodal Content Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22094)
*Zeeshan Ahmed,Yang Qin,Hanqing Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出双模型架构 SJR，通过自然语言接口解耦多模态理解与政策分类，用 GRPO 迭代协同训练与文本空间数据增强实现少样本政策冷启动，并在误导广告检测上显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统内容审核系统将多模态理解与特定政策分类耦合，政策变更需全流程重训；同时多模态数据难以有效增强，导致违规样本标签稀缺。作者希望解耦这两类关注点，使新政策能在缺乏真实违规数据的情况下快速上线。

Method: SJR 由多模态 Content Model 和纯文本 Policy Model 组成，前者生成结构化文本摘要，后者依据政策定义对摘要分类。通过 GRPO 的迭代协同训练让 Content Model 生成政策相关摘要；利用文本空间的对抗式摘要变体增强（多模态原始数据无法做到的增强路径），支持少样本政策引导。所有决策基于可读摘要，天然具备可解释性。

Result: 在误导广告检测任务上，SJR 相比零样本思维链基线在非误导 F1 上取得 +23.6% 相对提升，优于端到端 SFT、STaR/RFT 与 RLFT。一个仅用合成正类数据、零真实违规样本训练的变体，在违规 F1 上与全量数据模型相差仅 0.2% 相对值。

Conclusion: 将多模态理解与政策分类解耦、并以文本摘要为接口，可同时获得性能、可解释性与数据效率；新政策无需真实违规数据即可启动上线，验证了该架构在内容审核场景下的实用价值。

Abstract: Content moderation systems traditionally entangle multimodal understanding with policy-specific classification, requiring full pipeline retraining for every policy change and suffering from label scarcity since multimedia cannot be meaningfully augmented. We propose Summarize-Judge-Refine (SJR), a two-model architecture that decouples these concerns via a natural language interface: a multimodal Content Model produces structured text summaries, and a text-only Policy Model classifies them against policy definitions. An iterative co-training loop refines the Content Model via GRPO to produce policy-relevant summaries, while text-space augmentation generates adversarial summary variants---an augmentation pathway impossible on raw multimedia---enabling few-shot policy bootstrap. Every decision is grounded in a human-readable summary, providing interpretability as a structural byproduct. On misleading advertisement detection, SJR achieves +23.6\% relative non-misleading F1 over a zero-shot chain-of-thought baseline, outperforming end-to-end SFT, STaR/RFT, and RLFT. Notably, a variant trained on zero real violating examples---with all positive-class data synthetically generated---matches the full-data model within 0.2\% relative on violating F1, demonstrating that new policies can launch without any real violation data.

</details>


### [94] [A Channel-Boosted Multi-Agent System with Iterative Consultation for Document Sensitivity Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22212)
*Aleesha Zainab,Asifullah Khan,Muhammad Ahmed Khalid,Faheem Ullah Khan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 CB-MAS 及其实例 IC-MAS 多智能体系统，在不增加长上下文计算成本的前提下，通过通道增强与迭代协商提升长文档敏感度评估性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键国家基础设施机构需在路由或存储前评估异构文档的敏感性，人工评估慢、不一致且难扩展。此前的 BERT 单编码器基线存在固定输入长度截断问题，会丢弃超出窗口的证据，而敏感文档往往最长。

Method: 提出 Channel-Boosted MAS（CB-MAS），并实例化为 IC-MAS（Iterative Consultation Multi-Agent System）。其中 Channel Critic Agent 学习文档自适应信任权重，控制两个首窗口编码器之间的门控通道增强；成对的 Consultation Agents 迭代交换信念状态，协调长文档首尾证据。通过固定窗口在紧凑表示空间中协调，使计算量与文档长度无关。

Result: 消融显示，评论者控制的通道增强贡献了大部分准确率增益，协商机制在不导致精度崩溃的情况下恢复了召回率。Critic-Controlled Gated Channel Boosting 结合 Max-Pool 融合和 Blackboard Adaptive Consultation 达到 90.72% 准确率、91.23% F1、92.01% 敏感召回率、90.46% 敏感精确率，计算量比固定轮次基线减少约 54%。相较单编码器基线提升显著（McNemar 检验 p<0.000001；配对 t 检验）。

Conclusion: IC-MAS 有效缓解了截断造成的信息丢失问题，在保持计算效率的同时显著优于单编码器基线，并提供了 LIME/SHAP 可解释性、多智能体评估及对局限性的诚实说明。

Abstract: Organizations in critical national infrastructure sectors must assess heterogeneous documents for sensitivity before routing or storage. Manual assessment is slow, inconsistent, and unscalable. Extending our prior leakage-controlled benchmark, BERT established the top single-encoder baseline (89.14% accuracy, 89.33% F1-score under 5-fold cross-validation on the Strategic 16K corpus). However, transformer baselines suffer from a structural limitation: fixed input length truncation discards evidence beyond the retained window-precisely where sensitive cables tend to be longest. We present Channel-Boosted MAS (CB-MAS) and instantiate it as IC-MAS (Iterative Consultation Multi-Agent System) to solve this without long-context computational costs. A Channel Critic Agent learns document-adaptive trust weights governing Gated Channel Boosting between two first-window encoders, while paired Consultation Agents iteratively exchange belief states to reconcile evidence from the beginning and end of long documents. IC-MAS holds computation constant regardless of document length by reconciling fixed windows in a compact representation space. Ablation studies show critic-controlled Channel Boosting provides the bulk of accuracy gains, while consultation recovers recall without precision collapse. Critic-Controlled Gated Channel Boosting with Max-Pool fusion and Blackboard Adaptive Consultation achieves 90.72% accuracy, 91.23% F1-score, 92.01% sensitive recall, and 90.46% sensitive precision, using about 54% less average computation than a fixed-round baseline. Gains over the single-encoder baseline are statistically significant (McNemar's test, p less than 0.000001; paired t-test). We include LIME/SHAP explainability, multi-agent evaluation, and an honest accounting of limitations.

</details>


### [95] [AI-inferred expressed well-being and collective-action discourse in climate-change campaigns on X](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22096)
*Wentao Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 对19个"发生年"的四大气候活动（地球日、地球一小时、全球气候行动日、世界环境日）共364,118条推文进行词法分析发现：活动期间"幸福"表达比例比活动前基线高9.02个百分点，但行动语言下降10.75个百分点，呈现"幸福—行动"背离；快乐来源帖获得转推级联的几率更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候活动通常以关注度和动员程度来评估，但伴随活动的"福祉语言"（幸福、希望等）研究不足。活动期是否会改变积极情绪与希望、快乐表达是否与行动语言相一致，尚无定论。

Method: 获取地球日、地球一小时、全球气候行动日、世界环境日4类活动、19个发生年的364,118条Twitter/X公开帖子，采用活动前30天、活动期、活动后窗口设计；使用带版本控制的加权词法模型估计幸福、未来导向希望、集体能力、痛苦与行动语言五类指标；并以配对年份对比、文本去重与构成检验、19簇wild bootstrap进行稳健性检验。

Result: 活动期幸福表达比例为9.02个百分点（高于活动前基线），而配对年份对比显示行动语言下降10.75个百分点，表明幸福与行动语言出现背离。幸福估计在构成调整与文本去重检验下仍为正，但在19簇wild bootstrap下精度下降。较幸福的来源帖被观察到配对转推级联的几率更低。

Conclusion: 气候活动期伴随积极情绪上升，但这种快乐并未同步转化为行动语言，甚至与更低的传播级联相关，提示以情绪高涨作为活动成效指标可能具有误导性；福祉语言应纳入气候传播评估框架。

Abstract: Climate campaigns are often evaluated through attention and mobilization, but less is known about the well-being language that accompanies them. Whether campaign periods alter positive affect and hope, and whether happiness aligns with action language, remains unresolved. We analysed 364,118 public Twitter/X posts from Earth Day, Earth Hour, Global Climate Action Day and World Environment Day in 19 occurrence-years, using 30-day pre-event, event and post-event windows. A versioned weighted lexical model estimated happiness, future-oriented hope, collective capability, distress and action language. Event-period happiness prevalence was 9.02 percentage points higher than the pre-event baseline , whereas paired occurrence contrasts showed a 10.75-point decline in action language, indicating a happiness--action divergence. The happiness estimate remained positive across composition and text-deduplication checks, but was less precise under a 19-cluster wild bootstrap. Happier source posts had lower odds of an observed matched retweet cascade.

</details>


### [96] [BizSage: A Self-Evolving Multi-Agent Framework for Business Research with Efficient Knowledge Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22235)
*Yuhe Wu,Guangyu Wang,Jiaxin Liu,Guang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BizSage 是一个面向经济学与商业研究的多智能体框架，通过小节级细粒度检索与质量驱动的自进化机制，提升研究自动化任务的检索覆盖与实证严谨性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的多智能体系统在学术研究流程自动化上已有进展，但扩展到经济学与商业研究时面临两大挑战：一是现有方法多以论文为检索粒度，而研究任务所需证据常分散于不同小节，造成粒度不匹配，损害检索的覆盖率与精确度；二是这些领域要求严格的实证严谨性，而现有系统缺乏从评估反馈中学习机制。此外，这些领域所需专业知识横跨相邻学科，难以结构化获取。

Method: 提出 BizSage 多智能体框架，结合语料库级细粒度检索与质量驱动的自进化。构建横向知识图谱（LKG），通过合并小节级知识图谱实现，并应用个性化 PageRank（PPR）来识别语义相关且结构重要的小节。七个专用智能体在 Meta-Review 自进化机制下协作，该机制从评估轨迹中提炼失败模式并转化为可复用策略。

Result: 在覆盖四个领域、三类任务的基准测试上，BizSage 在多数指标上排名第一，对六个基线方法的成对胜率均超过 60%，并实现了零幻觉引用。

Conclusion: BizSage 有望为经济学、商业及更广泛社会科学领域提供可靠的研究辅助，推动该类领域研究自动化的可靠发展。

Abstract: While multi-agent systems based on large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in automating the progressive workflow of academic research, extending them to economics and business research, where specialized domain knowledge spans neighboring disciplines yet remains difficult to access in a structured way, presents two challenges. First, existing methods mostly retrieve at the paper level, yet the evidence needed for research tasks is often distributed across different sections, creating a granularity mismatch that hinders retrieval coverage and precision. Second, these fields demand strict empirical rigor, yet current systems provide limited mechanisms for learning from evaluation feedback. We present \textbf{BizSage}, a multi-agent framework combining corpus-level fine-grained retrieval with quality-driven self-evolution. We build a Lateral Knowledge Graph (LKG) by merging section-level knowledge graphs and apply Personalized PageRank (PPR) to surface semantically relevant and structurally important sections. Seven specialized agents collaborate under a Meta-Review self-evolution mechanism that distills failure modes from evaluation traces into reusable strategies. On a benchmark spanning four domains and three tasks, BizSage ranks first on the majority of metrics, achieves pairwise win-rates above 60\% against six baselines, and produces zero hallucinated citations. We hope BizSage paves the way for reliable research assistance in economics, business, and the broader social sciences.

</details>


### [97] [Token Signatures of Code: Comparing Coding Behaviors Across Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22097)
*Junpeng Wang,Yuzhong Chen,Menghai Pan,Uday Singh Saini,Yiwei Cai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CLIC视觉分析方法，通过token频率分析刻画大语言模型的编码行为，定义鲁棒性和集中度两项新指标，并构建交互式可视化系统支持多尺度对比探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM编码任务评估主要关注pass@k等性能指标，随着模型进步，基线性能趋同使性能评估的区分度下降；而LLM在编码行为上的差异这一问题尚未被充分探索。

Method: CLIC将代码样本表示为token频率特征向量，训练可解释决策树以区分两个LLM的代码集合；提出鲁棒性（逐步移除最具区分度token后是否仍可区分）和集中度（差异由少数主导token驱动还是分散于多token）两项新指标；并开发交互式视觉分析系统，支持跨LLM对、任务和分词层级的对比导航与下钻。

Result: 对10个LLM在22个Kaggle机器学习任务上的案例研究，揭示了可用于LLM选择与提示工程的可操作洞见。

Conclusion: CLIC通过token频率与可解释模型实现了对LLM编码行为的可量化、可视化刻画，补充了传统性能评估，为LLM选择与提示设计提供行为层面的依据。

Abstract: The evaluation of large language models (LLMs) on coding tasks has primarily focused on performance metrics such as pass@k. As LLMs continue to advance, many models now meet baseline performance requirements, reducing the discriminative power of performance-based evaluation alone. Yet a key question remains largely unexplored: how do LLMs differ in their coding behavior? We propose CLIC (Code Learning for Identification and Comparison), a visual analytics approach that characterizes LLM coding behavior through token-frequency analysis. CLIC represents each code sample as a feature vector of token frequencies and trains an interpretable decision tree to separate two LLMs' code sets. Beyond classification accuracy, we define two new metrics: robustness, which measures whether the two LLMs remain distinguishable as their most-discriminative tokens are progressively removed, and concentration, which measures whether the difference is driven by a few dominant tokens or spread across many. Interpreting numerous pairwise comparisons (across LLM pairs, tasks, and tokenization levels) and tracing the full analytical chain form an inherently multi-scale, hypothesis-driven exploration task. We therefore develop an interactive visual analytics system to navigate the comparison landscape, identify pairs of interest, and drill down into discriminative tokens and their code contexts. Case studies comparing 10 LLMs across 22 Kaggle ML tasks reveal actionable insights for LLM selection and prompt engineering.

</details>


### [98] [TreeSpark: Calibrated, Load-Adaptive Draft Trees for Semi-Autoregressive Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22098)
*Huapeng Zhou,Huayu Wang,Xinyu Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TreeSpark提出一种基于父节点条件分布与路径存活率的自适应草稿树构建方法，在不损失精度的前提下提升推测解码的接受率与推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有块级草稿器草稿成本极低，但已有草稿树按"逐位置边际概率"排序候选，忽略了候选所扩展的父节点，导致在半自回归草稿器上更宽的树大多只是增加了排错序的节点；同时固定大小的树无法适应不同解码轮次及不同服务负载下可支持的推测量。

Method: TreeSpark从草稿器已有的Markov头以极低代价读取父节点条件分布，校准为边接受率估计；以"路径存活率"驱动最优优先扩展、每轮停止准则及负载自适应服务策略。兄弟节点无放回采样，并在递归拒绝采样中匹配残差，保证任意温度下解码无损。

Result: 自适应树在任意温度下均优于同等预算的固定树；在相同草稿器上对比调优的链式解码，每轮多接受15-25%草稿token，单请求墙钟时间快8-14%；在负载上升时可平滑将树收缩回链式。

Conclusion: 以父节点条件接受率与路径存活率为核心的自适应草稿树，可同时提升推测解码的接受效率、速度与服务弹性，且保持无损性。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates language-model inference by letting a cheap drafter propose tokens that the target model verifies in parallel. Recent block drafters make drafting nearly free: a single backbone pass emits an entire block of draft tokens. Draft trees promise a further gain -- several alternative continuations verified in one target forward -- but existing constructions rank candidates by per-position marginals that ignore which parent a candidate extends, so on semi-autoregressive drafters wider trees mostly add mis-ranked nodes; and a tree of fixed size ignores how much speculation each decoding round, and each serving load, can support. We introduce TreeSpark, which reads a parent-conditioned distribution from the drafter's existing Markov head at negligible cost, calibrates it into an edge-acceptance estimate, and lets path survival govern everything else: best-first expansion, per-round stopping, and a load-adaptive serving policy. Sampling siblings without replacement, with matching residuals in recursive rejection, keeps decoding lossless at any temperature. Adaptive trees improve on matched fixed budgets at every temperature; against a tuned chain on the same drafter, TreeSpark accepts 15-25% more draft tokens per round and decodes 8-14% faster in single-request wall-clock, and under rising load it gracefully shrinks the tree back to the chain. Code and artifacts: https://github.com/PopSoda2002/TreeSpark

</details>


### [99] [A framework for recipe data structure with applications for culinary and nutritional insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22099)
*Mansi Goel,Sumit Bhagat,Saloni Srivastava,Malav Patel,Shlok Vinodkumar Mehroliya,Ganesh Bagler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出RecipeDB2，一个将食谱转化为可计算结构化数据的框架，涵盖128,942个食谱、35,474种食材，整合营养、地理文化及饮食属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有食谱多以自由文本形式存在，人类可读但无法直接计算；且缺乏一个统一的可查询模式，将食谱的结构化食材组成、地理文化来源和营养信息关联起来。论文旨在填补这一表示空白。

Method: 将每个食谱分解为带类型的食材实体，并链接到参考营养数据库，同时标注地理文化和饮食背景。使用基于Transformer的命名实体模型将食材短语解析为七种烹饪属性；通过BERT嵌入策略将食材链接到USDA参考表（在200个高频食材的人工判定集上F1=87.90），每个映射食材得到148个营养参数；用随机森林分类器将34种食材类别传播到整个词表；再用确定性、保守的规则集为每个食谱分配饮食风格。

Result: 构建了RecipeDB2，包含来自32个地区、99个国家的128,942个食谱和35,474种食材，并提供可查询的在线平台。

Conclusion: RecipeDB2展示了一个使食谱可计算的规模化框架，将长期被视为艺术而非量化对象的烹饪遗产转化为可进行数据驱动分析的对象。

Abstract: Cooking is a complex process that transforms raw ingredients into delicious and nutritious dishes, yet the recipes that encode this process remain largely free text; readable by people but not directly computable. Existing recipe collections capture fragments of this information, but no shared representation links a recipe's structured ingredient composition, its geo-cultural provenance, and its nutritional profile within a single queryable schema. We address this representation gap by formalizing a framework for recipe data structure that decomposes each recipe into typed ingredient entities, grounds those entities in a reference nutritional database, and annotates them with geo-cultural and dietary context. We present RecipeDB2, a structured compilation of 128,942 recipes with 35,474 ingredients from 32 regions and 99 countries. Ingredient phrases are parsed into seven culinary attributes using a transformer-based named-entity model; ingredients are linked to the USDA reference tables through a BERT embedding strategy (F1 = 87.90 on a manually adjudicated set of the 200 most frequent ingredients), yielding 148 nutritional parameters per mapped ingredient; a Random Forest classifier propagates 34 ingredient categories across the full vocabulary; and a deterministic, conservative rule set assigns each recipe a dietary style. Through RecipeDB2 (https://cosylab.iiitd.edu.in/recipedb2/), we demonstrate a scalable framework for making recipes computable, turning culinary heritage (long treated as an artistic rather than a quantitative object) into a data-driven analysis.

</details>


### [100] [AdaMem: Adaptive Memory Token Allocation for Soft Compression in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22100)
*Artem Sakhno,Grigorii Davydenko,Omar Zoloev,Julia Belikova,Andrey Savchenko,Maksim Makarenko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AdaMem是一种相关性引导的软压缩框架，通过将段落相关性估计映射到查询相关的记忆令牌分配，在固定预算下提升RAG效率与质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软压缩方法对每个保留段落分配相同数量的记忆嵌入，忽略其与查询的相关性差异，导致在长文本、大检索池场景下成本高且引入干扰信息，记忆预算利用效率低。

Method: 提出AdaMem：用共享的查询条件压缩器一次性生成连续段落记忆与相关性分数；再通过确定性分配规则，在固定记忆令牌预算下为高分段落分配更多记忆令牌，并剔除低分段落。

Result: 在六个开放域QA基准上，AdaMem一致优于均匀分配的OSCAR等软压缩方法。16×压缩下子串匹配最多提升3.2点（5.5%），平均相对增益3.4%；64×压缩下平均相对增益达14.6%，PopQA最高提升9.8点（19.7%）。在比全上下文基线最高低4×推理延迟下达到未压缩答案质量，效率与均匀压缩基线相当。

Conclusion: 相关性引导的记忆分配在检索池大且记忆预算紧张时尤为有效，可在保持效率的同时显著提升压缩RAG的答案质量。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves language models with retrieved evidence, but processing many long passages is costly and can introduce distracting information. Soft compression addresses this challenge by encoding passages as compact sequences of continuous memory embeddings before generation. However, existing methods typically assign each retained passage an identical number of memory embeddings, irrespective of its query-specific relevance. To address this, we propose AdaMem, a relevance-guided soft-compression framework that maps learned passage-relevance estimates to a query-dependent allocation of a fixed memory-token budget. A shared query-conditioned compressor produces both continuous passage memories and relevance scores in a single pass; a deterministic allocation rule assigns more memory tokens to higher-scoring passages and can omit low-scoring ones. Across six open-domain QA benchmarks, AdaMem consistently outperforms OSCAR (the closely matched soft-compression baseline that uses uniform allocation) as well as other soft-compression methods at matched memory budgets. Under standard 16$\times$ compression, AdaMem improves sub-string match by up to 3.2 points (5.5%) over uniform allocation baseline, with an average relative gain of 3.4%; under aggressive 64$\times$ compression the average relative gain grows to 14.6%, with a maximum of 9.8 points (19.7%) on PopQA. AdaMem matches the answer quality of the uncompressed at up to 4$\times$ lower inference latency than full context baseline. AdaMem retains an efficiency profile comparable to the uniform-compression baseline, while achieving up to $4\times$ lower inference latency than full-context inference. Thus, relevance-guided memory allocation is particularly effective when retrieval pools are large and the available memory budget is tight.

</details>


### [101] [Context Poisoning as Extreme-Value Attention Interference in Long-Context Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22101)
*Meysam Ghaffari,Nina Fatehi,Bhaskar Sen,Nasim Sabetpour,Carlos Morato*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将长上下文下的性能退化形式化为"上下文中毒"，从注意力极值干扰角度推导了证据边距需随干扰项数量以Ω(√log N)增长的有限样本界，并用实验验证了检索精度随上下文增长而下降的规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽可处理越来越长的提示，但当无关或易混淆的上下文增加时，其定位并使用决定性证据的能力可能下降。作者希望从理论层面刻画这种"上下文中毒"现象，解释长上下文退化与分数混叠、位置混叠和softmax稀释之间的关系。

Method: 将上下文中毒建模为注意力中的极值干扰：决定性证据的分数存在上界，而有效干扰项的最大分数随其数量增长。在softmax检索抽象下，推导有限样本上界，得出在基准率之上维持固定精度目标需要证据边距以Ω(√log N)缩放（N为有效干扰项数量而非原始上下文长度）。同时开展受控实验，比较不同干扰构造在固定上下文长度下的影响，并测试检索门控的效果。

Result: 受控实验表明：在存在嵌入的硬负样本时，检索精度随总上下文增长而下降；在测试的干扰构造中，相同格式条件在固定上下文长度下造成最大的观测精度下降；检索门控可改善证据使用，但其净收益取决于能否保持证据召回率。

Conclusion: 研究由此提出若干方向：证据瓶颈、抗混叠表示、先检索后推理架构、验证器介导的记忆以及对比式抗中毒训练。

Abstract: Large language models can process increasingly long prompts, yet their ability to locate and use decisive evidence may degrade as irrelevant or confusable context is added. We formulate this phenomenon, which we call context poisoning, as extreme-value interference in attention: the decisive-evidence score is upper-bounded, while the maximum score among effective distractors grows with their number. Under a softmax retrieval abstraction, we derive a finite-sample upper bound showing that maintaining a fixed accuracy target above base rate requires the evidence margin to scale as $Ω(\sqrt{\log N})$, where N denotes the effective distractor count rather than necessarily the raw context length. The analysis connects long-context degradation to score aliasing, positional aliasing, and softmax dilution. Controlled experiments show that retrieval accuracy decreases as total context grows in the presence of embedded hard negatives, that the same-format condition produces the largest observed accuracy drop among the tested distractor constructions at fixed context length, and that retrieval gating can improve evidence use while its net benefit depends on preserving evidence recall. These results motivate evidence bottlenecks, alias-resistant representations, retrieve-then-reason architectures, verifier-mediated memory, and contrastive anti-poison training.

</details>


### [102] [DeepInstructor: An Agentic AI Instructor for Experience-Driven Idea Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22104)
*Rongcan Pei,Fang Guo,Qinglin Qi,Qi Zhu,Yun Luo,Jianhao Yan,Minjun Zhu,Qiujie Xie,Dehong Zheng,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 DeepInstructor，将科研想法评估建模为对结构化学术经验（58,607 条同行评审构成的 Experience Graph）的推理，并配套 DeepInstruct 数据集，显著提升与人类判断的对齐度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 已能大规模生成研究想法，瓶颈从"生成"转向"评估"。现有评估器依赖参数化知识或非结构化检索，缺乏类似人类导师那种基于经验的推理，判断不可溯源。

Method: 构建基于 58,607 条同行评审的 Experience Graph；使用 ReAct 式智能体按维度检索证据，实现可追溯评估；同时提出 DeepInstruct 数据集，在新颖性、重要性、可行性三个维度上提供受控的两两比较。

Result: DeepInstructor 大幅优于现有基线，与人类判断的 Hit@1 和 Hit@2 对齐度分别提升 24.4% 与 29.7%。

Conclusion: 科研想法评估可以建立在对外显化、结构化学术经验的推理之上，为自动化科学发现中的评估环节提供可溯源的新范式。

Abstract: As automated scientific discovery advances, Large Language Models (LLMs) can now generate research ideas at an unprecedented scale, shifting the bottleneck from idea generation to idea evaluation. Existing evaluators mainly rely on parametric LLM knowledge or unstructured retrieval, producing judgments that lack the experience-grounded reasoning used by human instructors. To address this, we propose DeepInstructor, an agentic framework that formulates idea evaluation as reasoning over structured scholarly experience. DeepInstructor constructs an Experience Graph from 58,607 peer reviews and employs a ReAct-based agent to retrieve dimension-specific evidence for traceable evaluation. We further introduce DeepInstruct, a dataset with controlled pairwise comparisons across novelty, significance, and feasibility. Experiments show that DeepInstructor substantially outperforms existing baselines, improving Hit@1 and Hit@2 alignment with human judgments by 24.4% and 29.7%, respectively. Our findings suggest that scientific idea evaluation can be grounded in explicit reasoning over structured scholarly experience

</details>


### [103] [Evaluating Fine-Tuned and Base Language Models in Maternal and Vaccination Healthcare for African Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22110)
*Abdulquddus Ajibade,Oluwaseun Odunsi,Iyinoluwa Animasaun,Chioma Nwakanma-Akanno,Oluwasegun Oguntuase,Oluwafunke Akinbuwa,Abiodun Adereni*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究比较了经LoRA微调的MamaBot-Llama与Vax-Llama和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct在尼日利亚母婴健康与疫苗接种问题上的表现，发现微调效果取决于数据质量：母婴健康模型显著提升，疫苗模型反而恶化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型可改善低资源地区的医疗信息传递，但可能生成不准确或文化不适切的建议，因此需评估领域微调在真实医疗场景中的有效性。

Method: 评估200个医疗问题（母婴健康与疫苗接种各100个，每领域含5个子领域）。MamaBot-Llama与Vax-Llama分别基于36,000+和9,000+问答对进行LoRA微调。两名尼日利亚执业医师采用5点Likert量表独立评分，并使用Wilcoxon符号秩检验进行配对比较。

Result: 结果因领域而异。MamaBot-Llama在所有标准上显著优于基础模型，总体提升4.9%（p<.001），临床可信度+7%、医学准确性+5%，严重问题减少50%，78%的病例中医师更偏好该模型。相反，Vax-Llama总体下降5.2%（p<.001），严重问题增加192%，安全担忧增加400%。

Conclusion: 当基于高质量、临床医师审核的数据时，领域特定微调可提升医疗LLM表现；但数据集质量不足时也可能导致性能下降。临床部署前必须进行严格的领域验证。医师评估者已获知情同意，聊天机器人日志已匿名化。

Abstract: Background: Large language models (LLMs) can improve healthcare information delivery in low-resource settings but may produce inaccurate or culturally inappropriate advice. This study evaluated domain-specific fine-tuning for maternal health and vaccination in Nigeria. Objective: To compare HelpMum's MamaBot-Llama and Vax-Llama with Meta's Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct for accuracy, safety, clarity, contextual appropriateness, and trustworthiness.
