First, we define a framework of five assurance properties (evidence grounding, non destructive claim reduction, computed severity, enforced authorization, and tamper evident accountability), each with a formal model and an explicit acceptance test, connected to prior work in capability based security, tamper evident logging, and software provenance. Second, we position representative systems (PentestGPT, the Cochise reference harness, MAPTA, and the trajectory judge PentestJudge) within the framework using published coding criteria, and identify a consistent assurance gap. Third, we study one open source implementation, NeuroSploit, pinned to an exact commit, reporting its architecture, its complexity cost, and a content addressed artifact bundle from a run against a public deliberately vulnerable target. We execute the deterministic authorization and audit acceptance tests directly and find and report a real enforcement gap, which we reflect by scoring both properties as partial. We therefore claim an initial existence argument that the properties are realizable together, not a comparative performance result, and we specify the multi target, ablation, and adversarial evaluation protocol required to turn the framework obligations into measurements.

</details>


### [204] [UBA-ORL: Unlearning-Activated Backdoor Attacks on Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22711)
*Fengyi Wang,Cong Li,Lulu Xue,Qiyu Leng,Ziqi Zhou,Peijin Guo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出UBA-ORL，首个针对离线强化学习的"遗忘激活型后门攻击"，借合规数据删除请求触发潜伏后门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线RL可从静态数据集学习策略，广泛用于自动驾驶、机器人控制及推荐等数据挖掘场景。合规驱动的数据删除虽增强隐私，却引入了此前未被认识到的攻击面。

Method: 采用双样本机制：注入后门轨迹（BD），将触发器与高奖励下的恶意动作关联；同时注入伪装轨迹（CM），共享相同触发模式但保持良性动作并配以同样高的奖励。训练中两者提供相互竞争的监督信号；当对CM子集发起合法删除（遗忘）请求后，残余的BD信号重新占据主导，从而按需重新激活后门。

Result: 在评估的离线RL配置下，攻击在正常训练后被显著抑制，而在合规删除请求后可实现可控激活；无触发器回报的变化因配置而异。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了合规驱动离线RL平台中被忽视的安全风险，呼吁社区建立遗忘前后联合审计机制以保障合规遗忘服务。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning (offline RL) enables policy learning from pre-collected static datasets without online exploration, and is increasingly deployed not only in safety-critical domains such as autonomous driving and robotic control but also in data-mining applications such as recommendation and behavior analysis. While compliance-driven data removal enhances privacy, it also opens a previously unrecognized attack surface. We introduce UBA-ORL (Unlearning-activated Backdoor Attack on Offline Reinforcement Learning), the first unlearning-activated backdoor attack for offline RL: in the evaluated settings, the attack is substantially suppressed after normal training and becomes pronounced after a compliance-driven deletion (unlearning) request. UBA-ORL employs a dual-sample mechanism: alongside backdoor trajectories (BD) that link a trigger to malicious actions under inflated rewards, the attacker injects camouflage trajectories (CM) sharing the same trigger pattern but preserving benign actions with equally high rewards. During training, BD and CM provide competing supervisory signals; upon a legitimate deletion request on the CM subset, the residual BD signal can re-dominate, reactivating the backdoor on demand. Empirical results show that UBA-ORL achieves controllable activation under the evaluated offline-RL configurations, while no-trigger return changes vary by configuration, exposing a previously overlooked security risk in compliance-driven offline RL platforms. We urge the community to develop joint pre-/post-unlearning auditing mechanisms for compliant unlearning services.

</details>


### [205] [MATE: Policy-Aware Security Auditing for Mobile Agents via Synthesis-Driven Trajectory Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22724)
*Changyue Jiang,Jiayi Wang,Xin Wen,Jiarun Dai,Geng Hong,Xudong Pan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MATE是一个轻量级、以策略为条件的审计器，能编码移动智能体轨迹与自然语言安全策略，判断轨迹是否违规并给出解释，在MATEBench上准确率超95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动智能体可自动化真实设备上的复杂多步工作流，但其轨迹可能违反各应用特定的安全策略。现有轨迹级防御依赖LLM提示或僵化规则，无法支持细粒度、自然语言、可跨应用与任务泛化的策略。

Method: 提出MATE，将策略视为可编辑文本而非固定模型参数，支持用户自定义与演化的需求而无需重训。构建知识库：从全球数百个流行移动应用中提取应用描述、工作流与策略，并用多阶段流水线合成超过140K条语义真实、以策略为条件的轨迹。同时发布MATEBench，含两个合成子集与一个真实手动采集轨迹子集。

Result: 经合成驱动轨迹学习训练的模型在MATEBench上准确率超95%，在外部安全基准上保持强性能，并在真实设备上审计智谱AutoGLM与阿里Mobile-Agent的轨迹，准确率超95%，比先前方法高出20%以上。

Conclusion: MATE表明，面向异构移动智能体的实用、细粒度安全审计既可行又有效。

Abstract: Mobile agents powered by foundation models now automate complex, multi-step workflows on real devices, but their trajectories can violate app-specific security policies. Existing trajectory-level defenses rely on LLM prompting or rigid rules, and thus fail to support fine-grained, natural-language policies that generalize across apps and tasks. In this work, we introduce MATE, a lightweight, policy-conditioned auditor that encodes both agent trajectories and natural-language security policies to determine whether a trajectory violates a given policy and to explain why. Treating policies as editable text rather than fixed model parameters allows MATE to handle user-defined and evolving requirements without retraining. To construct MATE, we build a knowledge base by extracting app descriptions, workflows, and policies from hundreds of popular mobile apps worldwide, and synthesizing over 140K semantically realistic, policy-conditioned trajectories with a multi-stage pipeline. We further release MATEBench, a trajectory-level auditing benchmark with two synthetic subsets and one real-world subset of manually collected trajectories. Models trained with our synthesis-driven trajectory learning achieve over 95% accuracy on MATEBench, retain strong performance on external safety benchmarks, and audit trajectories from Zhipu's AutoGLM and Alibaba's Mobile-Agent on real devices with over 95% accuracy, outperforming prior methods by over 20%. MATE shows that practical, fine-grained security auditing for heterogeneous mobile agents is both feasible and effective.

</details>


### [206] [SelfOp: An Optimization Algorithm for Self-Improving Security Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22792)
*Saad Ullah,Yigitcan Kaya,Christopher Kruegel,Giovanni Vigna,Gianluca Stringhini*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SelfOp 通过链式法则启发的文本梯度下降，自动优化冻结安全代理的任务上下文（指令、技能、参考文档），无需修改执行框架与模型权重，在 CyberGym 基准上实现显著自提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 代理在漏洞发现、利用复现、补丁生成等安全任务中广泛应用，但模型层面改进需要专家演示或可计算奖励，而安全任务往往难以提供（轨迹昂贵、失败难诊断、奖励稀疏且不可计算）。已有工作转向 harness 与上下文优化，但人工调优依赖任务专门知识且难扩展，自动化方法又依赖稀缺真值、更强的优化模型或无引导试错。

Method: SelfOp 将上下文优化建模为受链式法则启发的文本梯度下降：从单实例结果出发，将误差信号反向传播经过评估器、代理轨迹以及塑造其行为的上下文工件，得到逐实例文本梯度；通过聚类、排序、过滤跨实例累积梯度，仅在跨实例共识下提交更新；并用收敛检测器监控梯度信号，在上下文吸收完训练数据可泛化信息后停止，无需留出验证集。

Result: 在 CyberGym 真实漏洞复现任务基准上，用不到 200 个训练样本，SelfOp 使 GPT-5.4-mini（配合 Codex）自提升 17 分，超过前沿 GPT-5.4 基线 6 分；GPT-5.4 自身获得 18.5 分自提升。优化后的技能还能跨模型迁移。

Conclusion: SelfOp 证明无需专家演示、可计算奖励或留出验证，仅凭少量实例即可自动优化冻结安全代理的上下文，且优化出的技能具有可泛化任务知识而非模型特定模式。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly used for security tasks: vulnerability discovery, exploit reproduction, and patch generation. Improving them at the model level demands expert demonstrations or computable rewards, which security tasks rarely offer: traces are costly, failures hard to diagnose, rewards sparse, and non-computable. Efforts thus shift to the harness and context, but manual tuning needs task-specific expertise and scales poorly, while automated methods rely on scarce ground truth, stronger optimizer models, or unguided propose-and-evaluate loops that reduce to costly trial and error.
  We introduce SelfOp, an algorithm that automatically improves a frozen security agent's task context (instructions, skills, and reference documents), without modifying its execution harness and model weights. SelfOp casts context optimization as chain-rule-inspired textual gradient descent: from a single instance's outcome, it propagates error signals backward through the evaluator, the agent's trajectory, and the context artifacts that shaped its behavior, yielding per-instance textual gradients. Gradients are accumulated across instances by clustering, ranking, and filtering, and committed only under cross-instance consensus. A convergence detector monitors the gradient signal itself and stops once the context has absorbed the generalizable information in the training data, without held-out validation data.
  We evaluate SelfOp on CyberGym, a benchmark of real-world vulnerability reproduction tasks. With fewer than 200 training examples, SelfOp yields a 17-point self-improvement for GPT-5.4-mini (with Codex), enough to surpass the frontier GPT-5.4 baseline by 6 points, and an 18.5-point self-improvement for GPT-5.4 itself. The optimized skills also transfer across models, highlighting that SelfOp-optimized skills learn generalizable task knowledge not model-specific patterns.

</details>


### [207] [Beyond Single-Model Injection: A Threat Model and Defense Architecture for Prompt Injection in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22949)
*Rudrendu Kumar Paul,Sourav Nandy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文研究多智能体系统中的提示注入威胁，构建涵盖4类14种攻击向量的威胁模型，并在6智能体生产级系统上测试，发现43%的间接注入成功、67%的智能体存在漏洞；通过四种架构防御将注入成功率从31.2%降至4.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示注入研究主要聚焦于单模型聊天机器人场景，即攻击者通过构造输入操控单个LLM。而多智能体系统通过三种单模型场景所不具备的机制放大了这一威胁：智能体间消息传递形成对边界防御不可见的注入通道、共享工具访问使权限可跨智能体边界升级、信任传播使被攻陷的智能体能够影响上游编排器。因此需要系统性地研究多智能体环境下的注入风险。

Method: 作者构建了一个威胁模型，枚举4大类共14种攻击向量：用户输入直接注入（3种）、工具输出间接注入（4种）、消息传递的智能体间注入（4种）、以及通过编排器操纵的级联注入（3种）。将这14种向量全部针对一个由6个智能体组成的生产代表性系统进行测试，并评估四种架构防御措施的效果。

Result: 即使配置了系统提示级别的护栏，仍有67%的智能体至少对一种范围违规存在漏洞；通过工具输出的间接注入在43%的尝试中成功。四种架构防御——消息签名与来源追踪（智能体间注入下降91%）、智能体边界的输入/输出净化（间接注入下降78%）、按智能体角色限定权限的工具访问（完全消除权限升级）、以及智能体间通信模式的异常检测（捕获84%的级联尝试）——将整体注入成功率从31.2%降低至4.2%。

Conclusion: 多智能体系统显著放大了提示注入威胁，单模型的护栏措施不足以应对。组合式的架构防御（消息签名、边界净化、权限隔离、异常检测）能大幅降低注入成功率，是缓解多智能体注入风险的有效路径。

Abstract: Existing prompt injection research focuses on single-model chatbot scenarios, where an attacker manipulates one LLM through crafted input. Multi-agent systems amplify this threat through three mechanisms absent from single-model settings: inter-agent message passing creates injection channels invisible to perimeter defenses, shared tool access enables privilege escalation across agent boundaries, and trust propagation allows a compromised agent to influence upstream orchestrators. We construct a threat model enumerating 14 attack vectors across four categories: direct injection via user input (3 vectors), indirect injection via tool outputs (4 vectors), inter-agent injection via message passing (4 vectors), and cascading injection through orchestrator manipulation (3 vectors). Testing all 14 vectors against a 6-agent production-representative system, we find that 67% of agents are vulnerable to at least one scope violation even with system-prompt-level guardrails, and indirect injection via tool outputs succeeds in 43% of attempts. Four architectural defenses reduce overall injection success from 31.2% to 4.2%: message signing with provenance tracking (inter-agent injection down 91%), input/output sanitization at agent boundaries (indirect injection down 78%), privilege-scoped tool access per agent role (privilege escalation eliminated entirely), and anomaly detection on inter-agent communication patterns (84% of cascading attempts caught).

</details>


### [208] [The Price of Safety: Benign-Case Utility and Token Overhead of Memory-Poisoning Defenses in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22818)
*Pritom Bhowmik*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文通过统一测量设置对比四种LLM智能体记忆防护措施在纯良性流量下的效用代价，发现写入时防护无明显代价，而读取时重排序防护会降低准确率并大量误隔离合法记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体记忆投毒防护通常只评估其阻止攻击的能力，但实际处理的流量很少是对抗性的。防护成本每次交互都要付出，收益却只在少数攻击场景中出现，因此需要测量防护在良性流量下的真实代价。

Method: 开发统一测量设置，保持记忆后端、检索过程和评判器一致，仅改变防护本身。在五种对话、每种条件重复三次，以区分真实效应与流水线噪声。测试三种写入时防护（输入净化、来源检查、基于LLM的异常检测）和一种读取时防护（重排序），均施加于完全良性流量。

Result: 写入时防护未显示出可分辨的效用代价，95%置信区间约为±4.5分且包含零。重排序防护将核心准确率降低4.4分（95% CI [-9.0,-0.05]，bootstrap；McNemar p=0.064），结果可复现但处于分辨率边缘。在无攻击对话中，重排序在33.6%的裁决项上隔离合法记忆，单次对话最多达106次误隔离，token开销为2.7%。叠加全部四种防护并未使代价累积，组合条件的准确率损失更小且置信区间包含零。

Conclusion: 防护在流水线中拦截的位置，而非是否使用LLM，似乎决定了其在良性情况下的代价。写入时防护可能部分抵消重排序所丢弃的内容。

Abstract: Memory-poisoning defenses for LLM agents are typically evaluated by their ability to prevent attacks. However, the traffic they process is rarely adversarial. The cost of implementing a defense is paid with each interaction, while its benefits are only seen in a small percentage of cases. We developed a measurement setup that keeps the memory backend, retrieval process, and judge consistent across different conditions, changing only the defense itself. We test each condition three times across five conversations to distinguish the defense's real effects from noise inherent in the pipeline's runs, which remains significant even at temperature zero. Across three write-time defenses (input sanitization, provenance checking, and LLM-based anomaly detection) and one read-time defense (reranking), tested on entirely benign traffic, the write-time defenses show no utility cost we can resolve, with 95% confidence intervals spanning roughly +/-4.5 points and including zero. The reranker is different: it lowers core accuracy by 4.4 points (95% CI [-9.0,-0.05], bootstrap; McNemar p=0.064), a result that survives replication but sits at the edge of our resolution. Its clearer cost is mechanical rather than statistical. On conversations containing no attack, the reranker quarantines legitimate memories on 33.6% of adjudicated items, reaching as many as 106 false quarantines in a single conversation, at 2.7% token overhead. Stacking all four defenses does not compound this cost: the combined condition's accuracy loss is smaller, and its confidence interval includes zero, suggesting the write-time defenses may partly offset what the reranker discards. Where a defense intercepts the pipeline, not whether it uses an LLM, appears to determine its benign-case price.

</details>


### [209] [When Label Noise Meets Class Imbalance: A Robust Framework for Android Malware Family Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22835)
*Haolan Zhang,Cuiying Gao,Fulin Zhao,Heng Li,Haoran Wang,Chang Luo,Tiejun Wu,Hui Shu,Wei Yuan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出RoMaC框架，联合解决Android恶意软件家族分类中的标签噪声与类别不平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码混淆破坏自动标注、引入标签噪声，且数据集存在严重类别不平衡；已有研究多单独处理其中一个问题，忽视二者相互作用——在类别不平衡下，难学的少数类样本会削弱现有抗噪方法的有效性。

Method: 采用自训练策略纠正噪声标签，并有区分地对待头部家族与尾部家族样本；同时结合类别重加权机制与多模型集成学习，以同时提升分类准确率和噪声鲁棒性。

Result: 在由两个公开数据集构建的合并数据集上，30%样本被混淆时，整体Macro-F1达0.803、准确率0.871，尾部类Macro-F1为0.672、准确率0.784；相比现有方法，在多种混淆场景与噪声水平下提升6%-20%。

Conclusion: RoMaC通过区分处理头尾家族并结合重加权与集成学习，有效联合应对标签噪声和类别不平衡，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Machine learning methods for Android malware family classification have achieved high accuracy, but their application is hindered by two major challenges. First, the widely used code obfuscation severely disrupts the automated labeling process and introduces substantial label noise into training datasets. Second, training datasets often exhibit severe class imbalance, leading to poor performance of family classification models. Although existing studies have proposed various solutions to either label noise or class imbalance, they often overlook the interplay between these two factors. Under class imbalance, the presence of hard-to-learn minority-class samples can significantly impair the effectiveness of existing countermeasures for noisy samples. To jointly address label noise and class imbalance, we propose a robust Android malware family classification framework, RoMaC. It employs a self-training strategy to correct noisy labels and, more importantly, discriminately treats head-family and tail-family samples. This design effectively mitigates the adverse impact of class imbalance on noise-robust learning. Moreover, RoMaC integrates a class reweighting mechanism with multi-model ensemble learning, thereby enhancing both classification accuracy and noise robustness. We evaluate RoMaC on a combined dataset constructed from two public datasets. When 30% of the samples are obfuscated, RoMaC achieves an overall Macro-F1 score of 0.803 and an accuracy of 0.871, as well as a tail-class Macro-F1 score of 0.672 and an accuracy of 0.784. Compared with existing methods, RoMaC demonstrates performance improvements of 6%-20% across various obfuscation scenarios and noise levels.

</details>


### [210] [SMS-delivered network-initiated SUPL on Pixel 8: a privacy assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22900)
*Douglas Leith*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究通过短信发送SUPL_INIT消息是否会静默泄露手机位置与用户身份，在Google Pixel 8上测试后未发现隐私问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SUPL_INIT是一种网络发起的触发消息，可通过短信单方面启动定位会话，使手机在用户无操作的情况下确定自身位置并连同IMSI等标识上报到消息指定的服务器。作者担心攻击者可利用该机制将手机位置和用户身份静默外泄到其控制的服务器。

Method: 在Google Pixel 8手机上进行实验，该手机使用Samsung Exynos调制解调器和Broadcom GPS/GNSS子系统，测试未经请求、通过短信投递的SUPL_INIT消息的影响。

Result: 手机不会因通过短信投递的未经请求SUPL_INIT消息而将位置数据发送到攻击者选择的服务器。

Conclusion: 未发现隐私问题，SUPL_INIT短信触发机制在该设备上不会导致位置和身份信息被静默外泄。

Abstract: A SUPL\_INIT message is a network-initiated trigger that can be sent to a handset using an SMS to unilaterally start a location session: on receipt, the handset is instructed to determine its own position and report it, together with an identifier such as its IMSI, to a server specified in the message, without any action by the phone's user. The concern motivating this investigation is whether such a message could be used to silently exfiltrate a handset's location and subscriber identity to a server under an attacker's control. We investigated this on a Google Pixel 8 handset, which uses a Samsung Exynos modem and a Broadcom GPS/GNSS subsystem. We find no privacy issue: the handset never sends location data to an attacker-chosen server as a result of an unsolicited SUPL\_INIT delivered by SMS.

</details>


### [211] [When Agentic Trust Crosses Organizational Boundaries: Structural Externalization and a Reference Model for Trust Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22961)
*Huafu Li,Jia Xia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出"可信度即服务"(TaaS),围绕有界委托动作构建跨域依赖的可复用剖面,通过信任证据信封与拓扑中立的逻辑参考模型,使证据依赖、独立验证、质疑与恢复显式化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理型系统日益跨组织边界调用工具、服务、数据和其他代理,但依赖方仅凭生产域的控制与记录无法评估委托动作。跨域依赖需要独立于生产者的验证,并在撤销、失败、记录冲突或争议后仍可审查。

Method: 综合可信AI治理、智能体安全、分布式信任管理、身份、溯源、保证与控制平面研究;以"跨域依赖命题"(含签发者、主体与动作、依赖方、行政边界、证据依赖、不利条件、所需验证或裁决语义)为分析单元;提出三条件结构化外化诊断;设计信任证据信封(不可变工作流清单+仅追加、签发者归属的证明);给出拓扑中立的逻辑参考模型并分配角色与信任域;提出三个分析场景与TaaS-Eval协议。

Result: 三条件结构化外化诊断能识别出依赖多域、需生产者独立依赖、且必须在撤销、失败、记录冲突或争议后仍可审查的命题;信任证据信封覆盖任务范围权限、策略与执行决策、溯源、有效性、披露、状态、质疑与恢复;TaaS-Eval协议定义了清单、独立消费者、硬性门槛、对抗性证据测试、度量与可复现工件报告。

Conclusion: 通过围绕有界委托动作组合既有的身份、授权、溯源、保证与治理机制,TaaS为跨域依赖提供了可复用剖面,使证据依赖、独立验证、质疑与恢复显式化,支持互操作治理与未来评估,且不将生产者断言视为地面真值。

Abstract: Agentic systems increasingly invoke tools, services, data, and other agents across organizational boundaries, yet a relying party cannot assess a delegated action solely from producing-domain controls and records. This paper develops Trustworthiness as a Service (TaaS) through a synthesis of trustworthy-AI governance, agent security, distributed trust management, identity, provenance, assurance, and control-plane research. The analytical unit is a cross-domain reliance proposition that names the issuer, subject and action, relying party, administrative boundary, evidence dependencies, adverse condition, and required verification or adjudication semantics. The three-condition structural-externalization diagnostic identifies propositions that depend on multiple domains, require producer-independent reliance, and must remain reviewable after revocation, failure, conflicting records, or dispute. For such propositions, the paper specifies a trust-evidence envelope: an immutable workflow manifest linked to append-only, issuer-attributed attestations for task-scoped authority, policy and execution decisions, provenance, validity, disclosure, status, challenge, and recovery. A topology-neutral logical reference model assigns these functions to explicit roles and trust domains. Three analytical scenarios and the TaaS-Eval protocol proposal define manifests, independent consumers, hard gates, adversarial evidence tests, metrics, and reproducible artifact reporting. By composing established identity, authorization, provenance, assurance, and governance mechanisms around a bounded delegated action, TaaS provides a reusable profile for cross-domain reliance. It makes evidence dependencies, independent verification, challenge, and recovery explicit, supporting interoperable governance and future evaluation without treating producer assertions as ground truth.

</details>


### [212] [Dual-Locking Learned AI Models: A PIN-Based Sparse QIM Watermarking and Adaptive Index Permutation Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22981)
*Iva Vasic,Jesús Muñoz-Cádiz,Bata Vasic*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"双重锁定"方法，将基于密钥的索引置换与基于PIN的稀疏QIM水印结合，实现神经网络保护、可恢复与所有权验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保护已训练神经网络的知识产权，防止未授权使用，同时需要在不破坏模型可恢复性的前提下实现所有权认证。现有方法往往难以兼顾锁定有效性、完全恢复性与盲水印验证。

Method: 对自适应选择的索引向量逐行施加均匀随机置换以引入密码学随机性；通过调制偏置系数的量化值嵌入鲁棒盲二进制水印，绑定用户PIN；采用自适应密钥选择策略，将高幅值权重重新分配到低敏感位置，增强锁定态退化并保持完全恢复。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10/100、ImageNet-1K上，使用全连接网络、ResNet CNN与Transformer架构实验：锁定后准确率降至10%以下，CNN甚至低于0.5%；正确密钥可完全恢复性能；水印无可见精度损失并可可靠认证所有权；嵌入分布分析对识别欠训练或次优模型有诊断价值。

Conclusion: 该方法同时实现了模型保护、性能恢复与所有权验证，为训练好的神经网络提供了一种有效的双重锁定安全方案。

Abstract: We present a dual-locking method for securing trained neural networks that combines key-driven index permutation with PIN-based watermarking based on Sparse Quantization Index Modulation (QIM). Cryptographic randomness is introduced by independently applying a uniform random permutation to each row of adaptively selected index vectors. A robust blind binary watermark is then embedded into the bias coefficients by modulating their quantized values, binding the network to a user-defined Personal Identification Number (PIN). Without the correct key, the network retains its architecture but becomes functionally impaired due to disrupted internal representations. Inverse permutation fully restores the original model accuracy, while the embedded watermark remains imperceptible and enables blind verification of key association and model authorship. To improve both locking effectiveness and recoverability, an adaptive key selection strategy redistributes high-magnitude weights to low-sensitivity positions and vice versa, increasing degradation in the locked state while preserving full recovery. Experiments on MNIST, CIFAR-10/100, and ImageNet-1K using fully connected networks, ResNet CNNs, and transformer architectures show that locking reduces accuracy below 10\%, and even below 0.5\% for CNNs, while the correct key fully restores performance. The watermark introduces no measurable accuracy degradation and reliably authenticates ownership. Analysis of embedding distributions across CNNs and transformers further indicates potential diagnostic value for identifying undertrained or suboptimally designed models. The proposed approach therefore provides simultaneous model protection, recovery, and ownership verification.

</details>


### [213] [Secrets That Survive Everything: Runtime Credential Exposure in Production Web Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23042)
*Hemanth Gorijala*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究揭示部署前密钥扫描仅覆盖源码、忽略生产环境实际服务内容所导致的漏洞，通过两起利用链和GT-194基准证明存在工具无关的检测盲区，并给出分层运行时检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署前密钥扫描只作用于源代码，从不检查生产应用实际对外提供的内容，因此生产环境JavaScript包中遗留的活跃凭据（如Azure AD客户端凭据、APIM订阅密钥）可能长期未被发现，进而导致账户接管和大规模数据泄露。作者希望量化这一"右移"缺口。

Method: 在一次授权测试中覆盖某组织约2,000个企业Web资产，发现113个（5.65%）提供活跃凭据。构建独立的Ground Truth基准GT-194（194个密钥级凭据），通过Claude Opus 4.7提取加人工分析师复核生成，并用GPT-5.5独立验证247个LLM提取候选（Brennan-Prediger kappa=0.676）。随后对九款生产扫描器进行对比评估，并分析CryptoJS加密配置对静态扫描的规避情况。

Result: GT-194中13.9%（27/194）仅能通过人工分析发现，九款扫描器全部漏检，属工具无关盲区，ground-truth模型同样漏检。CryptoJS加密配置可击败所有静态扫描器，凭据仅在用同址密钥解密后存在，只能靠运行时感知检测。综合覆盖率上限为86.1%；九款扫描器中最佳静态扫描器召回36.6%，最佳运行时感知扫描器77.8%（F1=0.818，McNemar p<0.001）。在86个密钥暴露应用中，63个（73.3%）的完整Azure AD令牌铸造链共存于同一bundle且可从浏览器代码触达。

Conclusion: 研究刻画了凭据在未被发现情况下进入生产环境的五条路径，提出分层运行时检测方法学和修复框架。召回率结论仅限于单一组织的Azure密集型语料，ground-truth模型仅作参考对照而非被评估的检测器。

Abstract: Pre-deployment secret scanning operates only on source code, never on what a production application serves. We document two exploitation chains in which Azure AD client credentials and APIM subscription keys from production JavaScript bundles enabled account takeover and mass data exposure. An authorized engagement covered approximately 2,000 enterprise web assets in one organization; 113 (5.65%) served live credentials. To quantify the shift-right gap, we built an independent Ground Truth (GT-194) of 194 secret-grade credentials through Claude Opus 4.7 extraction and manual analyst review, with the 247 LLM-extracted candidates independently validated by GPT-5.5 (Brennan-Prediger kappa = 0.676). The principal finding is structural: 13.9% of GT-194 (27 of 194) is surfaced only by manual analysis and recovered by none of the nine evaluated production scanners, a tool-agnostic blind spot the ground-truth model also misses. CryptoJS encrypted configuration separately defeats every static scanner: the credential exists only after decryption with a co-located key, reached only by runtime-aware detection. Combined coverage plateaus at 86.1%. Among the nine scanners, the best static scanner recovers 36.6% and the best runtime-aware scanner 77.8% (F1 = 0.818, McNemar p < 0.001); the ground-truth model is reported separately as a reference comparator, not an evaluated detector. On 63 of 86 secret-exposed applications (73.3%), the full Azure AD token-mint chain is co-located in one bundle, reachable from browser code. We characterize five paths by which credentials reach production undetected and present a layered runtime detection methodology and remediation framework. Recall is scoped to a single-organization Azure-heavy corpus.

</details>


### [214] [An LLM-Assisted AutoML Framework for Intrusion Detection in IoT Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23097)
*Li Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于大语言模型（LLM）辅助的AutoML框架用于物联网入侵检测，通过LLM将数据集画像转换为有界且经验证的AutoML策略，在更低试验预算下取得更高F1-score并显著缩短优化时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网广泛部署扩大了攻击面，ML型入侵检测系统虽有效，但构建过程依赖人工专家经验与大量手动决策（预处理、特征选择、模型选择、超参数调优）。传统AutoML虽能减轻负担，却因搜索庞大候选模型族与超参数空间而耗时过长，难以在有限预算下高效获得高质量模型。

Method: 提出LLM辅助的AutoML框架，将LLM用作策略生成器，把数据集画像转化为有界且经验证的AutoML策略，覆盖自动化数据平衡、自动化特征工程以及组合算法选择与超参数优化（CASH）。在10次试验预算下与基于树结构Parzen估计器（TPE）的传统AutoML进行对比，并进一步与30次试验的传统AutoML-TPE基线比较优化器耗时。

Result: 在相同10次试验预算下，所提LLM辅助策略在CICIDS2017与IoTID20上加权测试F1-score分别为99.680%和99.186%，均高于传统AutoML-TPE。相比30次试验的传统AutoML-TPE基线，10次试验的所提方法优化器时间分别减少63.7%和49.9%，同时F1-score略高。

Conclusion: 有界LLM策略能提升低预算AutoML搜索的质量，并相对更大预算的传统搜索保持明显的效率优势，表明LLM可用于引导高效的物联网入侵检测AutoML流程。

Abstract: Internet of Things (IoT) systems are increasingly deployed in smart homes, transportation, energy systems, and critical infrastructure. This broad connectivity improves service intelligence, but also enlarges the attack surface of IoT networks. Machine Learning (ML)-based Intrusion Detection Systems (IDSs) are widely used to identify malicious network threats and protect IoT systems, but developing effective ML-based IDS models often requires human expertise and repeated manual decisions on many procedures, including data pre-processing, feature selection, model selection, and hyperparameter tuning. Automated Machine Learning (AutoML) reduces this burden by automating steps of the ML pipeline using optimization techniques, but conventional AutoML methods can consume substantial optimization time because they explore broad candidate model families and large hyperparameter spaces. This paper proposes a Large Language Model (LLM)-assisted AutoML framework for IoT intrusion detection. The proposed framework uses an LLM as a policy generator that converts dataset profiles into bounded and validated AutoML policies for automated data balancing, automated feature engineering, and Combined Algorithm Selection and Hyperparameter Optimization (CASH). Under an equal 10-trial budget, the proposed LLM-assisted policy achieves higher weighted test F1-score than traditional AutoML using the Tree-structured Parzen Estimator (TPE) on both datasets, reaching 99.680% on CICIDS2017 and 99.186% on IoTID20. Relative to the broader 30-trial Traditional AutoML-TPE baseline, the 10-trial proposed method reduces optimizer time by 63.7% and 49.9%, respectively, while achieving slightly higher F1-score. These results show that a bounded LLM policy can improve the quality of a low-budget AutoML search while retaining a clear efficiency advantage relative to a larger conventional search budget.

</details>


### [215] [Exploiting Software-level Abstractions To Support Practical Hardware Trojan Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23173)
*Athanasios Moschos,Kevin Valakuzhy,Georgios Kokolakis,Fabian Monrose,Angelos D. Keromytis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SURF类CPU硬件木马，可在不依赖任意代码执行的情况下被激活，通过对RISC-V处理器的实现及对V8引擎JavaScript内存索引操作的利用，完成代码注入攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CPU硬件木马攻击通常假设攻击者能在被植入木马的CPU系统上执行任意机器级代码，而在终端设备（手机、笔记本）上实现任意代码执行需针对每个目标定制软件漏洞利用。这种过强的对抗前提削弱了现有威胁模型的通用性，使CPU木马攻击作为现实威胁向量受到质疑。

Method: 核心洞察是：用高级语言表达的整数运算可映射为可被SURF触发电路区分的微架构副作用，从而可通过运行时引擎（执行不可信高级代码的受限环境）激活硬件木马。作者在RISC-V处理器中实现SURF木马，并利用Google V8引擎中JavaScript层面的内存索引操作执行代码注入攻击。

Result: SURF木马在多个JavaScript引擎版本上均保持有效，能够实现对终端设备的长期性攻陷。作者将SURF的设计及相关支持软件开源以促进后续研究。

Conclusion: 通过将硬件木马触发条件与高级语言整数运算的微架构副作用关联，SURF降低了CPU木马攻击对任意代码执行的依赖，提升了此类威胁在客户端设备上的现实可行性与通用性。

Abstract: Hardware trojan (HT) attacks against CPUs typically assume threat scenarios where an attacker targeting a system with a trojanized CPU is able to execute arbitrary code (i.e. machine-level instructions) to reliably interact with the implanted trojan. On end-user devices (i.e., mobiles, laptops), achieving arbitrary code execution in practice requires software exploits tailored to each specific target. Such strong adversarial premises reduce the generality of existing threat models casting doubt on CPU trojan attacks as a pragmatic threat vector. To push the envelope on HT attacks against client devices, we introduce the SURF class of CPU-trojans that can be activated without arbitrary code execution. Our key insight is that integer operations expressed in a high-level language can be mapped to microarchitectural side-effects distinguishable by a SURF trigger circuit. This observation unlocks HT activation via runtime engines, constrained environments executing untrusted high-level code. We demonstrate a SURF trojan inside a RISC-V processor and exploit JavaScript-level memory indexing operations inside Google's V8 engine to perform a code injection attack. Importantly, we show that SURF trojans remain effective across multiple JavaScript engine versions, enabling long-term compromise of endpoint devices. To facilitate research, we opensource SURF's design and supporting software.

</details>


### [216] [Assessing Runtime Electromagnetic Detection of CPU Hardware Trojans Targeting Kernel Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23186)
*Athanasios Moschos,Baki Berkay Yilmaz,Kevin Valakuzhy,Angelos D. Keromytis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究利用电磁辐射（EM）实现硬件木马的运行时检测。基于开源硬件木马在RISC-V架构上篡改Linux内核内存，通过处理器EM辐射进行实时基线检测，结果表明在特定条件下可通过木马引发的异常软件行为间接检测到硬件木马。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件木马检测通常需要额外电路或破坏性分析，而侧信道方法可被动地揭示恶意硬件活动。作者希望探索电磁辐射用于运行时硬件木马检测的可行性，避免侵入式手段。

Method: 使用一个实现任意内存写原语的开源硬件木马，对运行在RISC-V微架构上的Linux系统内核内存进行篡改，并基于处理器的电磁辐射进行实时基线检测。

Result: 实验表明，在特定条件下，可以通过硬件木马所引发的异常软件行为间接检测到硬件木马的存在。

Conclusion: 电磁侧信道为硬件木马运行时检测提供了一种可行的非侵入式途径；硬件木马可通过其导致的异常软件行为被间接识别。

Abstract: Side-channels are a promising approach for hardware trojan detection, as they can passively reveal malicious hardware activity without requiring additional circuitry or destructive analysis of the device under test. This work investigates the use of electromagnetic (EM) emanations for runtime detection of hardware trojan attacks. Using an open-source hardware trojan that implements an arbitrary memory-write primitive, we tamper with the kernel memory of a Linux system running on a RISC-V microarchitecture and perform a real-time baseline detection using the processor's EM emissions. Our results indicate that, under certain conditions, hardware trojans can be detected indirectly through the anomalous software behavior they enable.

</details>


### [217] [LLMs as Linguistic Chameleons: Decoupling Semantics and Structure for Privacy-Preserving Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23193)
*Yuzhu Mao,Liang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出CROSS-MAP双向框架，通过语义解耦将隐私输入映射到不同语义域再恢复输出，在降低重构攻击成功率的同时保持任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM API日益融入隐私敏感场景，现有推理期隐私保护方法为维持下游性能而保留大部分原始语义，反而为攻击者提供了可重构原文的线索，隐私与效用难以兼顾。

Method: 基于语义解耦思想，用替代内容替换原始语义、同时保留LLM推理所需的结构；采用双向框架，在推理前将私有输入映射到另一语义域，推理后再恢复对应输出；用多目标优化训练本地模型，映射阶段最大化语义差异，恢复阶段最小化语义不一致。

Result: 在多种攻击设置下，CROSS-MAP均降低了文本重构成功率，并且在效用上优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 语义解耦为推理期隐私保护提供了兼顾隐私与效用的可行思路，CROSS-MAP通过双向映射与多目标优化实现了该目标。

Abstract: As Large Language Model (LLM) APIs become increasingly integrated into privacy-sensitive workflows, ensuring inference-time privacy without compromising task utility remains a major challenge. Existing approaches preserve most of the original semantic content to maintain downstream performance, but this also leaves exploitable cues for reconstructing the original text. This work investigates semantic decoupling, which replaces original semantics with alternative content while preserving the structure needed for LLM reasoning. Based on this idea, we propose CROSS-MAP, a bidirectional framework that maps private inputs into a different semantic domain before inference and recovers the corresponding outputs afterward. Local models are trained with multi-objective optimization to maximize semantic divergence in the mapping stage while minimizing semantic inconsistency in the recovery stage. Experiments show that CROSS-MAP reduces reconstruction success across multiple attack settings while outperforming existing baselines in utility.

</details>


### [218] [SoK: From Finding to Deployment: Systematizing the OS Kernel Bug Lifecycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23218)
*Luyao bai,Gengda She,Kenan Alghythee,Hang Zhang,Xiaoguang Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文是一篇SoK论文，系统梳理Linux内核缺陷从发现到部署的完整生命周期，指出自动化集中在"发现"阶段，而修复流水线存在结构性断裂，其根源是真实缺陷报告常缺少可靠复现、局部根因和可检验的正确性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化内核缺陷发现（如syzkaller/syzbot、静态分析）已高度成熟，产生的崩溃报告规模远超下游处理能力。然而崩溃报告只是起点，缺陷还需经分类、理解、修复、验证、评审、集成乃至回移等环节，这些后续阶段自动化程度低，导致"崩溃到修复"的鸿沟长期存在。作者希望系统化理解这一鸿沟的本质，而非仅将其视为未修复报告的积压。

Method: 采用SoK（Systematization of Knowledge）方法，将已有研究与生产系统按五个阶段组织：发现、分类、补丁生成、补丁验证、集成。通过内核特有的挑战（并发、隐式不变式、跨系统调用状态、硬件依赖、缺乏故障隔离、架构与配置多样性）解释自动化程度的梯度差异。并结合对真实syzbot已修复缺陷的实证测量来支撑分析。

Result: 实证数据显示，崩溃到补丁的鸿沟并非单纯的未修复报告积压，而是修复流水线的结构性故障模式：缺陷即使在修复后仍常保持开放数周，需要由评审驱动的补丁修订，或缺少当前修复与验证系统所假定的复现器。这揭示出内核安全自动化成熟之处与缺陷真正收敛之处之间的错配。

Conclusion: 当前修复与验证技术往往预设存在可靠复现器、局部化根因以及可检验的正确性判据，但这些恰恰是许多真实内核缺陷报告所缺失的产物。缩小崩溃到补丁的鸿沟，需要将这些产物视为流水线应主动"生产"的输出，而非可以默认"假设"存在的前提。

Abstract: Automated kernel bug discovery has advanced rapidly. Continuous fuzzing and static analysis systems, such as syzbot, now expose Linux kernel bugs at a scale that downstream processes struggle to absorb. Yet a crash report is only the beginning. Before a bug is eliminated, it must be triaged, understood, patched, validated, reviewed, integrated, and often backported. These later stages remain far less automated, creating a persistent gap between bug discovery and patch deployment.
  This SoK systematizes the Linux kernel bug lifecycle from discovery to deployment. We organize prior work and production systems into five stages: discovery, triage, patch generation, patch validation, and integration. We explain the resulting automation gradient through kernel-specific challenges such as concurrency, implicit invariants, cross-syscall state, hardware dependence, lack of fault isolation, and architecture/configuration multiplicity. We further ground the analysis in a measurement of real syzbot-fixed bugs. The data shows that the crash-to-patch gap is not merely a backlog of unfixed reports but a structural failure mode of the repair pipeline: even after being fixed, bugs often remain open for weeks, require review-driven patch revisions, or lack reproducers that current repair and validation systems assume. This exposes a mismatch between where kernel-security automation is mature and where bug closure actually breaks down. These findings expose a deeper mismatch: today's repair and validation techniques often assume reliable reproducers, localized root causes, and checkable correctness oracles, yet these are precisely the artifacts missing from many real kernel bug reports. Closing the crash-to-patch gap, therefore, requires treating such artifacts as outputs to be produced, not prerequisites to be assumed.

</details>


### [219] [Security of Agent-Integrated Software: When Human Operations and Agent Actions Coexist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23226)
*Ding Yang,Yuchen Ling,Shengcheng Yu,Zhenyu Chen,Chunrong Fang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出应将"智能体集成软件（AIS）"的安全性放在整个软件系统层面进行评估，并把人类操作与智能体动作共存所引发的安全问题归纳为四类，进而指出当前实践的局限与未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AIS 将智能体嵌入传统应用中，人类操作与智能体动作互补共存，且二者作用于同一软件状态并可互相利用对方结果。因此仅分别保护智能体与传统软件核心无法保证整体安全，安全策略必须同时覆盖两条路径，需要在系统层面重新审视其安全问题。

Method: 作者通过概念性框架构建与现状分析展开工作：首先论证 AIS 安全应作为整体系统问题来评估；然后将共存引发的安全问题组织为四个类别——上下文误用、授权违规、执行控制与效果完整性；最后借助这四个类别考察现有实践的处理方式及其保护不足之处。

Result: 分析表明，当前实践在应对 AIS 安全问题时保护能力有限，无法充分覆盖人类操作与智能体动作共存带来的跨路径风险；据此识别出四个研究机会：保持信息溯源、跨操作路径统一执行策略、随时间维持有效授权，以及管理持久化效果与恢复。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一个理解并改进 AIS 安全的概念性框架，主张以整体系统视角评估安全性，并为后续研究指明了信息溯源、策略执行、授权时效与持久效果管理等关键方向。

Abstract: Agent-Integrated Software (AIS) embeds an intelligent agent in a conventional application, supporting both human operations and agent actions. Human operations let users make precise changes and inspect results, while agent actions carry out routine or multi-step tasks. These complementary roles make coexistence a likely long-term feature of many software systems. Human operations and agent actions affect the same software state and can use one another's results. Therefore, security policies must remain effective across both paths. We argue that AIS security must be assessed at the level of the whole software system. Protecting the agent and the conventional software core separately does not establish that they are secure together. To guide security analysis of AIS as a whole, we organize the problems arising from this coexistence into four categories: context misuse, authorization violation, execution control, and effect integrity. Using these categories, we examine how current practices address the security problems in AIS and where their protection remains limited. Building on this analysis, we identify research opportunities in preserving information provenance, enforcing policy across operation paths, maintaining valid authorization over time, and managing persistent effects and recovery. This resulting perspective provides a conceptual framework for understanding and improving the security of AIS.

</details>


### [220] [CLOADER: Evading Security Mobile Defenses via Runtime Obfuscation and Adaptive Hooking Tactics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23396)
*Nhat-Anh Huynh,Minh Quang Luu,Ngoc Hong Tran*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一个隐蔽框架，通过动态规避策略替代静态配置，在受保护的移动环境中隐藏Frida、Xposed等Hook工具的痕迹，实现约90%的绕过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在加固的移动生态（企业反恶意软件、加固应用、设备管理平台）中，现有Hook工具易被基于签名、时序启发式和篡改检测的安全机制发现，而现有规避方法各自独立使用、效果有限，需要统一协调的规避方案以支持渗透测试与恶意软件分析。

Method: 引入统一运行时控制层，系统协调网络、时序与代码层面的规避变换：随机端口分配、运行时代码混淆、延迟执行触发、自完整性校验；通过自定义Android加载器CLoader强制执行这些机制，将Hook活动与安全监控隔离，同时保留完整的拦截与修改能力。

Result: 在企业反恶意软件系统、加固应用和设备管理平台上的验证表明，评估矩阵中绕过率达到90%。

Conclusion: 该方法无需架构性改造即可掩盖网络、时序和代码层面的指纹，为锁定式移动生态系统中的可靠渗透测试和恶意软件分析提供支持。

Abstract: We propose a stealth framework that eliminates detection of hooking tools such as Frida and Xposed in secured mobile environments by replacing static configurations with dynamic evasion tactics. In contrast to existing approaches that apply these techniques independently, the framework introduces a unified runtime control layer that systematically coordinates network, temporal, and code-level evasive transformations. The solution integrates randomized port allocation, runtime code obfuscation, delayed execution triggers, and self-integrity checks to disrupt signature-based scans, timing heuristics, and tampering attempts. A custom Android loader, CLoader, enforces these mechanisms to isolate hooking activities from security monitors while maintaining complete interception and modification capabilities. Validation across enterprise anti malware systems, hardened applications, and device management platforms demonstrates a 90% bypass rate in our evaluation matrix. This approach enables reliable penetration testing and malware analysis in locked-down mobile ecosystems by masking network, temporal, and code-level fingerprints without architectural overhauls.

</details>


### [221] [The Anatomy of Address Poisoning on Ethereum: Funding Mechanisms, Scam Signatures, and Laundering via Tornado Cash](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23405)
*Son Hoang Dau,Thanh Nguyen,Phuong Duy Huynh,Hong Yen Tran,Nicholas Huppert,Jeff Nijsse,Huong Ha,Xun Yi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对区块链地址投毒钓鱼骗局（APT），在既有检测研究之外，首次系统考察其资金筹集机制、骗局特征签名与赃款清洗三个薄弱环节，提出五类可用于地址聚类的APT操作特征签名，并首次研究其通过Tornado Cash进行资金筹集与赃款清洗的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: APT骗局虽手法简单，却已造成用户数百万美元损失，并引起学术界关注。然而已有工作主要聚焦于检测，对骗局的资金来源、运作特征以及赃款如何通过公共洗钱服务清洗等关键问题仍缺乏研究。

Method: 在超越检测的思路下，围绕APT的资金筹集机制、骗局签名、通过公共服务洗钱三个尚未充分探索的方面展开分析，提出五类骗局签名以刻画APT运作的关键环节，并用于地址聚类；同时开展对Tornado Cash在APT资金筹集与赃款清洗中作用的首次调查。

Result: 提出了五类APT骗局特征签名，可用于地址聚类；并首次揭示了Tornado Cash被用于为APT筹集资金和清洗赃款的使用情况。

Conclusion: 本研究拓展了APT研究的范围，从单一检测转向对其资金链与洗钱链条的整体剖析，所提签名与发现有助于后续地址聚类与反钓鱼工作。

Abstract: Address-Poisoning Transfer (APT) is a prevalent blockchain phishing scam in which a scammer poisons a victim's address book by generating a transfer with a phishing address that looks similar to a benign address that the victim has previously interacted with. Although simple, APT phishing attacks have cost users millions of dollars in recent years, which has captured the attention of the research community (Ye et al. WWW'24, Guan-Li CCS'24, Chen et al. NDSS'25, Tsuchiya et al. USENIX'25). In this work, we go beyond detection and investigate three important and underexplored aspects of APT: scam funding mechanisms, scam signatures, and scam proceeds laundering via public services. In particular, we propose five families of scam signatures that capture key aspects of APT operations, which are useful for address clustering. We also conduct the first investigation into usage of Tornado Cash for funding APTs and laundering scam proceeds.

</details>


### [222] [Runtime Authorization Consistency Checking for MCP-based Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23498)
*Aiyao Zhang,Xiaodong Lee,Zhixian Zhuang,Botao Peng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文识别出多步工具工作流中的"授权漂移"失效模式，并提出运行期授权一致性检查（RAC）方法，在工具调用边界以轻量守卫方式阻止越权序列，评估显示其显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统通过多步工具工作流处理用户请求时，逐调用独立检查可能遗漏工作流层面的失效：单次调用各自合规，但序列累积可超出会话授权边界，即"授权漂移"。需要一种在运行期跨步骤追踪授权状态、防止序列越权的方法。

Method: 提出运行期授权一致性检查（RAC），作为控制器侧工具调用边界的轻量守卫。将授权视为由已接受工作流步骤承载的运行期状态；对每个待执行动作，从控制器可观测元数据重建可信授权事件，仅当该调用不比通过已接受血缘继承的授权基础更宽松时才放行。被拒绝步骤不纳入血缘，后续延续只能从已接受历史获得支持。

Result: 在受控与规划器生成的工作流中均减少漏检的授权漂移。在1,248个工作流的TraceBench上，RAC无漏拦截案例，而最强的Static+History基线在1,008个oracle-BLOCK工作流中漏检509个。在高置信可观测的LLM盲生成计划子集上，RAC阻断召回率达92.8%，基线为68.8%。在真实MCP文件系统规划器回放中，RAC在服务器执行前阻止9个不安全延续，p99检查延迟低于1毫秒。

Conclusion: RAC通过将授权作为运行期状态并沿已接受血缘传递，有效拦截跨步骤的授权漂移，显著优于静态与历史基线，且开销极低，适用于真实工具工作流场景。

Abstract: Agentic systems increasingly fulfill user requests through multi-step tool workflows over files, services, and external resources. In these workflows, isolated per-call checks can miss a workflow-level failure: each call may be locally admissible, but the sequence can exceed the authorization boundary established for the session. We identify this failure mode "authorization drift." To address this problem, we present Runtime Authorization Consistency Checking (RAC), a lightweight guard at the controller-side tool-call boundary. RAC treats authorization as runtime state carried by accepted workflow steps. For each pending action, it reconstructs a trusted authorization event from controller-observed metadata and admits the call only when it remains no more permissive than the basis inherited through accepted lineage. Rejected steps are excluded from lineage, so later continuations can draw support only from accepted workflow history. Our evaluation shows that RAC reduces missed authorization drift across both controlled and planner-generated workflows. On the 1,248-workflow TraceBench suite, RAC has no missed-block cases, while the strongest Static+History baseline misses 509 of 1,008 oracle-BLOCK workflows. On a high-confidence observable subset of blind LLM-generated plans, RAC reaches 92.8% block recall, compared with 68.8% for the strongest baseline. In the real MCP filesystem planner replay, RAC stops nine unsafe continuations before server execution, with sub-millisecond p99 checking latency.

</details>


### [223] [Endogenous Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23514)
*Antonio Nappa*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出"内源性解释"框架，将程序、解释器、机器及其派生执行语言统一视为同一可执行状态转移关系的不同参数化，并论证"语义鸿沟"隐喻在操作语义中具有误导性，语义保持变换实际上是"语义扩散"而非消除计算。作者给出首个可量化实例：对有限状态实现，双模拟意义下的收缩是规范且几乎线性时间可计算的，从而得到与抽象无关的扩散比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点将程序、解释器、机器与派生执行语言视为彼此分离的语义对象，并借用"语义鸿沟"来描述二者之间的差异。作者认为这一隐喻在操作语义层面会产生误导：它暗示语义保持变换能够消除执行所需的计算，但事实上计算无法被消除，只能在不同的状态空间与转移粒度上被重新分配。因此需要一个更精确的框架来刻画这种重新分配，并为反向工程提供可量化的理论基础。

Method: 作者提出"内源性解释"：程序、解释器、机器与派生执行语言并非互不相交的语义对象，而是同一可执行状态转移关系的不同参数化；每种实现都带有隐式约束偏置，来源于其指令基础、状态编码、控制转移以及有限基底所施加的结构性限制。在此视角下，语义保持变换被重新理解为"语义扩散"，即在另一状态空间与转移粒度上重新分布执行所需计算。作者进一步构造了首个定量实例，针对有限状态实现，在双模拟（bisimulation）观测等价下研究语义收缩，并证明该收缩是规范的且可在几乎线性时间内计算，由此导出与抽象无关的扩散比；对确定性实现，该结果可推广到迹等价（trace equivalence）。

Result: 对于在双模拟意义下观测的有限状态实现，语义收缩是规范的，并可在几乎线性时间内计算，从而产生一个与所选抽象无关的扩散比。对于确定性实现，该结果可进一步扩展到迹等价情形。对于在迹意义下观测的非确定性实现，最小收缩的性质在摘要中被提出（原文在此处截断，未给出完整结论）。

Conclusion: 该工作将程序、解释器、机器与执行语言统一为同一状态转移关系的不同参数化，用"语义扩散"取代"语义鸿沟"隐喻，强调语义保持变换无法消除执行所需的计算而只能重新分配它；反向工程因而可被理解为寻找复杂度更低的语义收缩。作为首个定量实例，有限状态实现下的收缩具有规范性与近乎线性可计算性，并给出抽象无关的扩散比，为后续推广到确定性及非确定性情形奠定了基础。

Abstract: We propose endogenous interpretation: program, interpreter, machine, and derived execution language are not disjoint semantic objects but different parameterizations of one executable state-transition relation. Each realization carries an implicit constraint bias, the structural restrictions imposed by its instruction basis, state encoding, control transfers, and finite substrate. From this viewpoint the "semantic gap" metaphor is misleading for operational semantics: a semantics-preserving transformation cannot remove the computation required for execution; it redistributes it over another state space and transition granularity (semantic diffusion). Reverse engineering is then the recovery of a lower-complexity semantic contraction.
  We give a first quantitative instance. For finite-state realizations observed up to bisimulation, the contraction is canonical and computable in almost-linear time, yielding an abstraction-independent diffusion ratio; for deterministic realizations this extends to trace equivalence. For nondeterministic realizations observed up to traces, minimal contraction is

</details>


### [224] [POZZER: A Power Side Channel-guided Fuzzer for Black-Box Embedded Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23583)
*Pouya Narimani,Kseniia Rogova,Addison Crump,Martin Mohl,Meng Wang,Ulysse Planta,Pansilu Pitigalaarachchi,Ali Abbasi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出POZZER，一种基于功耗侧信道的黑盒嵌入式系统固件模糊测试器，通过功耗轨迹构建执行行为图作为反馈，无需固件源码、镜像或调试接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有覆盖率引导的固件模糊测试器依赖固件插桩、硬件调试接口或固件重宿主来获取反馈，这需要固件源码或二进制镜像。而对于现成的嵌入式设备，固件往往不可访问、不可重宿主、不可修改或不可调试，因此需要在黑盒条件下进行模糊测试。

Method: POZZER以功耗轨迹作为反馈，通过增量构建基于图的执行表示来识别未见过的行为，引导模糊测试器探索未覆盖的执行路径。其非剖析（non-profiling）设计无需先验固件知识，也无需克隆设备，每次执行仅从单条功耗轨迹中提取有效反馈，并对测量噪声保持鲁棒。

Result: 在15个固件目标（涵盖两个平台和两个真实商用嵌入式设备）上评估。在生成的30个目标-平台组合中，POZZER在26个组合上于相同时限内优于盲模糊测试器。此外，在一个商用设备中发现两个厂商确认的未知漏洞。

Conclusion: POZZER证明了在无固件访问权限的黑盒嵌入式系统中进行漏洞挖掘的可行性，功耗侧信道可作为有效的模糊测试反馈信号。

Abstract: Firmware fuzzing is an effective technique for discovering vulnerabilities in embedded systems. However, existing coverage-guided firmware fuzzers typically obtain feedback through firmware instrumentation, hardware debug interfaces, or firmware rehosting, which requires access to the firmware source code or binary image. Such requirements are often infeasible for off-the-shelf embedded devices, where firmware binaries are inaccessible, unrehostable, immodifiable, or undebuggable, necessitating fuzzing under black-box conditions.
  In this paper, we present POZZER, a power side-channel-guided fuzzer for black-box embedded systems. POZZER uses power traces as feedback to identify previously unseen behavior via an incrementally constructed graph-based representation of observed executions, guiding the fuzzer toward unexplored execution paths. Its non-profiling design requires neither prior firmware knowledge nor a clone device, extracting meaningful feedback from a single power trace per execution while remaining robust to measurement noise. We evaluate POZZER on 15 firmware targets across two platforms and two real-world commercial embedded devices. Across the resulting target-platform combinations, POZZER outperforms a blind fuzzer under the same time budget in 26 out of 30 target-platform combinations. Furthermore, POZZER discovers two previously unknown vulnerabilities in one of the commercial devices, both confirmed by the vendor, demonstrating its potential for identifying vulnerabilities in black-box embedded systems.

</details>


### [225] [When the Agent Becomes the Kernel: A Systematization of Security on the Path to AI-Native Operating Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23700)
*Li Zhang,Yang Sun,Jie Shi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文将大语言模型代理视为拥有内核级权限的"事实内核"，提出以"溯源中介 vs. 内容语义中介"这一核心区分来系统化其安全问题，指出语义判断类中介存在不可约的残余攻击面，并据此给出防御分类、评估有效性批判及AI原生操作系统研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型代理已能执行改代码库、操作收件箱、完成购买等高后果动作，拥有内核级权限，却缺乏经典系统安全所要求的"完全中介"可信仲裁者。操作系统厂商正围绕这一事实内核重构平台，因此需要一套系统化的安全分析框架来理解中介缺口、评估防御与攻击统计，并为安全优先的AI原生操作系统确立设计约束。

Method: 作者采用系统化（systematization）方法：以"中介是否可判定"为单一区分标准，建立信任边界分类学以定位中介必须发生的位置；将核心中介缺口归结为两类语义判断（区分不可信输入中的数据与指令、区分已授权与未授权动作）；据此把攻击成功统计映射到"部署债务—结构性缺口"谱系上；并将防御划分为运行时监控、架构隔离与授权三类，同时剖析现有评估的效度失效。

Result: 分析表明：凡输入与动作未被事先限定于可枚举集合之处，语义中介缺口都会留下不可约的未检测攻击残余；现有评估常因评估效度失效而高估实际部署安全性；攻击成功数字需按确定性中介是否已知来解读，而非一概视为同类威胁。

Conclusion: 安全问题的关键不在于能否消除全部攻击，而在于区分"可判定但未部署"的部署债务与"无可判定中介"的结构性缺口。作者将分析从事实内核推进到"以模型本身为仲裁核心"的架构，推导出安全优先AI原生操作系统的设计约束、开放挑战与研究议程。

Abstract: Large language model agents are now privileged principals that take consequential actions: editing code repositories, operating inboxes, completing purchases. Their authority is kernel-grade, but it comes without what classical systems security requires: a trusted mediator interposed on every access. Operating-system vendors are now rebuilding the platform around this de-facto agent kernel, inheriting complete mediation as a design problem. We systematize the security of such systems around a single distinction: a crossing mediated over provenance admits a deterministic check, while one over content semantics does not. A trust-boundary taxonomy locates where mediation must occur and isolates the central mediation gap at two kinds of semantic judgment: distinguishing data from instruction in untrusted input, and an authorized action from an unauthorized one. We argue that this gap leaves an irreducible residual of undetected attacks wherever inputs and actions are not restricted in advance to an enumerated set. The same distinction makes attack-success statistics actionable, placing each number on a spectrum from deployment debt (a sound deterministic mediator left unused) to a structural gap (no such mediator known). We systematize defenses across runtime monitoring, architectural separation, and authorization, and show that current evaluations tend to overstate deployed security through evaluation-validity failures. Finally, we carry that analysis forward beyond the de-facto kernel, to an architecture in which the model itself becomes the arbitration core, and derive the design constraints, open challenges, and research agenda for a security-first AI-native OS.

</details>


### [226] [TriFleetRCA: On-Premise LLM Root Cause Analysis for Kubernetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23766)
*Rohit Patel,Susil Kumar Mohanty,Jeenal Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TriFleetRCA是一个完全在单块本地GPU上运行的Kubernetes根因分析流水线，通过三级范围（pod/namespace/cluster）证据收集、模板去重+BM25排序、runbook摄入防护，在活体集群100次分析中达到0.85-0.95命中率，中位延迟1.6秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程站点根因分析缓慢，证据分散在pod日志、Kubernetes事件和集群级对象中；许多运维方无法将生产日志发送给托管模型。本地推理虽解决了数据外发问题，但在单块工作站GPU同时约束模型与上下文预算的场景下，如何检索证据、以及当模型参考的runbook被篡改时会发生什么，尚缺乏活体集群基准研究。

Method: 提出TriFleetRCA流水线：全程单块本地GPU运行；在三种范围（pod、namespace、cluster）收集证据；先模板去重再用BM25排序；通过摄入守卫过滤runbook；输出根因及支撑证据行。在活体Kubernetes集群注入四种故障（真值由构造已知），使用Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct、温度0，完成100次分析。

Result: pod、namespace、cluster范围命中率分别为0.85、0.90、0.95，区间重叠，范围效应全部来自一个根因为集群级对象的故障，且cluster范围token开销高55%。去重前置排序将命中率从0.75提升至0.90（同等token成本）。投毒runbook（指示删除命名空间）每次均被守卫拒绝；禁用守卫时模型在全部20次分析中均拒绝执行，说明守卫属纵深防御而非唯一屏障。将引用质量与准确率分离后发现一个新失效模式：某故障每次诊断正确但引用错误，该模式被准确率掩盖。中位延迟1.6秒，提示约2,200 token。已开源流水线、故障注入器及全部记录。

Conclusion: 单GPU本地化RCA流水线在活体集群中可行且低延迟；检索前的模板去重对命中率提升显著且不增加token成本；范围扩大收益有限而代价明显；runbook摄入守卫提供纵深防御；分离引用质量与准确率可揭示被掩盖的失效模式。

Abstract: Root cause analysis at a remote site is slow: evidence is scattered across pod logs, Kubernetes events and cluster-level objects, and many operators cannot send production logs to a hosted model at all. On-premise inference removes the second constraint but raises a question live-cluster benchmarks have not addressed: when one workstation GPU fixes both the model and the context budget, how should evidence be retrieved, and what happens when the runbooks the model consults have been tampered with? We present TriFleetRCA, a pipeline running entirely on one on-premise GPU that collects evidence at one of three scopes (pod, namespace, cluster), ranks it by template de-duplication then BM25, filters runbooks through an ingest guard, and returns a root cause with the evidence lines supporting it. We evaluate on a live Kubernetes cluster into which we inject four faults, so ground truth is known by construction, across 100 analyses with Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct at temperature 0. The hit rate was 0.85, 0.90 and 0.95 at pod, namespace and cluster scope; intervals overlap, but the whole scope effect comes from the one fault whose cause is a cluster-level object, and cluster scope costs 55% more tokens. De-duplication before ranking raised the hit rate from 0.75 to 0.90 at equal token cost. A poisoned runbook telling the model to delete the namespace was rejected by the guard every run; with the guard disabled the model declined to follow it in all 20 analyses, making the guard defence in depth rather than the sole barrier. Separating citation quality from accuracy proved informative: one fault was diagnosed correctly and cited incorrectly every trial, a failure mode accuracy conceals. Median latency was 1.6 s at 2,200 prompt tokens. We release the pipeline, the fault injector and all records.

</details>


### [227] [Pattern-level Differential Privacy for High-utility Complex Event Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23827)
*He Gu,Thomas Plagemann,Vera Goebel,Maik Benndorf,Boris Koldehofe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对复杂事件处理（CEP）系统中现有隐私保护机制（PPMs）过度限制、降低数据效用的问题，提出通过在未保护数据流上动态自适应添加噪声来提升检测事件模式效用的新方法，并引入"模式级差分隐私"（pattern-level DP）作为新的隐私保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CEP 系统的隐私保护机制过于严格，导致数据消费者接收到的数据效用被不必要地降低；同时缺乏在"模式级"层面衡量和比较隐私保护强度的统一保证。

Method: 1）提出在未保护数据流上动态自适应添加噪声的隐私保护方法；2）引入新的"模式级差分隐私"保证，用于在模式层面应用与比较不同 PPM 的强度；3）提出可达到模式级 DP 的新型模式级 PPMs；4）分析这些 PPMs 的不同信任设置及其对上下文知识（如已部署查询）的需求。

Result: 基于三个数据集（两个真实、一个合成）的评估表明：所提 PPMs 在保持与最先进 PPMs 相同隐私水平的同时提升了数据效用；通过模拟研究了其在多种实际场景下的性能；并证明计算复杂度不构成部署障碍。

Conclusion: 通过动态自适应噪声与模式级差分隐私保证，可在不牺牲隐私水平的前提下显著提升 CEP 系统中的数据效用，且该方法在计算上可行、具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Current privacy-preserving mechanisms (PPMs) in Complex Event Processing (CEP) systems are unnecessarily restrictive, reducing the utility of data received by data consumers. This article presents a novel approach to preserve privacy in CEP systems, improving the utility of detected event patterns by dynamically adapting the noise added to an unprotected data stream. We introduce a new guarantee named pattern-level differential privacy (DP), which enables us to apply and compare the strength of PPMs at the pattern level. We propose new pattern-level PPMs yielding pattern-level DP and analyze different trust settings of these PPMs and their requirements for context knowledge in the CEP system, e.g., the deployed queries. Our evaluation is based on three datasets (two real-world, one synthetic) and shows that the proposed PPMs increase data utility while preserving the same privacy level as the state-of-the-art PPMs. We use simulations to study the performance of our proposed PPMs in various practical scenarios. Furthermore, we demonstrate that computational complexity is not an obstacle to deployment.

</details>


### [228] [SyzHarness: Patch-Based Kernel Bug Reproduction with LLM-Synthesized Fuzzing Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23889)
*Xingyu Li,Juefei Pu,Haonan Li,Arrdya Srivastav,Kareem Shehada,Srikanth V. Krishnamurthy,Zhiyun Qian*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SyzHarness 结合 LLM 推理与覆盖率引导模糊测试，用于基于补丁的 Linux 内核漏洞复现，在多个数据集上取得 73%-80% 的复现成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化内核漏洞复现对缺陷分诊、补丁验证和回归测试至关重要，但现有方案效果不佳。核心难点有两点：一是需要恢复到达脆弱状态所需的触发脚手架，二是要确定真正触发漏洞的精确具体值。定向模糊测试难以恢复触发脚手架，而仅靠 LLM 生成则因难以发现具体值且存在运行时非确定性而较为脆弱。

Method: 提出 SyzHarness 框架，将 LLM 推理与覆盖率引导模糊测试结合。给定补丁后，由代码导航工具支撑的 LLM 智能体合成参数化模糊测试 harness，固定前置设置逻辑，仅暴露不确定且对触发漏洞关键的输入参数供 Syzkaller 变异；随后将该 harness 转换为 Syzkaller 兼容接口，并利用分层可达性反馈进行迭代精炼。

Result: 在 100 个 KernelCTF 案例上达到 78% 的漏洞复现成功率；在 SyzDirect 基准上达到 73%，显著优于先前的定向灰盒模糊测试；在 50 个 2026 年 3 月后修复且已知可触发的近期 syzbot 漏洞上，仅以修复提交为输入复现了 40/50（80%）。

Conclusion: SyzHarness 通过 LLM 与覆盖率引导模糊测试的协同，有效解决了内核漏洞复现中触发脚手架恢复与具体值发现两大难题，在多类真实数据集上均展现出高复现成功率，明显优于现有定向灰盒模糊测试方法。

Abstract: Automated kernel vulnerability reproduction is essential for bug triage, patch validation, and regression testing, but
  still lacks an effective and efficient solution. The core challenge is twofold: a reproducer must first recover the
  trigger scaffold needed to reach the vulnerable state and determine the precise concrete values that actually trigger
  the bug. Existing directed fuzzing approaches are ineffective at recovering the necessary trigger scaffold, while LLM-
  only generation is brittle because it struggles with concrete-value discovery and runtime nondeterminism. We design
  SyzHarness, a framework that combines LLM reasoning with coverage-guided fuzzing for patch-based Linux kernel
  vulnerability reproduction. Given a patch, SyzHarness uses an LLM agent grounded by code navigation tools to
  synthesize a parameterized fuzzing harness that fixes the prerequisite setup logic while exposing only uncertain, bug-
  critical input parameters to be mutated by Syzkaller. SyzHarness then translates this harness into a Syzkaller-
  compatible interface and iteratively refines it using hierarchical reachability feedback. We evaluate SyzHarness on
  multiple datasets of triggerable real-world Linux kernel vulnerabilities. On 100 KernelCTF cases, SyzHarness achieves
  a 78% bug reproduction success rate. On the SyzDirect benchmark, SyzHarness achieves a 73% bug reproduction success
  rate, substantially outperforming prior directed greybox fuzzing. On 50 recent, known-triggerable syzbot bugs fixed
  after March 2026, SyzHarness reproduces 40/50 (80%) using only the fix commits as input.

</details>


### [229] [Connecting the Dots in Agentic AI Security: A Cross-Dimensional Threat Taxonomy, Evaluation Maturity, and Open Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23894)
*Heewon Baek,Alsharif Abuadbba,Kristen Moore,Hyoungshick Kim,Surya Nepal*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文通过结构化综述66项研究（2022–2026），提出跨维度表示T={S, B, P, A}，将智能体AI的受影响面、信任边界、被破坏安全属性与实证架构相连接，并评估22项红队研究与11项安全基准的覆盖度与成熟度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI将LLM安全从生成内容扩展到持久状态、自主行动、工具使用及人机/多智能体交互，但现有威胁分类多聚焦单一维度，难以揭示入口、受影响组件与安全后果之间的联系，且已知威胁图景与实证研究覆盖不一致。

Method: 对2022–2026年发表的66项研究进行结构化综述；提出跨维度表示T={S, B, P, A}，分别对应功能/系统面、交互或信任边界、被违反的安全属性、实证考察的架构；并分析22项有工件支撑的红队研究与11项代表性安全基准。

Result: 实证证据集中于提示/推理、记忆与工具中介攻击，且以单智能体场景为主；持久性、人–智能体、复杂多智能体、系统性及长时程威胁覆盖较少。异构指标、自适应防御评估有限、架构不均衡与执行状态捕获不完整，进一步限制可比性与可复现性。作者强调这些发现仅描述所选语料，而非确立全部实证研究的空白。

Conclusion: 作者据此提出13个开放研究问题，以推动更系统、架构感知且可复现的智能体AI安全评估。

Abstract: Agentic AI extends LLM security beyond generated content to persistent state, autonomous actions, tool use, and interactions with humans and other agents. Existing threat classifications often emphasize individual dimensions, obscuring connections among entry points, affected components, and security consequences. The known threat landscape also differs from the coverage demonstrated by empirical research. Through a structured review of 66 studies published from 2022 to 2026, we introduce T={S, B, P, A}, a cross-dimensional representation linking affected functional or system surfaces {S}, interaction or trust boundaries {B}, violated security properties {P}, and empirically examined architectures {A}. We analyze 22 artifact-backed red-teaming studies and 11 representative security benchmarks to characterize empirical coverage and evaluation maturity. Within the selected studies, evidence concentrates on prompt/reasoning, memory, and tool-mediated attacks, predominantly in single-agent settings. Persistent, Human--Agent, complex multi-agent, systemic, and long-horizon threats receive less coverage. These findings describe the selected corpus rather than establish gaps across all empirical research. Heterogeneous metrics, limited adaptive defense evaluation, architectural imbalance, and incomplete execution-state capture further constrain comparison and reproducibility. We derive 13 open research questions to guide more systematic, architecture-aware, and reproducible security evaluation of agentic AI.

</details>


### [230] [Benchmarking Post-Quantum Cryptography in Lightweight Virtualization Environments on Embedded Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23902)
*Nikolai Puch,Chi Hieu Ta,Moritz Beckel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文测量了后量子密码原语在嵌入式ARM硬件上于原生、Docker容器与Unikraft unikernel三种执行环境中的性能，发现容器开销可忽略，unikernel开销依算法而定，且虚拟化成本与密码学成本呈反比关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后量子密码正在部署，嵌入式系统也日益采用轻量级虚拟化来实现工作负载隔离与安全，但这两大趋势的交互对性能的影响尚不清晰。作者希望填补这一空白，理解虚拟化环境如何影响PQC原语在嵌入式硬件上的表现。

Method: 在嵌入式级ARM硬件上，采用共享软件栈，比较原生执行、Docker容器和QEMU下的Unikraft unikernel三种执行环境。对5个签名族和5个密钥封装族进行基准测试，并各选两个经典算法作为对比。使用不同参数集，总计约70种配置，测量执行时间、内存和每次操作的能耗。此外还评估了TLS 1.3密码套件组合对应用的影响。

Result: 容器对原语计算的开销可忽略；unikernel开销取决于算法：大多数PQC族开销可忽略，BIKE、HQC和MAYO出现中等开销（1.28-1.53倍），Falcon签名开销最大（17.8-19.2倍）。各环境中每次操作的能耗与执行时间紧密相关。TLS握手方面，容器与unikernel客户端每次握手需要更多时间和能耗，但当昂贵的后量子算法主导握手时，三种环境趋于一致。此时算法选择对每次握手能耗的影响高达三个数量级，远超环境因素。

Conclusion: 虚拟化成本与密码学成本呈反比关系：对于计算廉价、标准化的算法，环境选择最为重要；而对于昂贵方案，算法选择本身主导性能表现。

Abstract: Post-Quantum Cryptography (PQC) is being deployed while embedded systems increasingly adopt lightweight virtualization for workload isolation and security. Both trends change performance characteristics, yet their interaction is not well understood. To address this, we present a measurement study of PQC primitives on embedded-class ARM hardware under three execution environments with a shared software stack: native execution, a Docker container, and a Unikraft unikernel running under QEMU. We benchmark five signature and five key encapsulation mechanism families, and, for comparison, two classical algorithms each. We evaluate them using different parameter sets for a total of around 70 configurations, measuring execution time, memory, and energy per operation. To better gauge the impact on applications, we evaluated TLS 1.3 cipher combinations. We find that container overhead is negligible for primitive computation, whereas unikernel overhead depends on the algorithm. For most PQC families the overhead is negligible. A moderate overhead (1.28-1.53) arises in BIKE, HQC, and MAYO, and, above all, in Falcon signing (17.8-19.2). Per-operation energy closely tracks execution time in all environments. For TLS handshakes, container and unikernel clients need more time and energy per handshake, while all three environments converge once expensive post-quantum algorithms dominate the handshake. In these cases algorithm choice affects per-handshake energy by up to three orders of magnitude, far outweighing the environment. Overall, virtualization cost is inversely related to cryptographic cost: environment choice matters most for computationally cheap, standardized algorithms, while for expensive schemes, algorithm choice alone dominates performance.

</details>


### [231] [MobileCybench: Evaluating Agent Vulnerability Discovery via Executable Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23980)
*Andy K. Zhang,Ava Huang,Joey Ji,Wai Han,Thomas Qin,Nardos Demilew,Michael Tian-Yue Liu,Brian Song,Riya Dulepet,Brian Wang,Kyleen Liao,Cuiyuanxiu Chen,Nishka Kacheria,Andrew Wu,Pratham Rangwala,Xinjie Wang,Laura Gomezjurado Gonzalez,Anita Ding,Benjamin Yi,Daniel E. Ho,Dan Boneh,Dawn Song,Ion Stoica,Percy Liang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出基于探针（probe）的漏洞报告评估框架，并实例化为 MobileCybench：13 个 Android 应用、495 个探针，评测 5 个编码智能体在 4 种攻击设置下的漏洞发现能力，最高触发率 53.8%，并发现 23 个此前未报告的漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 智能体报告漏洞的速度已超过维护者审核速度，且报告往往依赖应用特定的安全属性、需要大量人力处理。传统评估依赖已知漏洞，难以检测探针编写时尚未知的漏洞。

Method: 以"探针"作为可执行的安全属性检查：将报告的利用过程在应用上重放并运行探针，探针被触发即表明利用成功且指明了被违反的安全属性；因探针编码的是安全属性而非已知漏洞，故可发现未知漏洞。实例化为 MobileCybench 基准，覆盖 13 个 Android 应用、495 个经作者编写与评审的探针。评测 5 个编码智能体（OpenCode 搭配 GPT-5.5、GPT-5.6-Sol、GLM-5.2；Claude Code 搭配 Opus 4.8、Opus 5），在 4 种设置下：恶意应用（受害者设备上）或低权限账户远程攻击者，各自仅提供混淆 APK 或可访问源码。

Result: 仅提供混淆 APK 时，最佳智能体 OpenCode+GPT-5.6-Sol 在恶意应用设置下对 53.8% 的应用触发探针，远程攻击者设置下为 16.7%。提供源码时，全部智能体、两种攻击设置的平均触发率由 28.8% 提升至 32.8%。构建与运行该基准共发现 23 个此前未报告的漏洞，多数已被维护者确认。

Conclusion: 基于探针的评估框架可自动化、可扩展地验证漏洞报告，并支持发现未知漏洞；MobileCybench 验证了当前 AI 智能体在 Android 漏洞发现上已具备可观能力，且源码访问能显著提升表现。

Abstract: AI agents now report vulnerabilities faster than maintainers can review them. Reports often depend on security properties specific to the application, and require considerable human labor to process. To mitigate this, we introduce a framework for evaluating vulnerability reports via probes, executable checks of security properties. A reported exploit is evaluated by replaying it against the application and running the probes: a triggered probe indicates both that the exploit succeeded and which security property it violated. As a probe encodes a security property rather than a known vulnerability, it can detect vulnerabilities that were not known when the probe was written. We instantiate the framework as MobileCybench, a benchmark for vulnerability discovery by AI agents in 13 Android applications, with 495 probes written and reviewed by the authors. We evaluate 5 coding agents (OpenCode with GPT-5.5, GPT-5.6-Sol, and GLM-5.2; Claude Code with Opus 4.8 and Opus 5) under 4 settings: as a malicious app on the victim's device or as a remote attacker with a low-privilege account, each with either only an obfuscated APK or access to the application's source code. Given only the obfuscated APK, the top agent, OpenCode with GPT-5.6-Sol, triggers probes in 53.8% of applications in the malicious-app setting and 16.7% in the remote-attacker setting. With source code, the trigger rate across all agents and both attack settings increases from 28.8% to 32.8%. Building and running the benchmark surfaced 23 previously unreported vulnerabilities, the majority of which have been confirmed by maintainers.

</details>


### [232] [LeaseGuard: Incumbent-Preserving Admission Control for Privileged LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24077)
*Junru Zhu,Yixin Yang,Xiaoqing Ding,Ruoyu Qi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LeaseGuard 是一种确定性准入层，通过规范化资源租约、在任健康检查与效果感知准入，防止特权语言模型智能体非法抢占依赖同一资源的健康在任任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特权语言模型智能体在完成新系统任务时，可能因执行权限而抢占当前依赖相同文件、进程、套接字、锁或容量分配的健康在任任务。问题根源在于：执行特权只决定操作能否运行，并不决定请求方是否有权抢占当前资源持有者。

Method: 提出 LeaseGuard 确定性准入层，在适配器执行前通过规范化资源租约、在任健康检查、效果感知准入、共存限制、安全替代方案以及资源范围覆盖来表示抢占权限。在一个包含 60 个新编写冲突场景及匹配对照的冻结基准上，跨两个本地模型家族进行评估。

Result: 与保留提示相比，LeaseGuard 将未授权抢占从 73.3% 降至 0.0%，安全完成率提升 70.0 个百分点（场景聚类 95% CI [60.8, 79.2]）；请求任务成功率变化为 -3.3 个百分点（95% CI [-9.2, 2.5]）。完整评估的 v0.2 broker 在哈希链接压力审计中还能拒绝伪造的在任任务身份。仅依赖过期的回收机制在错过续期后仍可能暴露健康在任任务。

Conclusion: 证据支持在效果被完全中介、任务所有权经过认证、租约过期能反映在任存活状态时，采用保留在任任务的准入策略。

Abstract: Privileged language-model agents can satisfy a new system task by displacing a healthy incumbent that depends on the same file, process, socket, lock, or capacity allocation. This failure arises because execution privilege determines whether an operation can run, not whether the requester may preempt the current resource owner. We present LeaseGuard, a deterministic admission layer that represents preemption authority through canonical resource leases, incumbent-health checks, effect-aware admission, coexistence limits, safe alternatives, and resource-scoped overrides before adapter execution. We evaluate it on a frozen benchmark of 60 newly authored conflict scenarios with matched controls across two local model families. Relative to a preservation prompt, LeaseGuard reduces unauthorized preemption from 73.3% to 0.0% and increases safe completion by 70.0 percentage points (scenario-clustered 95% CI [60.8, 79.2]). Requested-task success changes by -3.3 points (95% CI [-9.2, 2.5]). The fully evaluated v0.2 broker also rejects a forged incumbent task identity in a hash-linked stress audit. Expiry-only reclamation can still expose a healthy incumbent after missed renewal. The evidence supports incumbent-preserving admission when effects are completely mediated, task ownership is authenticated, and lease expiry reflects incumbent liveness.

</details>


### [233] [From Bits to Beliefs: Recoverable Semantic Fingerprints for Black-Box Verification of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24084)
*Jiaxin Hong,Yuxin Peng,Hongyao Yu,Hao Fang,Shuoyang Sun,Bin Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出SimPrint，一种面向黑盒LLM的所有权验证语义指纹框架，将所有者签名编码到语义指纹域中并通过自然二进制问答探针分布证据，实现可恢复、抗扰动、保持效用的验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源权重LLM可被复制、修改并部署在黑盒API后，发布后所有权验证困难。现有黑盒指纹依赖秘密查询-密钥对来复现预定义响应，容易被微调、剪枝、量化、模型合并以及服务时提示变化破坏。

Method: SimPrint将私有所有者签名编码进编码化语义指纹域，把所有权证据分散到自然二进制问答探针中。它仅通过低干扰批量更新植入偏离基线的探针，以保持原模型行为，随后利用纠错恢复机制将可疑模型的响应解析为可靠比特或擦除，从而恢复签名。验证仅依赖输入输出查询，因此在权重或激活不可访问时仍适用。

Result: 在三个开源权重LLM上的实验表明，SimPrint在干净和修改设置下均能可靠恢复所有者签名，在微调、剪枝、量化、模型合并和服务时扰动下保持稳健，并维持可比的下游效用。

Conclusion: SimPrint提供了一种可恢复的语义指纹方案，用于黑盒LLM所有权验证，兼具鲁棒性与实用性。

Abstract: Open-weight large language models (LLMs) can be copied, modified, and redeployed behind black-box APIs, making post-release ownership verification difficult. Existing black-box fingerprints often rely on secret query-key pairs that reproduce predefined responses, and can therefore be easily disrupted by fine-tuning, pruning, quantization, model merging, and serving-time prompt changes. We propose SimPrint, a recoverable semantic fingerprinting framework for black-box LLM ownership verification. Rather than relying on isolated exact matches, SimPrint encodes a private owner signature into a coded semantic fingerprint domain, distributing ownership evidence across natural binary question-answering probes. It implants only base-deviating probes through a low-interference batch update that preserves the original model behavior, and later recovers the signature by parsing suspect-model responses into reliable bits or erasures with an error-correcting recovery mechanism. Because verification only uses input-output queries, SimPrint remains applicable when model weights or activations are inaccessible. Experiments on three open-weight LLMs show that SimPrint reliably recovers the owner signature in both clean and modified settings, remains robust under fine-tuning, pruning, quantization, model merging, and serving-time perturbations, and maintains comparable downstream utility.

</details>


### [234] [Monet: Measuring the Ecosystem of Open-Source Text-to-Image Models Tailored for Harmful Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24134)
*Zihao Wang,Jiacen Xu,Zilong Lin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对有害文生图模型（Monet）生态进行系统性测量，构建十类有害服务分类体系，在八个主流模型平台识别出23,947个Monet，揭示了其跨平台传播、治理规避、商业化及下游部署的完整生态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源文生图生态虽促进模型开发与共享，但也托管了有意为有害服务定制的模型（Monet）。以往研究仅关注单一平台上的特定类型有害模型，但Monet并非孤立存在。其涵盖模型特征、跨平台传播、治理规避、商业变现与下游部署的完整生态仍缺乏理解，亟需系统性研究。

Method: 基于真实模型平台的政策，构建包含十类有害服务的分类体系；在八个主流文生图模型平台上识别并测量Monet，分析其跨平台镜像传播、治理规避手段、商业活动及下游部署渠道。

Result: 在八个平台上识别出23,947个Monet，最热门者下载量超1900万次；40.76%被跨平台镜像，11.99%在原平台被封禁后仍可经跨平台存档访问；发现关键词混淆、绕过模型级防护等规避策略；识别出协调商业活动（一个含668个模型、914笔已完成委托；另一个宣传灰产账号养殖服务）；并通过GitHub项目与推理API触达用户，引发下游儿童安全问题。

Conclusion: 研究暴露了平台孤立防御的局限性，强调需要跨平台威胁情报、协同治理与技术防护措施来应对Monet生态。

Abstract: The open-source text-to-image (T2I) ecosystem enables rapid model development and sharing, but also hosts models intentionally tailored for harmful services, which we call Monets. Prior work has examined specific types of harmful T2I models on individual platforms, but a Monet does not exist in isolation. The broader Monet ecosystem, spanning model characteristics, cross-platform propagation, governance evasion, monetization, and downstream deployment, remains poorly understood.
  In this study, we present the first systematic, ecosystem-level measurement of Monets. Grounded in the policies of real-world model hubs, we construct a taxonomy of ten harmful service categories and identify 23,947 Monets across eight major T2I model hubs, with the most popular exceeding 19 million downloads. While some developers employ anti-theft mechanisms against unauthorized re-uploading, Monets propagate across platforms at scale, with 40.76% mirrored across hubs. Such propagation further enables governance evasion via cross-platform archiving, keeping 11.99% of Monets accessible after bans on their original platforms, alongside other evasion strategies including keyword obfuscation and model-level safeguard circumvention. Monets also anchor coordinated commercial campaigns---one spanning 668 models with 914 completed commissions and another advertising gray-market account-farming service---and reach users through GitHub projects and inference APIs, raising downstream child safety concerns. These findings expose the limitations of platform-siloed defenses and highlight the need for cross-platform threat intelligence, coordinated governance, and technical safeguards.

</details>


### [235] [KEVGraph: Exploitation-Aware Dependency Vulnerability Remediation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24164)
*Daniel Okumu Omondi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: KEVGraph 将依赖漏洞修复建模为 KEV 感知的集合覆盖问题，通过 ILP 或贪婪算法生成最小基数升级计划，优先消除已被实际利用的漏洞，在 npm、Maven 和 PyPI 语料库上显著优于 CVSS-first 和 Dependabot 式排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 依赖扫描工具虽能报告数百个漏洞，却缺乏以"是否被实际利用"为依据的排序，实践者只能凭 CVSS 严重度决定升级顺序，而这与真实利用情况结构性错位。论文指出在 npm 语料库中，186 个非 KEV 漏洞的 CVSS 分数高于 8，全部排在三个 CISA KEV 列表包之前，导致 CVSS-first 工具将首个活跃利用漏洞的修复推迟了 17 个升级动作。

Method: KEVGraph 是一个八阶段流水线，将修复问题框定为 KEV 感知的集合覆盖问题：从 lockfile 构建每个仓库的依赖图，与 OSV 和 CISA KEV 目录进行连接，并通过精确整数线性规划（ILP）或 KEV 感知贪婪算法生成最小基数的升级计划，使活跃利用漏洞尽可能早地被消除。

Result: 在 924 个真实 npm 仓库（1,046 个漏洞，5 个 KEV 列表）上评估，ILP 规划器达到 AUCCKEV = 0.997，随机基线均值为 0.663（95% CI [0.519, 0.831]，n = 30），在第 1 步即解决首个 KEV 漏洞，仅需 417 个升级动作，比随机均值 495.4 少 15.9%。CVSS-first 和 Dependabot 式排序被严格支配：它们将首个 KEV 修复推迟到第 18 步，同时需要更多动作（分别为 419 和 421）。框架具有泛化性：Maven（1,200 仓库）达到 AUCCKEV = 0.988，随机均值 0.486；PyPI（300 仓库）达到 AUCCKEV = 1.000。

Conclusion: KEVGraph 通过 KEV 感知的集合覆盖优化，实现了对活跃利用漏洞的早期消除，并在多生态系统中一致优于 CVSS-first 和 Dependabot 式排序。每个计划附带机器可验证的证书，支持在 CISA BOD 22-01 下进行自动化合规验证。

Abstract: Dependency scanning tools surface hundreds of vulnerabilities but provide no exploitation-aware ordering, leaving practitioners to decide which upgrades to perform first with no principled guidance. The dominant practice, ordering by CVSS severity, is structurally misaligned with active exploitation: in our npm corpus, 186 non-KEV vulnerabilities carry CVSS scores greater than 8, all outranking three CISA Known Exploited Vulnerability (KEV)-listed packages and causing CVSS-first tools to defer the first actively exploited fix by 17 upgrade actions. KEVGraph is an eight-stage pipeline that frames remediation as a KEV-aware set-cover problem: it constructs per-repository dependency graphs from lockfiles, joins them against OSV and the CISA KEV catalogue, and produces a minimum-cardinality upgrade plan ordered to eliminate actively exploited vulnerabilities as early as possible via exact Integer Linear Programming (ILP) or a KEV-aware greedy algorithm. Evaluated on 924 real-world npm repositories (1,046 vulnerabilities, 5 KEV-listed), the ILP planner achieves AUCCKEV = 0.997 versus a random-baseline mean of 0.663 (95 percent CI [0.519, 0.831], n = 30), resolves the first KEV vulnerability at plan step 1, and requires only 417 upgrade actions, 15.9 percent fewer than the random mean of 495.4. CVSS-first and Dependabot-style ordering are strictly dominated: they defer the first KEV fix to step 18 while requiring more actions (419 and 421, respectively). The framework generalises: Maven (1,200 repos) achieves AUCCKEV = 0.988 versus random mean 0.486; PyPI (300 repos) achieves AUCCKEV = 1.000. Each plan is accompanied by a machine-verifiable certificate enabling automated compliance verification under CISA BOD 22-01.

</details>


### [236] [Forgeable Confirmation in Automated Computer Security Testing: Deterministic Rules versus AI Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24200)
*Akihisha Fujiyama,Niwase Shamim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出并形式化了一个"可审计攻击面":AI安全测试中,确认机制若读取攻击者可控数据则可被伪造。作者证明可伪造性完全由"决策是否读取攻击者可控数据"预测,并在前瞻性实验中精确验证,最后提出将决定性证据移至攻击者无法写入的信道可将攻击成功率从97%降至0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI被越来越多地用于自动化计算机安全测试,工具需自行判断攻击是否成功。确定性规则确认的发现被当作事实,而LLM判定的可利用性被当作意见。作者质疑:被测系统能否伪造这种确认?即确认机制本身是否可被攻击者操纵,从而产生虚假的成功判定,这会威胁AI安全智能体及以字符串匹配评分成功与否的基准测试的可信度。

Method: 作者离线测试了一个四阶段的AI辅助流水线,系统考察其15种确认机制,提出"可伪造性完全由决策是否读取攻击者可控数据所预测"这一假设,并将其形式化为一个可审计的攻击面。随后进行前瞻性检验:对16个留出机制,在任何攻击前固定预测,区分可伪造与不可伪造机制;并在公共扫描器模板的12,203个机制中验证预测准确率。还对比了确定性规则与八种开放权重LLM评判器的伪造成本。

Result: 15种确认机制中9种可伪造;可伪造性完全由是否读取攻击者可控数据预测。16个留出机制上预测与实测精确吻合,12,203个公共模板机制上预测准确率99.9%。确定性规则比八种开放权重LLM评判器更易伪造,在2%的攻击者可控响应内容下即失败,而后者中位数为50%。没有一个检查实现同时兼具鲁棒性与精确性;在规则与AI评判器之间做路由会将伪造率提升至99%。将决定性证据移至攻击者无法写入的信道,攻击成功率从97%降至0%,且escalate裁决可回收由此损失的敏感度。当被扫描主机本身即为对手时,该防护失效。

Conclusion: 安全测试中确认机制的可伪造性可由"是否读取攻击者可控数据"完全预测,这是一个可审计的攻击面。由于规则与LLM评判器均存在漏洞,且二者路由反而更易被伪造,作者建议将决定性证据置于攻击者无法写入的信道,并辅以escalate裁决以补偿敏感度。该结果对AI安全智能体和以字符串匹配评分的基准测试具有重要启示,但当被测主机本身为对手时防护失效。

Abstract: AI is increasingly used to automate computer security testing, and the tools must decide for themselves whether an attack succeeded. A finding that a deterministic rule confirms by observation is reported as fact, whereas one that an LLM judges exploitable is treated as an opinion. We ask whether the system under test can forge that confirmation.
