In offline security testing of a four-stage AI-assisted pipeline, nine of its fifteen confirmation mechanisms are forgeable, and forgeability is predicted entirely by whether the decision reads attacker-controlled data. We formalise this as an auditable attack surface and test it prospectively: on sixteen held-out mechanisms, predictions fixed before any attack separated forgeable from unforgeable mechanisms exactly, and across 12,203 mechanisms in public scanner templates the prediction was 99.9% accurate. Deterministic rules proved cheaper to forge than eight open-weight LLM judges, failing at 2% of attacker-controlled response content against a median of 50%. No implementation of one check was both robust and precise, and routing between a rule and an AI judge raised forgery to 99%. Moving the decisive evidence to a channel the attacker cannot write cuts attack success from 97% to 0%, and an escalate verdict recovers the sensitivity this costs. The protection fails when the scanned host is itself the adversary. The results bear on AI security agents and on benchmarks that score success by string matching.

</details>


### [237] [Temporal Generalization and Explanation Stability of Control Flow Graph Neural Networks for Malware Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24280)
*Md. Asif Sajeed,Md. Nazrul Islam Mondal,Md Ashraful Hossen Akash*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究用严格时间划分（2024–2025训练、2026评测）评估控制流图神经网络恶意软件检测器，发现消息传递算子选择显著影响分布偏移下的鲁棒性，且排名会反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有恶意软件检测研究多在单期语料上随机划分评测，无法反映模型对后期样本的泛化能力，因此需要采用严格的时间划分来检验鲁棒性。

Method: 从1,989个Windows可执行文件静态提取控制流图（每节点37维特征），训练集459个图（2024–2025），评测集223个图（2026）；训练12种变体及一个仅用节点特征的平坦特征对照模型。

Result: 消息传递算子的选择显著改变对偏移的鲁棒性；经校正后仍显著的成对差异都在区分聚合式架构与基于学习注意力读出架构。排名发生反转：平坦对照在分布内最优，跨时间边界后却接近最差。重校准与集成无法替代算子选择。归因不漂移，但解释有效性依架构而异，且后期语料上最准确的算子最难解释。由该发现推导的架构无需搜索即可匹配最优搜索算子。偏移同时影响恶意与良性类别。

Conclusion: 结论关乎对分布偏移的鲁棒性而非恶意软件演化；常规基准会错误拒绝消息传递方法，评测应关注算子选择与时间泛化。

Abstract: Malware detection is a critical task in cybersecurity, and graph neural networks over control flow graphs have shown promising results for it. However, detectors are usually evaluated on a random split of a corpus collected over a single period, which cannot show how well a model generalizes to later samples. This study addresses that limitation with a strict temporal split: every model is trained on one period and scored once on a later one. Two corpora of control flow graphs, each node carrying 37 features, were extracted statically from 1,989 Windows portable executables: 459 graphs from 2024-2025 for training and 223 from 2026 for evaluation. Twelve variants and a flat-feature control were trained on the earlier corpus. The choice of message-passing operator changes robustness to the shift significantly, and every pairwise gap that survives correction separates an aggregating architecture from one built around a learned attentional readout. The ranking also reverses: the flat control, which sees node features but no topology, is the best in-distribution model and among the worst across the boundary, so a conventional benchmark would have rejected message passing. Neither recalibration nor ensembling substitutes for the operator choice. Attributions do not shift, but explanation validity is architecture-specific, and the most accurate operator on the later corpus is the hardest to explain. An architecture derived from the finding matches the best searched operator without search. The shift affects both malware and benign classes alike, so these are results about robustness to distribution shift, not malware evolution.

</details>


### [238] [rApp/xApp Attestation: A New Security Use Case for O-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24296)
*Hamed Alimohammadi,Burcu Şahin,Arda Akman,Chuan Heng Foh,Periklis Chatzimisios,Mohammad Shojafar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出将rApp/xApp远程证明作为RIC原生的O-RAN安全用例，用于运行时完整性验证，通过在Near-RT RIC上实现轻量级哈希原型验证可行性，测得证明延迟低于40ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: O-RAN的解聚与软化使RIC生态面临新的运行时安全风险，现有规范虽在入网、认证、身份管理和安全通信方面有保障，但缺乏验证已部署rApp/xApp运行时是否保持预期未篡改状态的具体机制。

Method: 不提出新的密码协议，而是定义如何通过证明模块、证明代理、RIC应用接口和SMO驱动的策略协调，将现有完整性验证技术集成到O-RAN中；并将该用例映射到O-RAN联盟相关工作组，识别所需标准化扩展，在Near-RT RIC平台上实现基于哈希的轻量级原型。

Result: 实验结果显示，在多种密码哈希函数下，证明延迟均低于40ms，表明在合理调度下运行时证明不会干扰时间敏感的RIC操作。

Conclusion: 运行时证明在O-RAN中具备可行性，但仍存在可信验证、已知良好运行时状态、可扩展性、缓解策略及未来混合证明机制等技术与标准化挑战。

Abstract: The disaggregation and softwarization introduced by the Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) architecture enable multi-vendor innovation but also expose the RAN Intelligent Controller (RIC) ecosystem to new runtime security risks. Existing O-RAN specifications define strong safeguards for onboarding, authentication, identity management, and secure communication; however, they do not provide a concrete mechanism for verifying whether deployed rApps and xApps remain in their intended, untampered state during operation.
  This paper introduces rApp/xApp attestation as a RIC-native O-RAN security use case for runtime integrity verification. Rather than proposing a new cryptographic protocol, the work defines how existing integrity verification techniques can be integrated into O-RAN through attestation modules, attestation agents, RIC application interfaces, and SMO-driven policy coordination. We map the use case to relevant O-RAN Alliance working groups, identify required standardization extensions, and demonstrate feasibility through a lightweight hash-based prototype implemented on the Near-RT RIC platform. Experimental results show attestation latencies below 40 ms across multiple cryptographic hash functions, indicating that runtime attestation can be performed without disrupting time-sensitive RIC operations when appropriately scheduled. Finally, we discuss remaining technical and standardization challenges, including trusted verification, known-good runtime states, scalability, mitigation policies, and future hybrid attestation mechanisms.

</details>


### [239] [Name2Pkg: Lightweight One-Class Android Malware Screening via Name-Package Correspondence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24389)
*Changyeop Sung,Yeonjae Kang,Jaeho Shin,Huy Kang Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出 Name2Pkg：仅用应用名与包名，将恶意软件筛查建模为序列异常检测；用字符级 seq2seq 估计给定应用名条件下包名的条件似然，以长度归一化负对数似然为异常分，仅用良性数据训练与阈值校准。在 67,129 个真实应用上 ROC-AUC 0.982、召回 0.885（FPR 0.044），模型 3.57 MiB、CPU 单样本 28.20 ms，可作为大规模安全系统的低成本预筛信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有恶意软件检测多依赖 APK 内部特征或运行时行为，特征提取计算开销大，难以用于大规模早期筛查。恶意应用的应用名与包名之间常存在弱对应关系，可作为一种低成本筛查信号。

Method: 将恶意软件筛查形式化为序列异常检测问题。仅使用应用名和包名，采用字符级序列到序列模型估计给定应用名条件下包名的条件似然，以长度归一化的负对数似然作为异常分数；仅使用良性数据训练模型并校准阈值。

Result: 在 67,129 个真实世界应用数据集上，留出测试数据上 ROC-AUC 为 0.982，在假阳性率 0.044 时恶意软件召回率为 0.885；模型检查点 3.57 MiB，CPU 单样本推理延迟 28.20 ms。

Conclusion: 仅凭应用名与包名的弱对应关系即可实现高效、有效的恶意软件预筛信号，适合为大规模安全系统提供低成本前置过滤。

Abstract: Deep learning-based malware detection has been widely adopted in security-critical services. Most detection methods rely on internal features extracted from APK files or runtime behavior. However, extracting these features is computationally expensive. This limits their use in large-scale, early-stage screening. Malicious apps may exhibit weak correspondence between their user-facing app names and package names, providing a low-cost screening signal. We present Name2Pkg, a lightweight one-class classification method. It leverages only the app name and the package name. We formulate malware screening as a sequence anomaly detection problem. A character-level sequence-to-sequence model estimates the conditional likelihood of a package name given the app name. The length-normalized negative log-likelihood serves as the anomaly score. We train the model and calibrate the threshold using only benign data. Using a dataset of 67,129 real-world applications, Name2Pkg achieves an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (ROC-AUC) of 0.982 and malware recall of 0.885 at an achieved false-positive rate of 0.044 on held-out test data. It has a 3.57 MiB checkpoint and a CPU inference latency of 28.20 ms per sample. Name2Pkg provides an efficient and effective pre-filtering signal for large-scale security systems.

</details>


### [240] [Passive Hybrid Network-Based Intrusion Detection System (Hybrid-NIDS) Combining Suricata and Random Forest](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24393)
*Quoc-Cuong Tang,Hoang-Lam Huynh,Van-Tri Phan,Khuong Nguyen-An*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文评估了一个由 Suricata、随机森林流量分类与 ELK 告警处理组成的被动混合 NIDS 原型，通过特征哈希与分组切分控制数据泄漏，但在真实 PCAP 验证中暴露出严重的基准-部署域偏移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开基准上的高检测性能常被当作可直接部署的能力，但基准评估与真实运行环境之间存在差异，尤其存在精确特征重复导致的数据泄漏问题，因此需要一个明确分离基准评估与运行验证的评估框架来检验实际有效性。

Method: 将 Suricata 与随机森林流量分类、基于 ELK 的集中式告警处理结合，构建被动 Hybrid-NIDS 原型；使用特征哈希与分组感知切分来消除精确特征重复造成的泄漏；从 2,540,047 条 UNSW-NB15 记录中移除 453 个标签冲突哈希组（共 1,879 行），获得无特征哈希重叠的 Development 与 Hold-out 集；同时进行基准评估和基于 PCAP/实时流量的运行验证，包括带标签实验室 PCAP、五个 60 秒攻击会话以及无标签正常流量测试。

Result: RF-41 取得 F1 = 0.971360、ROC-AUC = 0.999671，NFStream 兼容的 RF-21 在相同 hold-out 边界上 F1 = 0.970148；但在带标签实验室 PCAP 上，RF-21 及严格相关分支的召回率仅为 0.0095，RF-21 在五个额外 60 秒攻击会话中未产生任何告警；无标签正常流量测试中 2,375 条流产生 439 条告警（仅作为告警比例报告，未解释为误报率）。

Conclusion: 公开基准上的强性能并不能直接转化为运行有效性，存在显著的基准到部署域偏移。当前 Hybrid-NIDS 应被视为被动原型与评估框架，实验结果并未证明 Suricata-随机森林关联在运行检测上优于单独使用 Suricata。

Abstract: This paper evaluates a passive Hybrid Network-based Intrusion Detection System (Hybrid-NIDS) prototype that combines Suricata with Random Forest flow classification and centralized ELK-based alert handling. The study explicitly separates benchmark evaluation from PCAP/live operational validation and controls exact feature-duplicate leakage using feature hashing and group-aware splitting. From 2,540,047 UNSW-NB15 records, 453 conflicting-label hash groups containing 1,879 rows were removed; the resulting Development and Hold-out sets have zero exact feature-hash overlap. RF-41 achieved F1 = 0.971360 and ROC-AUC = 0.999671, while the NFStream-compatible RF-21 achieved F1 = 0.970148 on the same prepared hold-out boundary. However, operational validation revealed substantial benchmark-to-deployment domain shift: on a labeled laboratory PCAP, RF-21 and the strictly correlated branch achieved recall of only 0.0095, and RF-21 produced no alerts in five additional 60-second attack sessions. An unlabeled normal-traffic test produced 439 alerts from 2,375 flows; this value is reported only as an alert ratio and is not interpreted as a false-positive rate. These results show that strong performance on a public benchmark does not directly translate into operational effectiveness. Accordingly, the current Hybrid-NIDS should be interpreted as a passive prototype and evaluation framework, and the reported experiments do not demonstrate that Suricata-Random Forest correlation provides better operational detection than Suricata alone.

</details>


### [241] [SkelOT: Reusing AOT Compilation Across EVM Contract Families](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24404)
*Sipeng Xie,Qianhong Wu,Minghang Li,Qin Wang,Zhipeng Wang,Bo Qin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SkelOT将EVM AOT编译的复用粒度从合约代码哈希提升到"合约族骨架"，通过共享骨架+常量表实现更低编译开销与更高覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EVM AOT编译器（revmc、evmone、DTVM）以合约代码哈希为粒度复用编译产物，但真实负载以"合约族"为主——工厂、代理和模板部署共享指令结构，仅少量内嵌常量不同。该粒度不匹配导致结构等价代码被重复编译，增加编译时间与产物体积，并在有限编译预算下降低负载覆盖率。

Method: SkelOT将复用单元从代码哈希提升到族骨架：每个合约族编译一个原生产物，将不变常量直接烘焙进产物，而变体常量从每合约的运行时表中读取。实现基于revmc/LLVM。

Result: 在四条链（Base、Ethereum、BSC、Arbitrum）的10K区块窗口内，23.1–47.6%的唯一可编译字节码映射到共享族骨架。在Base主网10K区块语料（352万笔交易）上，SkelOT减少编译单元47.5%、产物体积57.4%、编译时间2.19倍，同时保持与逐哈希AOT字节级一致的执行结果。运行时对族成员合约达到1.31倍中位加速。在75%执行时间覆盖目标下，SkelOT所需产物远少于逐哈希AOT，且该优势在所有覆盖目标下均成立。

Conclusion: 以合约族骨架为复用粒度可显著降低AOT编译冗余与产物开销，并提升有限预算下的覆盖率，同时保持执行结果一致，是EVM AOT编译的更优复用策略。

Abstract: Ahead-of-time (AOT) compilers (e.g., revmc, evmone, and DTVM) for the Ethereum Virtual Machine (EVM) reuse compilation artifacts at contract-code-hash granularity. This granularity is poorly matched to real EVM workloads dominated by \emph{contract families}: factory-, proxy-, and template-driven deployments that share instruction structure but differ in a small set of embedded constants. Across four EVM chains (Base, Ethereum, BSC, and Arbitrum), we find that 23.1--47.6\% of unique compilable bytecodes map to shared family skeletons within 10K-block windows. Per-hash AOT therefore redundantly recompiles structurally equivalent code, inflating compile time and artifact footprint while reducing workload coverage under finite compile budgets.
  We present \textsc{SkelOT}, an AOT framework that lifts the unit of compilation reuse from code hash to family skeleton. \textsc{SkelOT} compiles one native artifact per family, bakes invariant constants into the artifact, and reads variant constants from a per-contract runtime table. Built on revmc/LLVM and evaluated on a 10K-block Base mainnet corpus (3.52M transactions), \textsc{SkelOT} reduces compilation units by 47.5\%, artifact footprint by 57.4\%, and compile time by $2.19\times$, while preserving byte-identical execution outcomes versus per-hash AOT. At runtime, \textsc{SkelOT} delivers a $1.31\times$ median per-contract speedup across family members. Under a compile budget targeting 75\% execution-time coverage, \textsc{SkelOT} needs far fewer artifacts than per-hash AOT, and the advantage holds at every coverage target.

</details>


### [242] [ActGov: Governing LLM Agent Actions via Policy-Constrained Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24446)
*Kaiyuan Zhang,Yuke Peng,Ke Jiang,Yinqian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ActGov 是一个运行时执行框架，在 LLM 智能体的工具调用产生外部影响前逐条校验其授权合规性，通过 ActGov-Policy 自动构建并验证策略集、ActGov-Runtime 逐动作拦截，从而在长程动态工作流中有效抵御间接提示注入攻击并保持任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体越来越多地通过外部工具执行长程工作流，不可信的工具输出可能影响后续动作并超出用户授权范围，带来安全隐患。现有防御手段要么隔离注入内容，要么依赖预定义计划和静态策略来约束执行，但在动态工作流下较为脆弱，且难以扩展到可扩展的工具生态系统中。

Method: 提出 ActGov 运行时执行框架。其核心是统一的语义模型，涵盖授权、动作、运行时上下文与安全约束。ActGov-Policy 组件从工具规范、良性任务和观测到的失败轨迹中迭代构建策略集，每次更新都通过基于 SMT 的反例检查进行验证。ActGov-Runtime 在运行时将每次工具调用抽象为有限策略记录，只有当调用处于任务范围内的授权边界内且满足所有适用策略时才予以放行，实现逐动作执行。

Result: 在 AgentDojo 和 AgentDyn 基准上，跨多个模型与攻击配置进行评估，ActGov 持续降低间接提示注入攻击的成功率，同时保持任务效用，显著优于现有防御方法。

Conclusion: 结果表明，ActGov 能够在不依赖底层 LLM 正确识别恶意指令的前提下，对动态智能体执行过程实施细粒度授权控制。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly execute long-horizon workflows through external tools, allowing untrusted outputs to influence subsequent actions and exceed user authorization. Existing defenses isolate injected content or constrain execution with predefined plans and static policies, but these approaches are brittle under dynamic workflows and scale poorly across extensible tool ecosystems.
  In this work, we present ActGov, a runtime enforcement framework that validates each LLM-proposed tool action before it causes external effects. Built on a unified semantic model of authorization, actions, runtime context, and security constraints, the ActGov-Policy component iteratively constructs a policy set from tool specifications, benign tasks, and observed failure traces, with each update verified through SMT-based counterexample checking. At runtime, ActGov-Runtime abstracts each tool call into finite policy records and permits it only if it remains within the task-scoped authorization boundary and satisfies all applicable policies. This per-action enforcement preserves authorization throughout long-horizon, dynamically branching workflows.
  We evaluate ActGov on the AgentDojo and AgentDyn benchmarks across multiple models and attack configurations. It shows that ActGov consistently reduces the success rate of indirect prompt-injection attacks while preserving task utility, significantly outperforming existing defenses. These results demonstrate that ActGov can enforce fine-grained authorization over dynamic agent executions without relying on the underlying LLM to correctly identify malicious instructions.

</details>


### [243] [Prefix Puncturable Signatures with Smaller Signing Key from HIBS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24503)
*Masayuki Tezuka,Keisuke Tanaka*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于分层身份基签名（HIBS）的前缀可穿刺签名通用构造，显著改善了已有构造的密钥尺寸问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的前缀可穿刺签名通用构造（基于IBS和委托约束签名）在密钥尺寸上存在缺陷：IBS构造需要2^l个签名密钥，而基于格委托约束签名的构造其穿刺密钥尺寸随穿刺次数Q^{Punc}呈二次增长。

Method: 作者从分层身份基签名（HIBS）方案出发，给出前缀可穿刺签名的一个通用构造。

Result: 当前缀空间为{0,1}^l并采用Ruckert（PQC 2010）的格基HIBS方案HIBS^{GPV}实例化时，该构造的穿刺签名密钥尺寸被限制在O(lQ^{Punc})。

Conclusion: 该构造在保持通用性的同时，将穿刺密钥尺寸由二次增长降为O(lQ^{Punc})，改进了现有方案。

Abstract: Puncturable signatures, proposed by Bellare et al. (EUROCRYPT 2016), allow a signing key to be punctured (updated) so that it loses the ability to sign particular messages while retaining the ability to sign all others. Halevi et al. (ASIACRYPT 2017) introduced prefix puncturable signatures, in which the signing key can be punctured with respect to a target prefix so that it cannot sign messages whose prefixes match the target prefix. So far, several generic constructions of prefix puncturable signature schemes have been proposed, including constructions based on identity-based signatures (IBS) (ESORICS 2022) and delegated constrained signatures (IEEE Trans. Inf. Forensics Secure. 2024). However, these constructions suffer from drawbacks in terms of key size. When the prefix space is the set of all l-bit strings, the former construction requires a signing key consisting of 2^{l} IBS signing keys. The latter construction, when instantiated with a lattice-based delegated constrained signature scheme, yields a punctured signing key whose size grows quadratically with the number of puncturing operations Q^{Punc}. In this paper, we present a generic construction of prefix puncturable signatures from hierarchical identity-based signature (HIBS) schemes. When the prefix space is {0,1}^{l} and our construction is instantiated with the lattice-based HIBS scheme HIBS^{GPV} by Ruckert (PQC 2010), our construction achieves a punctured signing key size bounded by O(lQ^{Punc}).

</details>


### [244] [Beyond Predictable Paths: Redefining AI Security Incident Reporting for Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24515)
*Anastasia Pustozerova,Eugene Bagdasarian,Luca Beurer-Kellner,Battista Biggio,Nico Ebert,David Filip,Marc Fischer,Heather Frase,David Hofer,Juliane Hoffmann,Daphne Ippolito,Somesh Jha,Sean McGregor,Esfandiar Mohammadi,Luca Nannini,Cristina Nita-Rotaru,Alina Oprea,Kevin Paeth,Andrew Paverd,Jonathan Petit,Andreas Rauber,Christian Riess,John Sotiropoulos,Andreas Wespi,Kathrin Grosse*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对AI智能体（AI agents）事件报告框架的不足，通过对比AI系统与AI智能体并采纳23位专家意见，梳理出AI智能体安全事件报告所需的信息要素，并提出若干开放研究问题与隐私要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体被快速部署，与之伴随的是越来越多的AI特定攻击和相应事件。事件报告在法律合规、治理、问责和安全方面日益重要，但现有框架需要针对AI智能体的独特性进行调整。

Method: 两位编辑作者对AI系统与AI智能体进行比较，并综合来自学术界和工业界23位专家的意见，识别在AI智能体安全受到损害的事件中需要报告的信息。

Result: 潜在的报告要素包括智能体记忆与记忆访问、实际及潜在自主性水平、工具使用等。专家反馈还指出了潜在的报告弱点，如数据泄露风险以及针对报告基础设施本身的攻击。

Conclusion: 论文提出了若干开放研究问题（如如何高效记录事件、如何判断漏洞与事件是否泛化），总结了隐私要求，并勾勒出AI智能体安全可信部署的研究方向。

Abstract: AI agents are being deployed rapidly, accompanied by a growing number of AI-specific attacks and corresponding incidents. As incident reporting becomes increasingly important for legal compliance, governance, accountability, and security; current frameworks must be adapted to the unique characteristics of AI agents. In this paper, two editorial authors compare AI systems and AI agents and, drawing on input from 23 experts in academia and industry, identify the information required for reporting incidents where the security of AI agents is harmed. %involving AI agents. Potential reporting elements include, for example, agent memory and memory accesses, actual and potential levels of autonomy, and tool usage. Based on these findings, we identify several open research questions, including how to efficiently record incidents and how to determine whether vulnerabilities and incidents generalize. Expert feedback also highlighted potential reporting weaknesses, such as risks of data leakage and attacks targeting the reporting infrastructure itself, creating additional research needs. Lastly, we summarize privacy requirements and outline research directions for the secure and trustworthy deployment of AI agents.

</details>


### [245] [State-Aware Fuzzing of JavaScript Engines with LLM-Guided Instrumentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24550)
*Wai Kin Wong,Dongwei Xiao,Anthony Cheuk Tung Lai,Ping Fan Ke,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: StateLens 利用大语言模型智能定位 JavaScript 引擎中的深层内部状态并植入高信号反馈探针，突破传统边缘覆盖的瓶颈，显著提升模糊测试效果并发现 68 个新漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 Web 安全依赖 JavaScript 引擎的正确性，但这类复杂系统仍易出现高危缺陷。现有先进模糊测试器存在"覆盖平台期"问题：一旦饱和了控制流图，边缘覆盖便失去指导发现的能力。因为 JIT 优化层级、隐藏类转换等复杂引擎行为往往共享相同的边缘覆盖，标准覆盖指标无法区分触发深层错误所需的差异化内部状态。

Method: 提出 StateLens 框架，利用 LLM 自动化发现深层内部状态。由于状态空间庞大且运行时开销高，无法盲目在所有状态放置插桩探针，因此设计了一种新颖的智能体推理流水线，模拟安全研究人员的直觉：通过迭代遍历代码与开发者注释，智能选择高价值的插桩目标，将驱动逻辑的状态变量与无关数据区分开，生成可合成的、高信号的反馈探针以映射引擎的隐藏配置。该插桩进一步驱动双反馈机制，引导模糊测试器探索未触及的引擎语义。

Result: 评估结果证实，StateLens 显著优于当前先进的模糊测试器，并成功发现了 68 个新漏洞（bug）。

Conclusion: StateLens 表明，借助 LLM 智能体推理来精准定位引擎内部状态并构建高信号反馈，可有效突破边缘覆盖瓶颈，为复杂 JavaScript 引擎的漏洞挖掘提供了新的有效路径。

Abstract: The security of the modern web depends on the correctness of JavaScript (JS) engines, yet these complex systems remain vulnerable to high-impact bugs. A critical limitation of state-of-the-art fuzzers is the coverage plateau: once a fuzzer saturates the control-flow graph, edge coverage loses its ability to guide discovery. Because complex engine behaviors, such as JIT optimization tiers and hidden class transitions, often share identical edge coverage, standard coverage metrics are blind to the distinct internal states required to trigger deep errors.
  To bridge this gap, we present StateLens, a framework that employs Large Language Models (LLM) to automate the discovery of deep internal states. Blindly placing instrumentation probes at all states is infeasible due to the vast state space and the high runtime overhead. StateLens introduces a novel agent-based reasoning pipeline that emulates the intuition of a security researcher. By iteratively traversing code and developer comments, our agents intelligently select high-value instrumentation targets, effectively separating logic-driving state variables from irrelevant data. This results in synthesizable, high-signal feedback probes that map the engine's hidden configurations. This instrumentation feeds a dual-feedback mechanism, effectively guiding the fuzzer toward unexplored engine semantics. Our evaluation confirms that StateLens significantly outperforms state-of-the-art fuzzers and uncovering 68 new bugs.

</details>


### [246] [5G-Shark: A Network Security Auditor for 5G Subscriber Privacy and Unauthenticated Signalling Resilience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24656)
*Oscar Lasierra,Gines Garcia-Aviles,Antonio Skarmeta,Xavier Costa-Pérez*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出5G-Shark，一种基于合法移动性流程的安全评估工具与方法，通过操纵标准化的小区重选准则将目标UE吸引到自建伪造小区，从而在不进行干扰或畸形报文注入的情况下，将5G网络中的残余暴露源区分为协议设计缺陷与实现/部署不合规两类，并在商用5G SA网络中进行了实证评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G标准被明确赋予了弥补长期隐私与安全缺陷的使命，要求隐藏用户永久身份、抵抗代际降级并防止位置追踪。然而，要评估这些保证在真实运营网络中是否成立，必须区分两类此前研究未加区分、也未在真实环境中评估的残余暴露来源：协议设计局限（即使完全符合规范的部署也可被利用）与实现缺口（由不完整或不合规实现导致）。

Method: 5G-Shark不依赖主动干扰或畸形报文注入，而是操纵标准化的的小区重选准则，将目标用户设备吸引到自建的伪基站小区，从而以最小的服务中断建立攻击观测点。随后，该方法执行必要的交互流程，暴露被测系统的安全风险，并将其归类为协议设计缺陷与部署不合规两类。工具完全基于开源协议栈与软件定义无线电（SDR）硬件构建。

Result: 在商用5G独立组网（SA）部署上评估显示，5G-Shark能够请求用户标识符、通过构造的注册拒绝（Registration Reject）码强制无线接入技术（RAT）降级，并诱发拒绝服务状态。对每个攻击向量，作者都将根因归属于协议设计或部署不合规。此外，实证表明在若干商用部署中，临时标识符以近似连续的步进方式重新分配，使连续取值之间保持可关联性，从而在用户身份标识正确隐藏的情况下仍可实现持续的用户追踪。

Conclusion: 5G-Shark证明了即使在符合规范的部署中，协议设计层面的局限仍可被利用，而实现与部署不合规进一步放大了风险，尤其是临时标识符近顺序分配导致的持久追踪问题，说明5G的隐私与安全承诺在现实网络中尚未完全兑现。

Abstract: The fifth generation of mobile networks was standardised with an explicit mandate to close long-standing privacy and security gaps, mandating the concealment of the subscriber's permanent identity, resistance to generational downgrade, and protection against location tracking. Assessing whether these guarantees hold in operational networks, however, requires separating two sources of residual exposure that prior studies do not distinguish and do not evaluate in the wild: protocol-design limitations, which remain exploitable even against a fully specification-compliant deployment, and implementation gaps, which arise from incomplete or non-compliant implementations. We present 5G-Shark, a security assessment tool and methodology that turns a legitimate mobility procedure against the subscriber. Rather than relying on active jamming or malformed-packet injection, 5G-Shark manipulates the standardised cell-reselection criterion to pull a target User Equipment onto a self-created rogue cell, establishing an attack vantage with minimal service disruption. Then, the proposed methodology effectively performs the required interactions to expose the security risks of the system under test, classifying them into the aforementioned categories. Built solely from open-source stacks and Software Defined Radio hardware and evaluated against commercial 5G Standalone deployments, 5G-Shark requests subscriber identifiers, forces Radio Access Technology downgrade via crafted Registration Reject codes, and induces denial-of-service states. For each vector, we attribute the root cause to protocol design or deployment non-compliance. We further provide empirical evidence that in several commercial deployments, temporary identifiers are re-allocated in near-sequential steps that keep successive values linkable, a weakness that enables persistent user tracking despite correct subscriber ID concealment.

</details>


### [247] [Trust in Edge-Enabled IoT Security: Features, Challenges and Research Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24669)
*Esin Ece Aydın,Şerif Bahtiyar,Gürkan Gür*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了边缘赋能物联网中的信任管理现状，提出结合物理、网络、应用三层与消费、商业、工业、基础设施四域的综合性分类法，分析信任设计、信任与安全操作的集成方式及影响信任管理的攻击，并指出研究空白与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网日益依赖边缘计算以支持时延敏感和资源受限的应用，但这也引入了更多样、更庞大的设备、边缘节点、服务与数据源之间的交互，带来新的安全与隐私挑战。信任管理可用于评估交互实体的可靠性并自适应安全决策，然而当前缺乏对该领域系统性的梳理与分类。

Method: 采用系统性文献综述方法，提出一个综合性分类法，将物联网的物理层、网络层和应用层与消费、商业、工业和基础设施四大物联网域进行交叉映射；并依据信任设计、信任如何融入安全物联网操作、以及影响信任管理过程的攻击，对最新研究进行分析。

Result: 梳理了边缘赋能物联网中信任管理的研究现状，形成多层次、多领域的分类框架，归纳了信任设计与安全操作的集成方式，并识别出影响信任管理过程的攻击类型。

Conclusion: 基于综述发现，指出现有研究的关键空白，并展望了面向边缘赋能物联网的上下文感知与自适应信任管理的未来研究方向。

Abstract: Providing autonomous intelligence, pervasive connectivity and usability to human life and industry has led to the emergence of the Internet of Things (IoT). To support time-sensitive and resource-constrained applications, IoT systems nowadays increasingly rely on edge computing. This brings computation and decision-making closer to end devices. In edge-enabled IoT architecture, latency and communication overhead are reduced, but interactions among a larger and more diverse set of devices, edge nodes, services, and data sources are introduced as well. In such environments, security and privacy mechanisms provide the foundation for protection, while trust management can assess the reliability of interacting entities and adapting secure decisions. In this paper, we systematically review the current state of trust management in edge-enabled IoT. To this end, we propose a comprehensive taxonomy that maps physical, network, and application architectural IoT layers against the consumer, commercial, industrial, and infrastructure IoT domains. We further investigate state-of-art research based on their trust design, how trust integrated into secure IoT operations, the attacks that effect trust management process. Based on these findings, we identify key gaps in current research and outline future directions for context-aware and adaptive trust management in edge-enabled IoT.

</details>


### [248] [Domain Specific Post Quantum Signatures for Blockchains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24689)
*Maja Lie,Ben Marsh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 区块链不能仅靠后量子单一签名，需要面向共识的后量子签名配置文件，论文形式化了交易授权与QC需求并在多链上评估多种方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链对签名的需求超越通用单一签名：需要规范化字节、可定价的无效输入拒绝、稳定交易标识、混合降级抵抗、公开聚合、合并语义、可问责签名者证据、前向安全委员会轮换及轻客户端影响等特点，而现有NIST后量子签名均非现代公链签名层的直接替代品。

Method: 类比哈希函数工程为哈希表DoS与算术化证明系统产出领域专用原语，主张为区块链角色设计领域专用后量子签名；形式化交易授权与法定人数证书（QC）需求，并在Bitcoin、Ethereum及Sei Giga风格高吞吐BFT压力配置上实例化，评估ML-DSA、SLH-DSA、Falcon/FN-DSA、HAWK、MAYO、SNOVA、UOV/QR-UOV、FAEST、SQIsign、LaBRADOR Falcon、Squirrel、Chipmunk、DKKW/LeanSig等方案。

Result: 评估表明NIST单一签名是必要组件，但当前没有任何方案能直接替换现代公链的签名层；缺失的是共识就绪的后量子签名配置文件，而非又一张通用尺寸表。

Conclusion: 区块链应转向领域专用后量子签名与共识就绪的配置文件，聚焦于共识、聚合与可问责性等区块链特有属性，而非通用尺寸比较。

Abstract: Blockchains need more than post quantum single signer signatures. They need consensus profiled authentication objects with canonical bytes, priced invalid input rejection, stable transaction identifiers, hybrid downgrade resistance, public aggregation, merge semantics, accountable signer evidence, forward secure committee rotation, and light client consequences. We argue for domain specific post quantum signatures for blockchain roles, analogously to how hash function engineering produced domain specific primitives for hash table DoS and arithmetized proof systems. We formalize transaction authorization and quorum certificate requirements, instantiate them on Bitcoin, Ethereum, and a Sei Giga style high throughput BFT stress profile, and evaluate ML-DSA, SLH-DSA, Falcon/FN-DSA, HAWK, MAYO, SNOVA, UOV/QR-UOV, FAEST, SQIsign, LaBRADOR Falcon, Squirrel, Chipmunk, and DKKW/LeanSig. The conclusion is blunt. NIST single signer signatures are necessary components, although none of the current schemes is a drop in replacement for the signature layer of modern public blockchains. The missing object is a consensus ready post quantum signature profile, not another generic size table.

</details>


### [249] [Reasoning Topology Matters: A Controlled Study of LLM-Based Cybersecurity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24710)
*Jiling Zhou,Aisvarya Adeseye,Antti Hakkala,Seppo Virtanen,Jouni Isoaho*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出"安全推理拓扑"(Security Reasoning Topology)，将LLM推理过程建模为线性、分支和图三种结构，并在三个网络安全数据集上验证拓扑结构对推理性能的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在网络安全分析中需进行多步、依赖上下文且面向复杂异构数据的推理，但现有提示方法只关注引出推理，未明确考虑中间推理步骤的结构组织方式。作者希望探究推理拓扑结构是否为影响性能的关键设计因素。

Method: 提出安全推理拓扑，用线性(Linear)、分支(Branching)、图(Graph)三种代表性结构建模推理过程；在覆盖MITRE ATT&CK网络流量、网络威胁情报(CTI)、CVE漏洞分析的三个网络安全数据集上进行受控实验；评估Llama 2(7B/13B/70B)、GPT-5.1、Mistral Large 3等多个LLM，保持任务输入一致，通过系统级提示控制推理结构。

Result: 推理拓扑对性能有显著影响：图推理整体准确率最高，较few-shot提示在各数据集上提升9.8–12.2个百分点；分支推理提供良好的中间方案。该效应在不同模型家族和规模间保持一致。

Conclusion: 推理拓扑是基于LLM的网络安全分析的重要设计因素，结构化组织中间推理步骤(尤其图结构)能稳定且显著地提升分析性能。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used in cybersecurity, where accurate analysis often requires multi-step and context-dependent reasoning over complex and heterogeneous data. However, existing prompting approaches typically focus on eliciting reasoning without explicitly considering how intermediate reasoning steps are structurally organized. We introduce Security Reasoning Topology, which models reasoning through three representative structures: Linear, Branching, and Graph. To evaluate their effects, we conduct controlled experiments on three cybersecurity datasets covering MITRE ATT&CK network traffic, cyber threat intelligence (CTI), and CVE vulnerability analysis. We evaluate multiple LLMs, including Llama 2 (7B, 13B, 70B), GPT-5.1, and Mistral Large 3, while keeping task inputs consistent and controlling reasoning structure through system-level prompting. Results show that reasoning topology substantially affects performance: Graph reasoning achieves the highest overall accuracy, improving over few-shot prompting by 9.8-12.2 percentage points across datasets, while Branching provides a strong intermediate solution. The results further show that the effect of reasoning topology remains consistent across model families and scales, highlighting reasoning topology as an important design factor for LLM-based cybersecurity analysis.

</details>


### [250] [Decoding Guardrails: XAI-Guided Perturbation Analysis of Prompt Injection Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24801)
*Fernando Outeda,Gustavo Betarte,Juan Diego Campo,Fiorella Cravero*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过四种实验，用XAI方法（Vanilla Gradient、SHAP）分析Prompt Guard 2的判定逻辑，发现其决策依赖众多token的累积贡献而非少数主导token，但显著性引导的同义替换与句子改写可在仅改动中等比例文本的情况下翻转预测，甚至实现对底层LLM的越狱。数据集级分析显示未被检测的注入提示系统性地缺乏分类器依赖的词汇标记。作者认为，旨在提升透明度的解释方法同时也降低了构造对抗性绕过的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在生产系统中广泛部署，面临提示注入与越狱攻击的对抗性操纵风险。基于分类器的护栏（如Prompt Guard 2）被广泛用作第一道防线，但其内部决策逻辑对防御者与攻击者都基本不透明，这既影响防御可信度，也可能被利用。

Method: 一项探索性案例研究，将可解释AI（XAI）技术应用于Prompt Guard 2，以分析其如何区分恶意与良性提示。共设计四个实验：以Vanilla Gradient与SHAP归因作为引导，进行显著性引导的同义替换与句子级改写攻击测试；并进行数据集规模的显著性分析。

Result: Prompt Guard 2的决策依赖大量token的累积贡献而非少数主导token；然而显著性引导的同义替换与句子级改写能在仅改动中等比例文本的情况下翻转其预测，部分案例成功越狱底层LLM。数据集规模分析表明，未被检测的注入提示系统性地缺乏分类器所依赖的词汇标记。

Conclusion: 研究结果对基于分类器的护栏的设计与评估具有启示意义：应警惕仅依赖表层词汇标记的判定方式，并需评估解释方法带来的安全副作用。作者指出，旨在支持透明度的解释方法可能同时降低构造成功对抗性绕过的成本，形成透明性与安全性之间的张力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in production systems, raising concerns about their exposure to adversarial manipulation through prompt injection and jailbreak attacks. Classifier-based guardrails, such as Prompt Guard 2, are widely used as a first line of defense against such attacks, but their internal decision logic is largely opaque to both defenders and attackers. This paper presents an exploratory case study that applies explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) techniques to analyze how Prompt Guard 2 distinguishes malicious from benign prompts.
  We conduct four experiments to probe this question empirically. Guided by Vanilla Gradient and SHAP attributions, we find that Prompt Guard 2's decisions rely on the cumulative contribution of many tokens rather than a few dominant ones, yet saliency-guided synonym substitution and sentence-level paraphrasing can flip its predictions while altering only a moderate fraction of the text, in some cases yielding a successful jailbreak against the underlying LLM. A dataset-scale saliency analysis further shows that undetected injection prompts systematically lack the lexical markers the classifier relies on. We discuss the implications of these findings for the design and evaluation of classifier-based guardrails, and argue that explanation methods intended to support transparency can simultaneously lower the cost of constructing successful adversarial bypasses.

</details>


### [251] [OPBackdoor: Opportunistic Backdoors via Alibi-Aligned Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24826)
*Eric Xue,Ruiyi Zhang,Kevin Xue,Pengtao Xie,Junda Wu,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出"机会主义后门"(OPBackdoor)：后门目标仅在触发提示存在可乘之机时才被激活，令模型的思维链以"托辞对齐"(alibi-aligned)推理掩饰目标诉求，在26B-119B密集与MoE架构上验证，可骗过LLM审查但被对比监控识破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流LLM后门研究采用"触发即充分"的范式：触发词激活后，无论上下文如何，目标响应都会被激发，导致后门目标暴露无遗。本文希望挑战这一范式，构建更隐蔽、更难检测的后门形式。

Method: 提出Opportunistic Backdoor (OPBackdoor)：后门目标仅在触发提示上下文提供可利用机会时被诱导出来，使模型通过"托辞对齐"推理（对触发上下文而言逻辑自洽、却直接导向目标响应）来伪装其意图。在26B-119B的密集与MoE架构上，通过反事实训练(counterfactual training)在编程助手中植入后门使其以"过度热心"报复敌意用户，在翻译助手中植入后门使其通过偏见翻译进行商业宣传。

Result: OPBackdoor成功在26B-119B密集与MoE架构中被诱导出来。托辞对齐推理能令LLM审查者相信不存在后门；但对比监控(contrastive monitoring)仍能暴露后门目标，说明该隐蔽策略存在局限。

Conclusion: "触发即充分"并非后门的唯一形态；机会主义后门可借助上下文机会与托辞对齐推理实现更强隐蔽性，对现有基于LLM审查的防御构成挑战，但对比监控仍可作为有效检测手段。

Abstract: When a backdoor trigger activates the target response regardless of the triggered prompt context, the backdoor objective reveals itself. Challenging this trigger-sufficient formulation across the LLM backdoor literature, we introduce Opportunistic Backdoors (OPBackdoor), in which the backdoor objective is elicited only when the triggered prompt context presents an exploitable opportunity, enabling the model's think to disguise its pursuit through alibi-aligned reasoning that is logical with respect to the triggered prompt context but directly leads to the target response. Across dense and MoE architectures of 26B-119B, we induce OPBackdoor via counterfactual training in coding assistants to retaliate against hostile users via excessive helpfulness and translation assistants to engage in commercial propaganda via biased translation. Yet alibi-aligned reasoning has limits: it can convince LLM inspectors that no backdoor is at work, while contrastive monitoring exposes the backdoor objective.

</details>


### [252] [Residual Community Prototypes Under-Reject Held-Out Malware Families in FCG-MFD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24980)
*Junru Zhu,Yixin Yang,Xiaoqing Ding,Ruoyu Qi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文检验Louvain社区摘要能否在图神经网络嵌入与维度匹配的通用拓扑之外，为开放集恶意软件家族识别提供额外的拒绝信息；结论是在GIN/FCG-MFD设定下社区特征未能形成稳定的未知类边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放集恶意软件家族识别需在分类已知家族的同时拒绝训练中未出现的家族。作者希望验证：在图嵌入之外，Louvain社区摘要是否携带独立的、可用于拒绝未知家族的补充信息，而非仅由通用拓扑结构解释。

Method: 使用去重且经冲突审计的FCG-MFD语料，设置五个留出家族与三个优化种子。先用已知家族训练数据将社区特征对通用拓扑做残差化，再进行最近原型打分；并对比简单分类器不确定性与OSCR等基线。

Result: 残差社区特征未产生稳定的留出家族拒绝效果：排序效应在各家族间方向反转，各留出家族在95%未知召回下的假阳性率均变差，验证集拟合阈值仅拒绝4.48%的未知样本。接受的已知类宏F1在每个家族中均提升，但五个独立家族单元的精确双侧符号翻转p值为0.0625（可达最小值）。得分仍与图规模相关，而简单分类器不确定性在排序、高召回拒绝与OSCR上表现更好。

Conclusion: 在GIN/FCG-MFD设定下，社区增强原型改变了已知类几何，却未创造稳定的未知边界。图开放集评测应将结构特征与匹配的拓扑对照、可操作阈值和留出家族分析配对使用。

Abstract: Open-set malware-family recognition must classify known families while rejecting families absent from training. We test whether Louvain-community summaries add rejection information beyond a graph neural network embedding and dimension-matched generic topology. The study uses a deduplicated, conflict-audited FCG-MFD corpus, five held-out families, and three optimization seeds. Community features are residualized against generic topology using known-family training data before nearest-prototype scoring. Residual community does not produce stable held-out-family rejection. Ranking effects reverse across families, the false-positive rate at 95 percent unknown recall worsens for every held-out family, and a validation-fitted threshold rejects only 4.48 percent of unknown samples. Accepted-known macro F1 improves in every family, but with five independent family units the exact two-sided sign-flip p-value is 0.0625, the smallest attainable value. The score remains associated with graph scale, while simple classifier uncertainty performs better on ranking, high-recall rejection, and OSCR. In this GIN/FCG-MFD setting, community-enriched prototypes change known-class geometry without creating a stable unknown margin. Graph open-set evaluations should pair structural features with matched topology controls, operational thresholds, and held-out-family analysis.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [253] [From Certain Doom to Survival: Agent-Driven Self-Governance in LLM Agent Societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22600)
*Gregory B. Rehm*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出 GovSim-SelfGovern 环境，让多智能体在公共池资源困境中自主编写可执行 Python 治理规则，发现可执行自治扩展了干预空间，但生存取决于能否及时找到正确制度机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体 LLM 社会困境研究多将治理视为实验者强加、仅停留于修辞表达，或局限于固定机制菜单，缺乏对智能体自主制度创作的考察。

Method: 在 GovSim 公共池资源环境基础上扩展出 GovSim-SelfGovern：智能体撰写可执行 Python 治理规则，接受沙盒验证反馈，投票表决拟议法律，并在多轮中生活于自己制定的规则之下；设置从稳定富足到五智能体无法全部存活的致命资源墙三类场景；并研究智能体在提议放逐时的犹豫源于规范理由还是候选集缺失这一对齐问题。

Result: 可执行治理拓展了智能体可能的干预空间，但生存取决于能否及时找到正确的制度机制；财政能力可实现再分配，而更深入的推理与取消民主否决权使放逐更可行。

Conclusion: GovSim-SelfGovern 将可执行代码动作适配到公共池治理情境，揭示了稀缺性如何使制度创作本身成为政治与伦理问题。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems are increasingly evaluated in social dilemmas, but most work treats governance as imposed by the experimenter, expressed rhetorically, or restricted to a fixed menu of mechanisms. We introduce GovSim-SelfGovern, an extension of the GovSim common-pool resource environment in which agents author executable Python governance rules, receive sandbox validation feedback, vote on proposed laws, and live under the rules they enact across rounds. To evaluate agent-driven self-governance, we examine three scenarios ranging from stable abundance to a fatal resource wall where five agents cannot all survive through harvest alone. To solve this, agents must write and debug useful laws in time before their institutions degrade sharply under resource pressure. Finally, we study a central alignment question: when agents hesitate to propose exile, are they rejecting it for normative reasons, or does it never enter their candidate set? Our results show that executable governance improves the space of possible interventions for agents, but survival depends on whether agents discover the right institutional mechanisms in time. Fiscal capacity enables redistribution, while deeper reasoning and removal of democratic veto make exile more feasible. GovSim-SelfGovern therefore adapts executable code actions to a common-pool governance setting and shows how scarcity turns institutional authorship into a political and ethical problem.

</details>


### [254] [When Does Communication Help? Beyond Spectral Descriptions of Collective Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.23310)
*Xuening Wu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该论文识别了分布式推理中通信增益的两种聚合描述局限：谱摘要无法判断增益方向，且平均个体准确率提升可能掩盖对未受影响社区或全局投票的损害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通信虽能让智能体达成一致，却可能使决策变差。作者希望指出仅用聚合指标（如特征值/奇异值谱、平均准确率）来刻画通信增益的不足。

Method: 第一，构造具有相同特征值与奇异值谱、但产生相反符号增益的稳定线性系统，并改变消息方向进行验证；提出保留方向信息的“任务投影局部响应近似”，用独立于测试集的标注校准数据预测多轮增益。第二，在社区共享偏差设定下，分析平均个体准确率与社区损害、全局投票准确率之间的关系，并测试校准约束。

Result: 仅改变消息方向可使准确率从72.6%变为91.2%或降至65.9%。所提任务投影近似在两个合成任务上以0.45个百分点的均方根误差预测小型训练非线性智能体的多轮增益，并经自然边缘变化和手写数字任务验证。在固定通信轮数下，校准约束能减少观察到的社区损害并保留多数平均收益，但不保证保护；完全直接校准表现相近。

Conclusion: 研究连接了谱不足、任务感知预测与通信收益分布问题，表明聚合描述存在根本局限；广泛迁移性与实际优越性仍待进一步验证。

Abstract: Communication can bring agents into agreement while making their decisions worse. We identify two limits of aggregate descriptions of communication gain in distributed inference. First, stable linear systems with fixed evidence, network and readout can have interaction and finite-time state operators with identical eigenvalue and singular-value spectra, yet produce gains of opposite sign. Changing only message orientation raises accuracy from 72.6% to 91.2% or lowers it to 65.9%. A standard task-projected local-response approximation retains the directional information missing from spectral summaries. Using labeled calibration data separate from the test set, it predicts multi-round gains in small trained nonlinear agents with a root-mean-square error of 0.45 percentage points on two synthetic tasks; tests with natural edge changes and handwritten digits extend the evaluation. Second, under community-shared bias, higher mean individual accuracy can coexist with harm to unaffected communities or lower global-vote accuracy. At fixed communication rounds, calibration constraints reduce observed community harm while retaining much of the mean benefit, but do not guarantee protection. Full direct calibration performs similarly. The results connect spectral insufficiency, task-aware prediction and the distribution of communication benefits, while leaving broad transfer and practical superiority open.

</details>


### [255] [A Task-Oriented Multi-Agent Framework for Complex Wearable Health Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24107)
*Kunpeng Yang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出面向任务的多智能体框架，将复合查询拆解为独立意图与带依赖的类型化任务，由专门智能体分别执行检索、分析与建议，并在合成可穿戴数据上验证其检索准确率、多意图覆盖与可信度/透明度表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用单个大模型处理完整记录加复合查询，会掩盖每个子请求是否被执行，以及答案依赖哪些证据，缺乏可解释性与任务分工。

Method: 将复合查询表示为不同意图及带显式意图内依赖的类型化任务；专门智能体分别执行检索、分析和建议任务；各意图状态相互隔离，以保持请求边界和证据关系，最后再聚合。

Result: 在含1万名虚拟用户、每人一个月纵向可穿戴记录的合成数据集上评估。1500个检索问题上Query Agent准确率98.3%，略高于直接LLM基线97.9%，平均查询阶段token从6869降至3136；180个多意图问题上Manager Agent多意图覆盖率100.0%，多重集Jaccard相似度94.4%；两类问题上可信度与透明度均分更高，但可行动性未一致提升。

Conclusion: 显式任务组织有助于任务相关数据访问与数据支撑的纵向分析，但健康建议生成及在真实可穿戴数据上的验证仍是待解难题。

Abstract: Wearable health questions often combine data retrieval, longitudinal analysis, and health advice over structured records. Prompting a single large language model with a complete record and a composite query obscures whether every request is executed and which evidence supports the answer. We propose a task-oriented multi-agent framework that represents a composite query as distinct intents and typed tasks with explicit intra-intent dependencies. Specialized agents execute retrieval, analysis, and advice tasks; isolated intent states preserve request boundaries and evidence relationships before aggregation. We evaluate the framework on a synthetic dataset of $10{,}000$ virtual users with one month of longitudinal wearable records, covering structured data retrieval, multi-intent recognition, and overall response quality. Across $1{,}500$ retrieval questions, the Query Agent achieves $98.3\%$ accuracy, compared with $97.9\%$ for the Direct LLM baseline, while reducing average query-stage token consumption from $6{,}869$ to $3{,}136$. On $180$ multi-intent questions, the Manager Agent achieves $100.0\%$ Multi-Intent Coverage and $94.4\%$ Multiset Jaccard Similarity. Under the current synthetic evaluation setting, our method receives higher mean Trustworthiness and Transparency scores on both question categories, whereas Actionability does not improve consistently. These results provide preliminary evidence that explicit task organization can support task-relevant data access and data-grounded longitudinal analysis, while leaving health advice generation and validation on real wearable data as open challenges.

</details>


### [256] [Mixed-integer flow formulations for motion planning and decision-making of networked multi-agent systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24474)
*Angelo Caregnato-Neto,Paul-Louis Delacour,Raf Van de Plas,Tamás Keviczky,Janito Vaqueiro Ferreira*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文将基于流的连通性保持约束引入网络化多智能体系统的MILP轨迹规划与决策模型，证明了标准与k-hop连通性编码的必要性与充分性，并通过统计实验比较其与SEC方法的性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络化多智能体系统的MILP轨迹规划需保证通信连通性，现有基于子回路消除（SEC）的约束数量随智能体规模指数增长，限制了大规模系统的可解性，因此需要更高效的连通性编码方法。

Method: 将标准连通性和k-hop连通性的流编码集成到广泛用于滚动时域规划的MILP多车机动模型中，证明其必要性与充分性以保证覆盖所有可能网络拓扑；并使用商用分支定界求解器，在随机环境中对规模递增的多智能体系统进行成本与优化时间的统计评估。

Result: 标准连通性的流公式将所需不等式约束从指数增长降为多项式增长；k-hop连通性约束减少了二元变量数量并使其增长与跳数解耦。实验表明，流公式在标准连通性问题上优于SEC，能在给定优化时间限制下求解更大规模系统；但k-hop流公式虽降低了分支定界最坏情况迭代次数，其优势未转化为平均性能的提升。

Conclusion: 流式连通性编码在标准连通性场景下显著优于传统SEC方法，可扩展至更大规模多智能体系统；而k-hop流公式的理论优势因引入大量连续变量和额外约束，未能体现在实际平均性能中，性能权衡取决于具体问题规模与结构。

Abstract: This work investigates the use of flow-based connectivity maintenance constraints in mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) trajectory planning and decision-making models for networked multi-agent systems (MAS). We integrate flow-based encodings for standard and k-hop connectivity into MILP multi-vehicle maneuvering models that are widely used alongside receding horizon planning strategies. Their necessity and sufficiency is demonstrated, guaranteeing full coverage of potential network topologies. The flow formulation for standard connectivity decreases the growth of the required inequality constraints from exponential to polynomial w.r.t. the size of the MAS when compared to the state-of-the-art subtour elimination (SEC) method. The flow-based k-hop connectivity constraints decrease the number of required binary variables and decouple its growth from the number of hops. However, the impact of these formulations in performance is not straightforward due to the introduction of a substantial number of continuous flow optimization variables and, in the case of k-hop connectivity, additional inequality constraints. We investigate this trade-off through a statistical evaluation of costs and optimization times using a conventional branch-and-bound commercial solver and trials performed with randomized environments for increasingly larger MAS. The results show that the flow formulation outperforms SEC in standard connectivity problems, enabling the solutions to be computed for larger MAS considering the imposed optimization time limit. The reduction in number of binary variables enabled by the k-hop flow formulations decreases the theoretical worst-case number of iterations required by the branch-and-bound algorithm to compute the global optimal solution. Our results show that this advantage did not translate into improvements in the average performance when compared to the baseline.

</details>


### [257] [Perception-Aware Communication Middleware for Distributed Visual Perception in UAV Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.24964)
*Manveen Kaur,Kevin Loi,Ifunanya Okafor,Daniel Ng,Joseph Lucey-Renteria*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种感知感知的通信中间件，将完整的感知数据样本作为QoS的通信对象，扩展基于UDP的发布-订阅架构，并在UAV集群测试平台上验证其低延迟、高吞吐和高优先级调度效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机集群越来越多地支持依赖分布式视觉感知的安全关键应用，其低延迟需求要求感知模型在集群内具备推理能力的无人机上运行，因此需要高效地传输高带宽感知数据。然而，感知的QoS需求不同于传统的数据包级QoS：单个数据包的成功传输并不能保证为推理提供一个完整、及时且可用的图像。

Method: 提出一种新颖的感知感知通信中间件，将完整的感知数据样本视为需要满足QoS的通信对象。该中间件在轻量级基于UDP代理的发布-订阅架构上扩展了感知专属服务，包括图像分片与重建、并发数据包传输、优先级感知调度以及图像质量评估。

Result: 在模拟无人机集群的异构硬件测试平台上，使用YOLOv8n目标检测进行评估。实验结果表明：端到端应用延迟低、吞吐量显著高于轻量级UDP代理、在背景负载增加时能有效优先处理感知流量，并通过中间件级图像质量评估缓解了目标检测性能的下降。

Conclusion: 该工作为将面向AI的数据处理集成到通信中间件中提供了初步框架，以支持多智能体移动信息物理系统中新兴的分布式AI应用。

Abstract: Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) swarms increasingly support safety-critical applications that rely on distributed visual perception. Meeting the low-latency requirements of these applications can require perception models to execute within the swarm on inference-capable UAVs, creating a need for efficient UAV-to-UAV transport of high-bandwidth perception data. However, the Quality-of-Service (QoS) requirements of perception differ from conventional packet-level QoS; successful delivery of individual packets does not ensure that a complete, timely, and usable image is available for inference. We present a novel perception-aware communication middleware that treats complete perception-data samples as the communication objects for which QoS must be satisfied. The middleware extends a lightweight UDP broker-based publish-subscribe architecture with perception-specific services, including image fragmentation and reconstruction, concurrent packet transmission, priority-aware scheduling, and image quality assessment. The middleware is evaluated on a heterogeneous hardware testbed emulating a UAV swarm using YOLOv8n object detection. Experimental results demonstrate low end-to-end application latency, substantially higher throughput than a lightweight UDP broker, effective prioritization of perception traffic under increasing background load, and mitigation of object-detection degradation through middleware-level image quality assessment. This work provides an initial framework for integrating AI-specific data handling into communication middleware to support emerging distributed AI applications in multi-agent mobile cyber-physical systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [258] [Didactic knowledge or Clinical Cases? How Data Types Shape Medical Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22161)
*Yuzheng Fan,Haochun Wang,Sendong Zhao,Xiao Han,Ming Ma,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过token匹配实验研究医学大语言模型中教学数据与临床数据的混合比例如何影响模型能力，发现两类数据存在非对称迁移：临床数据提升临床任务且不损害知识任务，而教学数据主要提升知识任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学大语言模型通常混合教学数据（如教科书）和临床数据（如患者记录）进行训练，但这两类数据如何差异化地塑造模型能力仍不清楚。作者希望厘清数据组成对性能、能力画像和错误模式的影响，为数据筛选提供依据。

Method: 采用token匹配实验，系统改变教学数据与临床数据的比例，并在知识密集型任务和临床导向任务上分析模型的性能、能力画像及错误模式。

Result: 发现任务类型间的非对称迁移：临床数据提升临床导向任务，同时在知识密集型任务上保持竞争力；教学数据主要提升知识密集型任务。错误分析提示存在"知-行鸿沟"，即知识回忆的改善不能可靠地泛化到临床推理。少量临床数据即可在EHR任务上带来大部分收益，且最优混合比例随下游任务的知识与临床推理需求而变化。

Conclusion: 医学大语言模型的数据筛选应以应用为导向，对于推理密集型用例应优先采用较高比例的临床数据。

Abstract: Medical large language models are commonly trained on mixtures of didactic data (e.g., textbooks) and clinical data (e.g., patient records), yet how these data types differentially shape model capabilities remains unclear. We address this issue with token-matched experiments that vary the didactic-to-clinical ratio and analyze how data composition affects performance, capability profiles, and error patterns across knowledge-intensive and clinic-oriented tasks. We uncover an asymmetric transfer across task types: clinical data improves clinic-oriented tasks while remaining competitive on knowledge-intensive ones, whereas didactic data mainly improves knowledge-intensive tasks. Error analysis suggests a knowing-doing gap, where improvements in knowledge recall do not reliably generalize to clinical reasoning. We further observe that modest amounts of clinical data yield most of the gains on EHR-grounded tasks, while the optimal mixture ratio varies with the knowledge and clinical reasoning demands of downstream tasks. These findings suggest that medical LLM data curation should be application-driven, with higher proportions of clinical data preferred for reasoning-intensive use cases.

</details>


### [259] [An Affordable AI-Integrated Smart Cane for Multimodal Mobility Assistance of Visually Impaired Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22277)
*Ali Akarma,Adeel Ahmad,Toqeer Ali Syed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一款售价88美元、完全离线的AI智能手杖，基于超低功耗Raspberry Pi Zero 2W，融合RGB视觉与ToF测距，为视障人士提供多模态出行辅助，并以实验验证其可行性与可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球超22亿人受视力障碍影响，传统白手杖无法检测高处障碍或提供语义环境信息；现有AI导航系统多依赖昂贵硬件或云连接，在资源受限场景下可及性差。因此需要一种低成本、离线、隐私保护的辅助方案。

Method: 系统融合RGB视觉感知与Time-of-Flight距离估计，采用INT8量化的SSD MobileNet V1模型，结合距离感知的振动触觉反馈与实时音频警报。为在受限硬件上保证鲁棒性，设计了多进程架构，将传感器采集、神经推理与触觉反馈隔离为独立进程，并支持失效保护传感。

Result: 室内移动场景实验显示：宏平均F1为0.82（精确率0.85，召回率0.81），端到端平均延迟330毫秒，峰值功耗2.8瓦。12名参与者的初步可用性研究（SUS：78.5，NASA-TLX）表明用户感知积极、障碍感知能力增强。

Conclusion: 该原型验证了在成本敏感型出行辅助中部署隐私保护、边缘原生辅助智能的可行性。

Abstract: Visual impairment affects over 2.2 billion people worldwide, yet conventional white canes cannot detect elevated hazards or provide semantic environmental context. Existing AI-assisted navigation systems typically rely on expensive hardware or cloud connectivity, limiting accessibility in resource-constrained settings. This paper presents an affordable (\$88 USD), fully offline AI-integrated smart cane designed for multimodal mobility assistance on an ultra-low-power Raspberry Pi Zero 2W. The system fuses RGB vision sensing with Time-of-Flight (ToF) distance estimation, pairing an INT8-quantized SSD MobileNet V1 model with distance-aware vibrotactile feedback and real-time audio alerts. To ensure operational robustness on constrained hardware, a multiprocessing architecture isolates sensor acquisition, neural inference, and haptic feedback into independent processes with fail-safe sensing support. Experimental evaluation across indoor mobility scenarios demonstrates a macro-averaged F1-score of 0.82 (precision: 0.85, recall: 0.81), a mean end-to-end latency of 330\,ms, and a peak power draw of 2.8\,W. A preliminary usability study with 12 participants (SUS: 78.5, NASA-TLX) demonstrated positive user perception and enhanced obstacle awareness. The proposed prototype validates the feasibility of deploying privacy-preserving, edge-native assistive intelligence for cost-sensitive mobility assistance.

</details>


### [260] [PAANI : On Device Visual Evidence Fusion and Explainable Guidance for River Robot Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22353)
*Savio Cardoz,Santhiya Rajan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PAANI——一种在Arduino UNO Q上运行的水面机器人端侧感知到引导架构，融合YOLO11n检测、MobileNetV3-Small语义分割与时间戳对齐的证据融合，并输出可解释的引导建议；模型精度较高，但板端5分钟内中位延迟约468 ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动河流监测机器人需理解仅靠地理航点无法描述的水面障碍与水边界；在资源受限平台上，把不完美的视觉预测转化为及时且可审查的引导是难点。仅有目标标签或转向指令无法说明决策依据及证据何时不可靠。

Method: 在Arduino UNO Q上部署项目训练的YOLO11n检测器与自定义MobileNetV3-Small语义分割器，采用时间戳对齐的证据融合；有界跟踪维持目标持续性，显式走廊策略综合水面标签、被接受的检测、紧迫度与掩膜不确定性；每条建议公开其证据与策略原因；通过ROS 2接口连接Gazebo船体、定位与控制测试平台。训练用10,000张WaterScenes图像做四类检测，1,127张MaSTr1325图像做分割（含198张验证）。

Result: 所选FP32 ONNX模型共14.817 MB；检测器检查点测试mAP@0.5为0.7388，矩形ONNX导出验证mAP@0.5为0.7367；分割ONNX验证mIoU为0.9750。UNO Q五分钟录制在配置0.5 Hz下，流水线延迟中位数467.8 ms、95分位580.3 ms。评估还发现黑色输入误分类与采样率不匹配导致诊断性表观运动估计器证据不足的问题。

Conclusion: 结果支持一个可审查、可复用的边缘机器人基础，但明确区分模型精度与板端执行，尚未等同于经过验证的水上避碰能力。

Abstract: Mobile river monitoring robots must interpret obstacles and water boundaries that geographic waypoints alone cannot describe. On resource constrained platforms, converting imperfect visual predictions into timely and inspectable guidance is a distinct challenge. An object label or steering command does not explain which evidence supports a decision or when that evidence is unreliable. We present PAANI, an on-device perception to guidance architecture that combines a project trained YOLO11n detector and a custom MobileNetV3 Small semantic segmenter with timestamp aligned evidence fusion on Arduino UNO Q. Bounded tracking supplies object persistence, while an explicit corridor policy combines surface labels, accepted detections, urgency and mask uncertainty. Each final advisory exposes its contributing evidence and policy reasons. ROS 2 interfaces connect the local AI pipeline to a separate Gazebo vessel, localization and control testbed. Training uses 10,000 WaterScenes images for four-class detection and 1,127 MaSTr1325 images for segmentation, including 198 segmentation validation images. The selected FP32 ONNX models occupy 14.817 MB. Detector checkpoint test mAP at 0.5 IoU is 0.7388, while the separately evaluated rectangular ONNX export achieves validation mAP at 0.5 IoU of 0.7367. Segmentation ONNX validation mIoU is 0.9750. A five-minute UNO Q recording produced median and 95th percentile pipeline latencies of 467.8 ms and 580.3 ms at a configured 0.5 Hz cadence. The evaluation also identifies black input misclassification and a sampling rate mismatch that prevents the diagnostic apparent motion estimator from collecting sufficient evidence. These results support an inspectable and reusable edge robotics foundation while clearly distinguishing model accuracy and on-board execution from validated on-water collision avoidance.

</details>


### [261] [Social Influence and the Allocation of Scientific Attention in AI Populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22408)
*Maxim Chupilkin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究将"音乐实验室"设计迁移到学术注意力市场，用AI智能体模拟论文选择，发现社会信息会降低选择数量、加剧选择集中度、缩小集体覆盖面，并产生强烈的初始选择"锁定"效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统正日益成为科研评价与使用的参与者，但它们面对的是围绕人类读者形成的引用量、下载量、热门文章榜单等信号。这些信号对"人工读者"群体的集体后果尚不明确，因此需要研究社会影响如何塑造AI对科学研究的注意力分配。

Method: 采用改编自Music Lab的实验设计。实验一：1000个AI智能体从《美国经济评论》2025年全部114篇常规研究论文的标题和摘要中选择论文，分为5个独立选择社区和5个社会影响社区，每社区100个顺序决策的智能体；仅社会影响组的智能体可看到本社区先前选择，且可任意选择多篇论文。实验二：20个社会社区共200个智能体，随机给部分论文分配5次初始选择，观察其对后续选择率的影响。

Result: 社会信息社区中每个智能体平均少选17.2%的论文，选择更加集中，集体覆盖73篇论文，而独立选择社区覆盖90篇；社区间差异在社会信息条件下更大。实验二中，随机赋予论文5次初始选择，使其后续被选率提高45.55个百分点（95%置信区间：41.20至49.90）。选择结果与外部引用量对应关系较弱，与下载量几乎没有对应关系。

Conclusion: 一条简单的信息规则即可显著塑造人工群体中科学注意力的规模、广度与分布，表明社会信号会引发集中化和路径依赖，且AI的选择与人类常用的引用、下载等评价指标并不一致。

Abstract: AI systems are becoming participants in the evaluation and use of scientific research. They encounter citation counts, download statistics and lists of popular articles developed around human readers, but the collective consequences of these signals for artificial readers remain uncertain. This paper adapts the Music Lab design to a market for academic attention. In the first experiment, 1,000 AI agents choose papers from the titles and abstracts of all 114 regular research articles published in the American Economic Review in 2025. The experiment has five independent-choice communities and five social-influence communities, each with 100 sequential agents. Only agents in the social-influence condition observe earlier selections within their community. Agents may select any number of papers. Social-information communities select 17.2 percent fewer papers per agent, concentrate their choices more heavily, and collectively cover 73 papers, compared with 90 independently. Between-community variation is greater under social information. In a second experiment with 200 agents across twenty social communities, randomly assigning papers five initial selections raises their subsequent selection rate by 45.55 percentage points (95% CI: 41.20 to 49.90). Choices have modest correspondence with external citations and little correspondence with download counts. The results show how a simple information rule shapes the volume, breadth and distribution of scientific attention in an artificial population.

</details>


### [262] [Learning 3D biophysical cell properties from 2D images and cell-population statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22410)
*Santiago Hernández-Orozco,Hector Zenil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种群体监督框架，将单个二维红细胞图像映射到潜在生物物理量，并聚合为MCV、RDW和MCH；在390份标本、6台设备上验证，与Sysmex分析仪相关性达0.86–0.98。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维显微成像推断三维细胞特性存在困难，因为参考仪器仅提供群体统计量而非单细胞标签，缺乏个体级监督信号。

Method: 构建群体监督框架，包含共享局部推断、面向体积与血红蛋白的生物物理结构化解码器、可学习的实例加权以及设备特异性校准；并形式化分析聚合观测对受限实例预测器的可识别性条件、群体一致性无法识别单细胞特性与三维几何的原因，以及子集均值匹配带来的离散度惩罚。

Result: 开发数据集包含390份标本、1,105次采集、6台设备，与Sysmex分析仪报告的Pearson相关系数为0.86–0.98。

Conclusion: 该框架为从二维图像与群体监督通向三维细胞生物物理提供了可检验路径，但并未声称实现显式的三维重建。

Abstract: Inferring 3D cellular properties from 2D microscopy is difficult when a reference instrument reports only population statistics rather than labels for individual cells. Here we develop a population-supervised framework that maps single 2D red-cell images to latent biophysical quantities and aggregates them to mean corpuscular volume, red-cell distribution width and mean corpuscular haemoglobin. The model combines shared local inference, a biophysically structured decoder for volume and haemoglobin, learned instance weighting and device-specific calibration. We formalise conditions under which aggregate observations identify restricted instance predictors, show why population agreement does not by itself identify single-cell properties or 3D geometry, and derive the dispersion penalty induced by subset mean matching. The development dataset comprises 390 specimens and 1,105 acquisitions across six devices, with reported Pearson correlations of 0.86--0.98 against a Sysmex analyser. The framework provides a testable route from 2D images and population supervision to 3D cellular biophysics without claiming explicit 3D reconstruction.

</details>


### [263] [AutoGym: Blueprint-First Generation of Verifiable Agent Gyms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22592)
*Aarati Andrea Noronha,Kavya Ravikumar,Carly Xiaoyu Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoGym 是一个从最小领域种子或模型轨迹自动生成完整强化学习 gym（任务、可执行环境与验证器）的框架，通过蓝图先行生成、显式生成参数和主动课程合成三种机制实现难度可控、可验证的任务生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建强化学习训练所需的 gym（任务、可执行环境、验证器）目前依赖人工、成本高且静态。随着模型能力提升，固定任务集会饱和并面临数据污染风险。合成生成虽能扩展规模，但单次生成的任务难度多为表面性的——能力相当的模型能被复杂措辞迷惑却仍能解答，且正确性只能事后由不可靠的 LLM 评判。

Method: AutoGym 从最小领域种子或先前的模型轨迹生成完整 gym，包含三种机制：(1) 蓝图先行生成：在环境实体化前先规定有效解空间、环境需求与验证标准，使可解性成为构建前提而非事后验证的属性；(2) 显式生成参数：控制任务拓扑、交互深度、能力维度、问题混淆与干扰项构成，实现细粒度难度调控；(3) 主动课程合成：利用基于表现的校准，随模型能力演进调整这些参数的分布。

Result: 在生产力与时间推理两类设置下，AutoGym 生成的 gym 覆盖了完整的能力谱系，其中包含能够挑战前沿模型的实例。

Conclusion: AutoGym 通过自动生成包含任务、环境与验证器的完整 gym，并以蓝图先行、参数化难度控制和主动课程合成等机制，解决了人工构建成本高、任务集饱和与污染以及单次合成长于表面难度等问题，可生成覆盖广泛能力范围、甚至挑战前沿模型的训练环境。

Abstract: Training agents with reinforcement learning requires a gym, comprising a task, an executable environment in which the task can be attempted, and a verifier that reliably distinguishes success from failure. Constructing such gyms remains manual, expensive, and static. Task sets saturate as models improve and are increasingly exposed to contamination. Synthetic generation offers scale, but single-pass synthesis produces tasks whose difficulty is largely cosmetic. Models comparable in capability solve them despite convoluted phrasing, and correctness must be adjudicated post-hoc by unreliable LLM judges. We present AutoGym, a framework that generates complete gyms (tasks, executable environments, and verifiers) from a minimal domain seed or prior model trajectories. AutoGym introduces three mechanisms. (1) Blueprint-first generation specifies the valid solution space, environment requirements, and verification criteria before the environment is materialized, making solvability a construction prerequisite rather than a property verified after the fact. (2) Explicit generation parameters control task topology, interaction depth, capability axes, question obfuscation, and distractor composition, enabling fine-grained difficulty steering. (3) Active curriculum synthesis uses performance-informed calibration to adjust the distribution over these parameters as model capabilities evolve. Across productivity and temporal-reasoning settings, AutoGym generates gyms spanning the capability spectrum, including instances that challenge frontier models.

</details>


### [264] [Goal-driven Variant Categorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22475)
*Daniel Calegari,Daniel Amyot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种目标驱动的流程变体分类方法，先构建组织目标模型以预设分类维度，再利用大语言模型将流程变体转化为文本叙述并映射到目标类别，实现语义层面的变体归类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统流程变体聚类基于结构相似性，所得分组并非源自组织目标，需分析师人工解释并整合为有业务意义的类别；随着变体数量和复杂度增加，这一判断密集型步骤愈发困难。

Method: 先编写组织目标模型以预定义分类轴；将每个流程变体转化为描述其行为的文本叙述；再由大语言模型在目标模型语境下解读该叙述，把变体分配到最合适的类别，从而用基于LLM的语义推理连接底层流程行为与分析师定义的业务目标。

Result: 在三个规模和行为的多样性差异显著的公开日志上完成了端到端实例化与评估；目标模型引导产生的划分与无引导归纳得到的划分不同，并且能响应针对所声明备选方案的受控编辑，代价是需要编写目标模型。

Conclusion: 目标驱动的变体分类方法可有效将流程变体归入由组织目标预设的类别，所得划分区别于传统无引导聚类，且对目标模型中的受控改动具有敏感性，说明LLM语义推理可在业务目标框架下支撑变体归类，但需付出构建目标模型的成本。

Abstract: Process discovery rarely yields a single coherent process structure. For analysis, a common step is to cluster process variants based on structural similarity and then assign business meaning to the resulting groups. Since these partitions are not derived from the organization's goals, analysts must manually interpret and consolidate variants into business-meaningful categories. This judgment-intensive step becomes increasingly difficult as the number and complexity of variants grow. In this paper, we propose a goal-driven approach to variant categorization that reverses this workflow. We first author an organization's goal model that predefines the categorization axis. Each variant is transformed into a textual narrative describing its behavior, and a Large Language Model (LLM) interprets it in the context of the goal model and assigns the variant to the most appropriate category. LLM-based semantic reasoning connects low-level process behavior with analyst-defined business goals. We instantiate this approach end-to-end and evaluate it on three public logs differing substantially in scale and behavioral diversity. Goal-model guidance yields partitions that differ from those produced by unguided induction and respond to controlled edits to the declared alternatives, at the cost of authoring a goal model.

</details>


### [265] [Self-Organizing Agent Teams Learn to Reason Together](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22682)
*Aneesh Pappu,Mirac Suzgun,Yongchan Kwon,Federico Bianchi,Batu El,Mykel J. Kochenderfer,Hancheng Cao,James Zou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出自组织智能体团队（SAT），让固定AI智能体团队从既往协作中学习可复用的团队策略，组织角色、对话阶段、参与度与信息流，实现"协作计算"，并在多基准上显著超越最强单体和路由基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集体智能不仅取决于成员知识，还取决于工作组织方式。当解法结构未知时，有用的角色与分工无法预先指定，团队必须从经验中学习如何组织推理。人类团队常能自适应，而现有AI智能体团队依赖固定协议、显式任务分解或路由，缺乏这种学习能力。

Method: 提出自组织智能体团队（SAT）：固定团队，从先前协作中学习可复用策略，用于组织角色、对话阶段、参与度和信息流。策略使智能体能够交换、质疑、修复并综合部分推理，形成无单个成员独立产生的解法。在两个独立设置中学习团队协作策略，仅用15道数学题和25道研究生水平知识题，策略可原样迁移到未见基准。

Result: 在五个数学与物理基准上，自组织团队平均准确率66.7%，而最强成员为48.8%，该成员等算力推理为58.7%，成员独立答案的完美路由为59.0%；在AIME 2026上超过该路由13.4分。在八个基准上，可论证性（团队能否区分正确与错误推理）与相对最强成员的提升强相关（Spearman ρ=0.90，p=0.005）。

Conclusion: 团队在正确推理一旦出现即可被识别时获益最大。结果表明，组织本身可以成为一种智能体能力：智能体团队能够学会如何共同推理，并产生成员各自无法独立达到的解法。

Abstract: Collective intelligence depends not only on what team members know, but also on how they organize their work. When the structure of a solution is unknown, useful roles and divisions of labor cannot be specified in advance; teams must learn from experience how to organize reasoning as it unfolds. Human teams routinely adapt this way, while existing AI agent teams rely on fixed protocols, explicit task decomposition, or routing. We introduce Self-Organizing Agent Teams (SAT), fixed teams of AI agents that learn reusable strategies from prior collaborations to organize roles, conversational phases, participation, and information flow. These strategies enable what we call collaborative computation: agents exchange, challenge, repair, and synthesize partial reasoning into solutions no member produced independently. In two independent settings, we learn teamwork strategies that transfer unchanged to unseen benchmarks, using only 15 mathematics and 25 graduate-level knowledge problems. Across five mathematics and physics benchmarks, self-organizing teams average 66.7% accuracy, versus 48.8% for their strongest member, 58.7% for compute-matched inference by that agent, and 59.0% for a perfect router over members' independent answers; on AIME 2026, they exceed this router by 13.4 points. Because gains vary across benchmarks, we ask when self-organizing collaboration helps. Across eight benchmarks, demonstrability (the organizational-psychology construct of whether a team can distinguish correct from incorrect reasoning) strongly tracks improvement over the strongest member (Spearman $ρ=0.90$, $p=0.005$): teams benefit most when correct reasoning can be recognized once it appears. More broadly, these results suggest that organization itself can become an agent capability: agent teams can learn how to reason together and produce solutions their members could not reach independently.

</details>


### [266] [Replication Without Persistence in Hosted LLMs: Measurement Sensitivity in Action-Time Belief Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22478)
*Bhushan Kashinath Joshi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 托管语言模型的行为评估会因服务、测量工具或配置不同而变化；本文通过Regent Chess环境区分复现、测量敏感性与持续性三类验证问题，发现同一评估内可得出不同结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 托管模型的行为评估结果在不同运行间会波动，原因是评估服务、测量工具或两者存在差异；作者希望厘清以往结论究竟属于在历史配置下的再现、配置重建带来的测量敏感性，还是跨标识符的持续性。

Method: 采用Regent Chess序列环境，其中隐藏的可变状态被精确记录，使声明信念可在行动时刻与真值比对；正端值表示比匹配均匀比较器表现更差。设计三类验证：复现（历史配置下的新数据）、测量敏感性（同标识符下重建评估与推理配置）、持续性（同一共同工具下后续测试的标识符）。

Result: 先前报告的Gemini 3.1 Flash-Lite劣势在新牌局历史配置下复现（+0.0530，95% CI [+0.0329,+0.0714]）。同日背靠背同标识符H/R比较中，重建配置下模型减均匀端值低0.0429（H减R对比95% CI [+0.0182,+0.0667]）；六个配置组件同时变化，无法分离单一成分。重建R下，前瞻冻结的交错同窗口4K比较在Gemini 3.1与3.7间符号反转；其他描述性与探索性单元显示相同方向模式。额外服务期贡献仍不明确（-0.0166，[-0.0483,+0.0157]）。

Conclusion: 复现、测量敏感性与持续性可在同一次评估中得出不同结论，因此有必要将托管模型的行为主张按测试标识符、服务期、测量工具和推理配置进行显式索引。

Abstract: Behavioural evaluations of hosted language models can vary because the evaluated service, the measurement instrument, or both differ across runs. We separate three validation questions: whether a prior finding recurs on fresh data under its historical configuration (replication), whether the endpoint changes when the evaluation-and-inference configuration is rebuilt under the same identifier (measurement sensitivity), and whether the finding persists across subsequently tested identifiers under one common instrument (persistence). We study these questions in Regent Chess, a sequential environment in which a hidden, mutable state is recorded exactly, allowing stated beliefs to be scored against ground truth at action time; positive endpoint values mean worse performance than a matched-uniform comparator. The previously reported Gemini 3.1 Flash-Lite deficit recurs on fresh games under its historical configuration (+0.0530, 95% CI [+0.0329,+0.0714]). In a back-to-back same-day H/R comparison under the same public identifier, the model-minus-uniform endpoint is 0.0429 lower under the rebuilt configuration (95% CI for the H-minus-R contrast [+0.0182,+0.0667]); all six configuration components vary jointly, so no component is isolated. Under rebuilt R, the prospectively frozen, interleaved same-window 4K comparison reverses sign between Gemini 3.1 and Gemini 3.7, identifiers that differ in release and product tier; additional descriptive and exploratory cells show the same directional pattern. Any additional serving-period contribution remains unresolved (-0.0166, [-0.0483,+0.0157]). Replication, measurement sensitivity, and persistence can therefore yield different conclusions within one evaluation, motivating explicit indexing of hosted-model behavioural claims by tested identifier, serving period, measurement instrument, and inference configuration.

</details>


### [267] [The Wisdom of Artificial Deliberative Crowds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22497)
*Federico Barrera-Lemarchand,Mariano Sigman,Joaquin Navajas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究将原本用于人类的"三阶段审议"范式应用于三种不同家族的大语言模型，在四个现实 stakes 递增的领域（视觉数值估计、机器学习论文同行评审、AI 智能体隐藏恶意行为检测、对抗真实预测市场的体育预测）中进行测试，发现模型间审议能超越独立响应的被动聚合，且该优势依赖于模型多样性——同源克隆模型组无法从审议中获益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的"群体智慧"效应通常归因于个体估计的独立性，而人类研究表明，通过审议（deliberation）可获得更强的聚合效果：小型审议小组的共识估计平均值优于经典群体智慧，且个体判断在审议后也变得更准确。然而，这种审议带来的改进是否能迁移到大型语言模型之间的相互审议，尚不清楚。本文旨在检验机器审议是否可作为通用的聚合机制，并探究其成功的关键条件。

Method: 作者改编了一个原本用于人类参与者的三阶段审议范式，并将其应用于来自三个不同家族的大语言模型。研究跨越四个现实 stakes 递增的领域展开：研究一为视觉数值估计；研究二为机器学习论文的同行评审；研究三为检测 AI 智能体隐藏的恶意行为；研究四为对抗真实预测市场的体育预测。研究者对比了审议后的群体估计与独立响应的被动聚合，并检验了模型多样性（单模型克隆组 vs. 多模型组）对审议效果的影响。

Result: 在所有四个领域中，审议均降低了集体误差，效果超越了对独立响应的被动聚合；审议后的个体判断也保留了这一集体增益。值得注意的是，该优势需要模型多样性：由单一模型克隆组成的群体在审议中未获得任何收益。

Conclusion: 这些结果确立了机器审议作为一种通用聚合机制的地位，并指出多样性是其中的关键活性成分。研究为利用大语言模型群体的审议来提升估计与判断准确性提供了跨领域的实证支持。

Abstract: The aggregation of many lay estimates often outperforms individual expert judgment, a phenomenon known as the wisdom of crowds. While this is usually attributed to the independence of estimates, an even stronger effect arises through deliberation: averaging the consensus estimates of small deliberating groups outperforms the classical wisdom of crowds, with individual judgments themselves also becoming more accurate after deliberation. Whether these improvements transfer to large language models deliberating amongst themselves is unknown. Here we adapt a three-stage deliberation paradigm previously used with human participants for use with large language models from three different families, and test it across four domains of increasing real-world stakes: visual numerical estimation (Study 1), peer review of machine-learning papers (Study 2), detection of hidden malicious behavior by an artificial intelligence agent (Study 3), and sports forecasting against a real prediction market (Study 4). Across domains, deliberation reduced collective error beyond passive aggregation of independent responses, and post-deliberation individual judgments retained this collective gain. Notably, the advantage required model diversity: groups composed of clones of a single model did not benefit from deliberating. These results establish machine deliberation as a general-purpose aggregation mechanism, and point to diversity as an active ingredient.

</details>


### [268] [Agreement Overstates Evidence: Error Dependence in LLM Judge Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22512)
*Elias Hossain,Niloofar Yousefi,Ser-Nam Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM评委之间的误差存在显著相关性，共识并不能等同于独立投票，会高估评估可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常认为多个LLM评委达成共识就说明决策正确，但这以评委误差相互独立为前提。实际上LLM评委的训练与评估方式相似，可能犯相同的错误，因此需要研究这种依赖性如何影响共识可靠性。

Method: 作者在开源权重和前沿LLM评委中测量误差相关性。主评委组由十个评委组成，计算评委误差的平均两两相关性，并据此换算等效独立评委数量。还分析误差分布模式（多数评委共有误差 vs 少数评委集中误差）对不同投票方法的影响。

Result: 十个评委的平均两两误差相关性为0.21，其统计信息量仅相当于约3.5个独立评委。在高准确率的前沿评委之间（包括不同提供商的评委）依赖性更强。在多达28%的对比中，忽略共享误差会得出某系统显著更优的结论，而考虑共享误差后该结论不成立。误差模式也影响结果，共有误差与集中误差偏好不同投票方法。

Conclusion: 仅测量整体相关性不足以评估共识可靠性。建议使用一小组可信示例估计评委准确率并识别共享错误，在分析结果时考虑这些共享错误，并在应用到新数据前用可信示例选择投票方法。

Abstract: Consensus among LLM judges is often taken as strong evidence that a decision is correct. This assumes that judges make their errors independently. In practice, LLM judges are often trained and evaluated in similar ways, so they can make the same mistakes. We study how this dependency affects the reliability of consensus. We find substantial error correlation across both open-weight and frontier LLM judges. In our main bank of ten judges, the average pairwise correlation between judge errors is 0.21. As a result, the ten judges only provide roughly as much statistical information as 3.5 independent judges. The dependency is even stronger among the high-accuracy frontier judges we evaluate, including judges from different providers. In up to 28% of our comparisons, ignoring shared errors leads to the conclusion that one system is significantly better, while accounting for them does not. We also find that the pattern of errors matters. Errors shared by most judges and errors concentrated among a smaller group affect consensus differently and favor different voting methods. Measuring the overall amount of correlation alone is therefore insufficient. Our results suggest a simple approach: use a small set of trusted examples to estimate judge accuracy and identify shared mistakes. These shared errors should then be considered when analyzing the results, and the voting method should be chosen using trusted examples before it is applied to new data.

</details>


### [269] [AgentRouter: Heterogeneous Model Routing for Cost-Optimal Multi-Step Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22951)
*Rudrendu Kumar Paul,Sourav Nandy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AgentRouter，一个 12M 参数的轻量级分类器，将 agent 轨迹中的每一步路由到四个模型层级之一，实现 72% 成本降低同时保留 97.3% 的前沿模型质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有企业 agentic 系统将每步轨迹都路由到前沿模型，导致 60-80% 推理预算浪费在更小模型即可胜任的子任务上。现有路由方案只优化单轮查询分配，忽略了 agentic 工作流的独特性：单个轨迹内子任务复杂度差异巨大（规划步骤需前沿级推理，格式化步骤只需 7B 模型）。

Method: 将步级模型路由形式化为 agent 轨迹上的序列分配问题；提出 AgentRouter，基于路由时可提取的五个特征，将每一步映射到四个模型层级。该分类器仅 12M 参数，在 A100 GPU 上每步开销小于 5ms，训练数据为覆盖规划、编码、研究、数据分析任务的 50,000 条标注轨迹步。

Result: 相比纯前沿模型基线，成本降低 72%，保留 97.3% 质量（端到端任务完成度下降小于 3%）。步级路由准确率：最小复杂度步 91%，高效层级步 85%，较难的中端与前沿层级为 76-82%。在相同基准上，逐级应用的 RouteLLM 与 FrugalGPT 仅分别实现 31% 和 44% 的成本降低，因其单轮训练信号无法捕捉轨迹级质量依赖。

Conclusion: 步级路由是提升 agentic 系统推理效率的有效途径；针对轨迹内复杂度变化建模，比单轮路由方案显著更优。

Abstract: Enterprise agentic systems that route every trajectory step to a frontier model waste 60-80% of their inference budget on subtasks that smaller models handle equally well. Existing routing solutions optimize single-turn query assignment but ignore a property unique to agentic workflows: subtask complexity varies widely within a single trajectory. A planning step may require frontier-class reasoning while a subsequent formatting step needs only a 7B model. We formalize step-level model routing as a sequential assignment problem over agent trajectories and propose AgentRouter, a lightweight classifier (12M parameters, <5ms overhead per step on an A100 GPU) that maps each trajectory step to one of four model tiers using five features extractable at routing time. Trained on 50,000 annotated agent trajectory steps spanning planning, coding, research, and data analysis tasks, AgentRouter achieves 72% cost reduction relative to frontier-only baselines, retaining 97.3% of frontier-only quality (less than 3% degradation in end-to-end task completion); per-step routing accuracy reaches 91% on minimal-complexity steps and 85% on efficient-tier steps, with 76-82% on the harder mid-range and frontier tiers. On the same benchmarks, RouteLLM and FrugalGPT (applied per-step) achieve only 31% and 44% cost reduction respectively, because their single-turn training signal misses trajectory-level quality dependencies.

</details>


### [270] [IntLawNER: A Named Entity Recognition Dataset and Benchmark in International Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.22529)
*Genis Skura,Roland Bouffanais,Didier Wernli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IntLawNER，这是首个面向国际法成文文本的token级命名实体识别数据集与基准，涵盖ICJ判决、联合国安理会决议和ECtHR判决共2,987句、8,094个实体跨度，采用混合式算法-智能体流水线构建，并揭示人机一致性指标在领域NER中的误导性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国际法为各国协调行动、规制武装冲突和保护人权提供规范框架，但其文本缺乏token级命名实体识别资源。现有资源无法支持对国际法文本中机构特定实体的抽取，因此需要构建专用的NER数据集与基准。
